📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本周研究揭示了气候变化与城市化加剧登革热传播、B细胞受体依赖性增强感染的新机制、巴西DENV-3新谱系的快速扩散、以及重症登革热中意识障碍-ARDS-MODS三联征的高致死风险,同时强调了多模式诊断和疫苗评估框架的重要性。
  • 65%的纳入研究显示气温升高与登革热传播增加正相关,城市化与风险增加相关,传播向高地和城郊扩展[10.1371/journal.pntd.0013758]。
  • B细胞受体依赖性增强(BDE)是登革病毒感染的新机制,B细胞是急性期最大病毒储存库,补充了抗体依赖性增强模型[10.1128/jvi.02216-25]。
  • 巴西DENV-3谱系III_B.3.2自2022年引入后快速扩散,其蛋白基序缺失可能促进复制优势,2025年DENV-3病例已超DENV-1[10.1007/s11262-026-02259-2]。
  • 重症登革热ICU死亡三联征为意识障碍(RR 103.20)、ARDS(RR 7.55)和MODS(RR 6.08),血小板减少与死亡无关[10.7759/cureus.113041]。
  • This week's findings reveal that climate change and urbanization intensify dengue transmission, a novel B cell receptor-dependent enhancement mechanism, rapid spread of a new DENV-3 lineage in Brazil, and a high-mortality triad of impaired consciousness-ARDS-MODS in severe dengue, while emphasizing multimodal diagnostics and comprehensive vaccine evaluation.
  • 65% of 65 reviewed studies reported a positive association between rising temperatures and increased dengue transmission, with urbanization linked to higher risk and geographic expansion into highland and peri-urban areas [10.1371/journal.pntd.0013758].
  • B cell receptor-dependent enhancement (BDE) is a novel mechanism of dengue infection, with B cells as the largest reservoir of infected cells during acute dengue, complementing antibody-dependent enhancement [10.1128/jvi.02216-25].
  • DENV-3 lineage III_B.3.2, introduced in Brazil in 2022, rapidly spread with protein motif deletions potentially conferring replicative advantage; by December 2025, DENV-3 cases surpassed DENV-1 [10.1007/s11262-026-02259-2].
  • In severe dengue ICU patients, the mortality triad comprised impaired consciousness (RR 103.20), ARDS (RR 7.55), and MODS (RR 6.08); thrombocytopenia showed no mortality link [10.7759/cureus.113041].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期登革病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • SPC 和 WHO 发布的多国情况报告详细介绍了太平洋地区的登革热 暴发。2025年,报告了12起暴发;2026年,新喀里多尼亚、汤加和瓦努阿图谢法省正式宣布暴发。库克群岛于 2026 年 5 月 25 日宣布其 暴发 结束。
  • 据 anewsofindia.com 报道,QDENGA 已成为印度首个获批的登革热 疫苗。批准状态在标题中显示为已确认的进展。 [news-c525c3b04693d488]。
  • 一项 2a 期、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验使用 DENV-3 对照人类感染模型评估了 mosnodenvir 作为 登革病毒 感染预防药物的 抗病毒药物 活性和安全性。 [news-65bcdf9ceeb0c31e]。
  • 中央药品标准控制组织 (CDSCO) 批准武田的 QDENGA (TAK-003) 疫苗,标志着印度第一个获得许可的登革热 疫苗,旨在预防所有四种 登革病毒 血清型。 [news-5ca8740b440b246c]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • A multicountry situation report by SPC and WHO detailing dengue outbreaks across the Pacific.
  • QDENGA has become India's first approved dengue vaccine, according to a report by anewsofindia.com. The approval status is presented as a confirmed development in the headline. [news-c525c3b04693d488].
  • A phase 2a, double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled trial assessed the antiviral activity and safety of mosnodenvir as a prophylactic against dengue virus infection using a controlled human infection model with DENV-3.
  • The Central Drugs Standard Control Organisation (CDSCO) approved Takeda's QDENGA (TAK-003) vaccine, marking India's first licensed dengue vaccine designed to protect against all four dengue virus serotypes.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLoS Neglected Tropical Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-20  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pntd.0013758  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 20, 7, e0013758
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在变暖的世界中抗击登革热:气候和城市化如何影响低收入和中等收入国家的 传播(快速回顾)。

Battling dengue in a warming world: How climate and urbanization shape transmission in low- and middle-income countries (a rapid review).
作者： Nicole Saad, Jiaqin Wu, Myha Hill, Scott C Dorris, Jingyi Liu, Finn Wernet, Yu-Hsiang Wu, Jasnoor Kaur Anand, Abrahim Sawez, Weijun Yu
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热 是一种全球流行的 媒介 传播疾病,低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 承受着不成比例的负担。传播 模式越来越受到全球气温上升和快速城市化的影响,但最近没有综述综合这些环境因素如何重塑中低收入国家的登革热动态。方法:我们按照注册方案(PROSPERO:CRD42025635982)进行了快速系统评价。检索了六个数据库(MEDLINE、Embase、Global Health、SciELO、Global Index Medicus 和 Web of Science),查找 2020 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间发表的同行评审研究。研究筛选了影响中低收入国家登革热 传播 的气候或城市化因素。研究质量使用混合方法评估工具进行实证研究和结构化评估进行建模研究。调查结果以叙述方式综合。结果:在纳入的 65 项研究中,65% 报告称气温升高与登革热 传播 增加之间存在正相关关系,这通常归因于蚊子活动加剧和 传播 季节延长。35% 的研究对城市化进行了评估,无计划的增长、非正规住区和基础设施不足往往与风险增加相关。几项研究报告了向高地和城郊地区的地理扩张,以及季节性变化,包括更早的发病和更长的 传播 时期。城市热岛和土地利用变化通常与局部 传播 热点相关。29% 的研究记录了政府的应对措施,尽管许多研究受到零散的 监测 系统、有限的跨部门协调或气候数据未充分纳入公共卫生规划的阻碍。结论:气候变化和城市化正在重塑中低收入国家登革热的时空动态。适应性应对措施需要在弹性城市基础设施中加强 监测 和气候知情规划,以减少未来的 监测 风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 给低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 带来了不成比例的负担,这些国家的 传播 模式越来越受到全球气温上升和快速城市化的影响。本研究旨在综合这些环境因素如何重塑中低收入国家的登革热动态。
设计与对象
这项快速系统评价遵循注册方案(PROSPERO:CRD42025635982),包括 2020 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间发表的 65 项同行评审研究。该评价重点关注影响中低收入国家登革热 传播 的气候或城市化因素的研究。
核心方法
检索了六个数据库,并使用用于实证研究的混合方法评估工具和用于建模研究的结构化评估来评估研究质量。对研究结果进行叙述性综合,以评估特定环境变量的影响。
主要结果
在纳入的 65 项研究中,65% 报告气温升高与登革热 传播 增加之间呈正相关,而 35% 评估城市化,将计划外增长和基础设施不足与风险增加联系起来。研究还记录了向高地和城郊地区的地理扩张、季节变化以及城市热岛和局部 传播 热点之间的关联。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,气候变化和城市化正在重塑中低收入国家登革热的时空动态。这篇综述提供了最新的证据综合,将这些环境驱动因素与 传播 范围扩大和季节延长联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
适应性应对措施需要在有弹性的城市基础设施内加强 监测 和气候知情规划,以减少未来的 监测 风险。研究结果强调需要将气候数据纳入公共卫生规划,以解决零散的 监测 系统问题。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告具体的设计限制或证据基础强度的详细评估。该审查依赖于对不同方法和质量评估的研究进行叙述性综合。

Battling dengue in a warming world: How climate and urbanization shape transmission in low- and middle-income countries (a rapid review).

Authors: Nicole Saad, Jiaqin Wu, Myha Hill, Scott C Dorris, Jingyi Liu, Finn Wernet, Yu-Hsiang Wu, Jasnoor Kaur Anand, Abrahim Sawez, Weijun Yu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue fever is a globally prevalent vector-borne disease, with low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) experiencing a disproportionate burden. Transmission patterns are increasingly influenced by rising global temperatures and rapid urbanization, yet no recent review has synthesized how these environmental factors are reshaping dengue dynamics in LMICs. METHODS: We conducted a rapid systematic review following a registered protocol (PROSPERO: CRD42025635982). Six databases (MEDLINE, Embase, Global Health, SciELO, Global Index Medicus and Web of Science) were searched for peer-reviewed studies published between January 2020 and November 2024. Studies were screened for climate or urbanization factors affecting dengue transmission in LMICs. Study quality was assessed using the Mixed Methods Appraisal Tool for empirical studies and a structured assessment for modeling studies. Findings were synthesized narratively. RESULTS: Of the 65 included studies, 65% reported a positive association between rising temperatures and increased dengue transmission, often attributed to intensified mosquito activity and longer transmission seasons. Urbanization was assessed in 35% of studies, with unplanned growth, informal settlements, and inadequate infrastructure frequently associated with increased risk. Several studies reported geographic expansion into highland and peri-urban areas, as well as seasonal shifts, including earlier onset and prolonged transmission periods. Urban heat islands and land-use change were commonly associated with localized transmission hotspots. Government responses were documented in 29% of studies, though many were hindered by fragmented surveillance systems, limited cross-sector coordination, or insufficient integration of climate data into public health planning. CONCLUSION: Climate change and urbanization are reshaping the spatial and temporal dynamics of dengue in LMICs. Adaptive responses require strengthened surveillance and climate-informed planning in resilient urban infrastructure to reduce future transmission risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever disproportionately burdens low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), where transmission patterns are increasingly influenced by rising global temperatures and rapid urbanization. This study aimed to synthesize how these environmental factors are reshaping dengue dynamics in LMICs.
Design and population
This rapid systematic review followed a registered protocol (PROSPERO: CRD42025635982) and included 65 peer-reviewed studies published between January 2020 and November 2024. The review focused on studies examining climate or urbanization factors affecting dengue transmission in LMICs.
Core methods
Six databases were searched, and study quality was assessed using the Mixed Methods Appraisal Tool for empirical studies and a structured assessment for modeling studies. Findings were synthesized narratively to evaluate the impact of specific environmental variables.
Main results
Of the 65 included studies, 65% reported a positive association between rising temperatures and increased dengue transmission, while 35% assessed urbanization, linking unplanned growth and inadequate infrastructure to increased risk. Studies also documented geographic expansion into highland and peri-urban areas, seasonal shifts, and associations between urban heat islands and localized transmission hotspots.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that climate change and urbanization are reshaping the spatial and temporal dynamics of dengue in LMICs. This review provides a recent synthesis of evidence linking these environmental drivers to expanded transmission ranges and prolonged seasons.
Scientific and public-health significance
Adaptive responses require strengthened surveillance and climate-informed planning within resilient urban infrastructure to reduce future transmission risks. The findings highlight the need to integrate climate data into public health planning to address fragmented surveillance systems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or a detailed assessment of the strength of the evidence base. The review relied on narrative synthesis of studies with varying methodologies and quality assessments.

Journal: American journal of perinatology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-17  |  DOI: 10.1055/a-2910-1168  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

2023-2024 年阿根廷流行期间的先天性登革热感染:多中心登记。

Congenital Dengue Infection during the Epidemic in Argentina in the 2023-2024 Period: A Multicenter Registry.
作者： Eva Verónica Deschutter, Alejandra Bontcheff, Verónica Higa, Marianela Borra, Alejandra Gaiano, María Florencia Cechetto, Elcira Olmedo, Horacio Zelaya, Claudia Mercado, Miriam Calvari
摘要中文翻译目的:描述确诊登革热感染新生儿的临床、实验室和随访特征。研究设计:观察性、描述性、回顾性和多中心研究,包括 2023 年 7 月至 2024 年 7 月期间在阿根廷六个中心住院的确诊先天性登革热的新生儿。确认需要对母亲和新生儿进行病毒学或血清学检测。数据通过 REDCap 匿名收集并使用 RStudio 进行分析。结果:共纳入 58 名新生儿。大多数孕妇会出现发烧、肌痛和头痛的症状。母体诊断主要通过NS1抗原(59%)和PCR(43%)确诊;9% 进行了血清分型,其中 4⁄5 血清型对应于 DENV1。共有 52 名孕妇住院。出生时中位孕周为 38 周,21% 的新生儿早产。大多数分娩都是剖腹产。入院时,74%的新生儿没有症状;26% 的人表现出呼吸困难或体温不稳定。通过 PCR 在 59% 的新生儿中证实了 诊断。两名早产新生儿需要机械通气。中位住院时间为 9 天。在出生后的第一周内,57% 的新生儿出现血小板减少症。没有新生儿死亡或后遗症的记录。一位母亲因 重症登革热 休克而死亡。结论:在这项多中心登记中,74% 的新生儿无症状,21% 为早产儿,出院时和 30 天的随访中没有死亡或后遗症。要点: · 98% 的孕妇感染登革热症状较轻,其中一人死亡。· 约 74% 的先天性登革热新生儿无症状。· 57% 的新生儿在出生后第一周出现血小板减少症,第 7 天达到高峰。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述确诊先天性登革热感染新生儿的临床、实验室和随访特征。
设计与对象
这项观察性、描述性、回顾性、多中心研究纳入了 2023 年 7 月至 2024 年 7 月期间在阿根廷六个中心住院的 58 名确诊患有先天性登革热的新生儿,以及 52 名孕妇。
核心方法
确诊感染需要对母亲和新生儿进行病毒学或血清学检测,其中母亲诊断主要通过NS1抗原(59%)和PCR(43%)进行检测,新生儿诊断在59%的病例中通过PCR确诊;数据通过 REDCap 匿名收集并使用 RStudio 进行分析。
主要结果
大多数孕妇出现发烧、肌痛和头痛症状,98% 的人有轻度感染,但有 1 人因 重症登革热 休克死亡;21% 的新生儿早产,74% 入院时无症状,57% 在出生后第一周内出现血小板减少症,并在第 7 天达到高峰。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,大多数患有先天性登革热的新生儿没有症状,血小板减少症是常见现象,出院时和 30 天的随访中没有记录到新生儿死亡或后遗症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调先天性登革热在新生儿中通常无症状,但具有血小板减少症的风险,为临床管理提供信息,同时也指出了严重孕产妇结局的可能性。
局限与证据强度
由于回顾性观察设计可能会受到选择和信息偏差的影响,证据强度中等,并且该研究依赖于现有的临床数据,没有对照组。

Congenital Dengue Infection during the Epidemic in Argentina in the 2023-2024 Period: A Multicenter Registry.

Authors: Eva Verónica Deschutter, Alejandra Bontcheff, Verónica Higa, Marianela Borra, Alejandra Gaiano, María Florencia Cechetto, Elcira Olmedo, Horacio Zelaya, Claudia Mercado, Miriam Calvari
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To describe clinical, laboratory, and follow-up characteristics of neonates with confirmed dengue infection. STUDY DESIGN: Observational, descriptive, retrospective, and multicenter study including neonates with confirmed congenital dengue hospitalized at six centers from Argentina, between July 2023 and July 2024. Confirmation required virological or serological testing in both mother and neonate. Data were collected anonymously via REDCap and analyzed with RStudio. RESULTS: A total of 58 neonates were included. Most pregnant individuals presented with fever, myalgia, and headache. Maternal diagnosis was confirmed mainly by NS1 antigen (59%) and PCR (43%); 9% were serotyped, with 4⁄5 serotypes corresponding to DENV1. In all 52 pregnant individuals were hospitalized. Median gestational age at birth was 38 weeks, and 21% of neonates were preterm. Most deliveries were cesarean. At admission, 74% of neonates were asymptomatic; 26% showed respiratory distress or temperature instability. Diagnosis was confirmed by PCR in 59% of neonates. Two preterm neonates required mechanical ventilation. Median hospital stay was 9 days. Within the first week of life, 57% of neonates developed thrombocytopenia. No deaths or sequelae were recorded in neonates. One mother died because of severe dengue with shock. CONCLUSION: In this multicenter registry, 74% of neonates were asymptomatic and 21% preterm, with no deaths or sequelae at discharge and 30 days of follow-up. KEY POINTS: · In 98% of pregnant individuals with dengue infection was mild, and one died.. · About 74% of neonates with congenital dengue were asymptomatic.. · Thrombocytopenia was seen in 57% of neonates within the first week of life with a peak at day 7..
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the clinical, laboratory, and follow-up characteristics of neonates with confirmed congenital dengue infection.
Design and population
This observational, descriptive, retrospective, and multicenter study included 58 neonates with confirmed congenital dengue hospitalized at six centers in Argentina between July 2023 and July 2024, alongside 52 pregnant individuals.
Core methods
Confirmation of infection required virological or serological testing in both mother and neonate, with maternal diagnosis primarily via NS1 antigen (59%) and PCR (43%), and neonatal diagnosis confirmed by PCR in 59% of cases; data were collected anonymously via REDCap and analyzed with RStudio.
Main results
Most pregnant individuals presented with fever, myalgia, and headache, and 98% had mild infection though one died from severe dengue with shock; 21% of neonates were preterm, 74% were asymptomatic at admission, and 57% developed thrombocytopenia within the first week of life with a peak at day 7.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that the majority of neonates with congenital dengue were asymptomatic and that thrombocytopenia was a frequent finding, with no neonatal deaths or sequelae recorded at discharge and 30 days of follow-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform clinical management by highlighting that congenital dengue often presents asymptomatically in neonates but carries a risk of thrombocytopenia, while also noting the potential for severe maternal outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the retrospective, observational design which may be subject to selection and information bias, and the study relied on available clinical data without a control group.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

登革病毒 3 型谱系 III_B.3.2 在巴西的分子特征和近期传播:蛋白质基序和 流行病学 的计算机分析

Molecular characterization and recent spread of dengue virus type 3 lineage III_B.3.2 in Brazil: in silico analysis of protein motifs and epidemiology
作者： Alicia Pereira de Morais, Rodrigo Cunha Oliveira
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 属于黄病毒科,是导致全球相关性发热虫媒病毒病的原因。在巴西,自上世纪以来,四种病毒血清型一直在流行,其中 DENV-3 经常与更严重的临床病症和重大流行病相关。本研究旨在对巴西流行的 DENV-3 毒株及其 流行病学 进行基序表征。我们从 DATASUS/TABNET 中获取了 2013 年至 2025 年按血清型分类的病例信息。此外,利用 DENV-3 (NC_001475.2) 的 基因组 参考序列对 GenBank 和 GISAID 数据库中可用的 184 个巴西样本的完整 基因组 进行了分析。使用 MAFFT、BioEdit、Nextclade 和 GeneDoc 软件对序列进行编辑和分析,以识别谱系和氨基酸基序,然后提交到 IQ-Tree 和 FigTree 程序中进行系统发育分析。结果表明,血清型 1 历来占主导地位,但与前一年相比,2024 年 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 病例大幅增加。到2025年12月,DENV-3病例数已超过当年的DENV-1病例数。在整个 基因组 中鉴定出九个氨基酸基序,特别是 N-肉豆蔻酰化、酪蛋白激酶 II 磷酸化、蛋白激酶 C 磷酸化和 N-糖基化位点。直到 2022 年,主要谱系是 3III_C.2 和 3III_C.2.2,当时巴西引入了 3III_B.3.2 谱系,这与病例的增加有关。与之前的谱系相比,该基因型出现了替换并且缺乏蛋白质基序,表明这种改变有助于其复制成功。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 是一种全球相关的发热性虫媒病毒病,在巴西,DENV-3 经常与严重的临床病症和重大流行病相关。本研究旨在对巴西流行的 DENV-3 毒株及其 流行病学 进行基序表征。
设计与对象
该研究利用了 2013 年至 2025 年从 DATASUS/TABNET 获得的按血清型分类的病例信息,并分析了 GenBank 和 GISAID 数据库中提供的 184 个完整的 基因组 巴西样本。基因组分析使用 DENV-3 (NC_001475.2) 的 基因组 参考序列。
核心方法
使用 MAFFT、BioEdit、Nextclade 和 GeneDoc 软件对序列进行编辑和分析,以识别谱系和氨基酸基序,然后使用 IQ-Tree 和 FigTree 程序进行系统发育分析。该研究确定了特定位点,例如 N-肉豆蔻酰化、酪蛋白激酶 II 磷酸化、蛋白激酶 C 磷酸化和 N-糖基化。
主要结果
结果表明,血清型 1 历来占主导地位,但与前一年相比,2024 年 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 病例大幅增加。到 2025 年 12 月,DENV-3 病例已超过当年的 DENV-1 病例。谱系 3III_B.3.2 于 2022 年推出,与之前的谱系相比,出现了替换且缺少蛋白质基序。
解释与创新
作者认为,谱系 3III_B.3.2 中蛋白质基序的取代和缺失有助于其复制成功并与病例增加相关。从主要谱系 3III_C.2 和 3III_C.2.2 到 3III_B.3.2 的转变标志着巴西 DENV-3 流行病学格局的近期变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了 基因组监测 追踪与病例数上升相关的特定 DENV-3 谱系的出现和传播的重要性。了解成功谱系的分子特征可以为登革热 暴发 的风险评估和公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、样本量限制或数据源中的潜在偏差。由于依赖于现有的数据库序列和回顾性病例数据而没有详细的临床验证,证据强度被评估为中等。

Molecular characterization and recent spread of dengue virus type 3 lineage III_B.3.2 in Brazil: in silico analysis of protein motifs and epidemiology

Authors: Alicia Pereira de Morais, Rodrigo Cunha Oliveira
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The dengue virus (DENV) belongs to the Flaviviridae family and is responsible for a febrile arbovirus disease of global relevance. In Brazil, there has been endemic circulation of the four viral serotypes since the last century, with DENV-3 being frequently associated with more severe clinical conditions and major epidemics. This study aimed to perform the motif characterization of DENV-3 strains circulating in Brazil and its epidemiology. We obtained cases information by serotype from DATASUS/TABNET from 2013 to 2025. Furthermore, 184 complete genomes of Brazilian samples available in the GenBank and GISAID databases were analyzed with the genome reference sequence of DENV-3 (NC_001475.2). The sequences were edited and analyzed with the MAFFT, BioEdit, Nextclade, and GeneDoc software to identify lineages and amino acid motifs and were subsequently submitted to phylogenetic analyses in the IQ-Tree and FigTree programs. The results indicated a historical predominance of serotype one, but with a considerable increase in DENV-2 and DENV-3 cases in 2024 compared to the previous year. By December 2025, DENV-3 cases had already surpassed those of DENV-1 for that year. Nine amino acid motifs were identified throughout the genome, especially sites of N-myristoylation, casein kinase II phosphorylation, protein kinase C phosphorylation, and N-glycosylation. The predominant lineages were 3III_C.2 and 3III_C.2.2 until 2022, when lineage 3III_B.3.2 was introduced in Brazil, being associated with the increase in cases. This genotype presented substitutions and the absence of protein motifs compared to previous lineages, suggesting that such alterations contributed to its replicative success.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus is a globally relevant febrile arbovirus disease, and in Brazil, DENV-3 has been frequently associated with severe clinical conditions and major epidemics. This study aimed to perform the motif characterization of DENV-3 strains circulating in Brazil and its epidemiology.
Design and population
The study utilized case information by serotype obtained from DATASUS/TABNET from 2013 to 2025 and analyzed 184 complete genomes of Brazilian samples available in the GenBank and GISAID databases. The genomic analysis used the genome reference sequence of DENV-3 (NC_001475.2).
Core methods
Sequences were edited and analyzed with MAFFT, BioEdit, Nextclade, and GeneDoc software to identify lineages and amino acid motifs, followed by phylogenetic analyses using IQ-Tree and FigTree programs. The study identified specific sites such as N-myristoylation, casein kinase II phosphorylation, protein kinase C phosphorylation, and N-glycosylation.
Main results
Results indicated a historical predominance of serotype one, but a considerable increase in DENV-2 and DENV-3 cases occurred in 2024 compared to the previous year. By December 2025, DENV-3 cases had surpassed those of DENV-1 for that year. Lineage 3III_B.3.2, introduced in 2022, presented substitutions and the absence of protein motifs compared to previous lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that the substitutions and absence of protein motifs in lineage 3III_B.3.2 contributed to its replicative success and association with the increase in cases. The shift from predominant lineages 3III_C.2 and 3III_C.2.2 to 3III_B.3.2 marks a recent change in the epidemiological landscape of DENV-3 in Brazil.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of genomic surveillance to track the emergence and spread of specific DENV-3 lineages associated with rising case numbers. Understanding the molecular characteristics of successful lineages can inform risk assessment and public health responses to dengue outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, sample size constraints, or potential biases in the data sources. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the reliance on available database sequences and retrospective case data without detailed clinical validation.

B 细胞受体依赖性增强 登革病毒 感染的分子和生物物理要求

Molecular and biophysical requirements for B cell receptor-dependent enhancement of dengue virus infection
作者： Gaby Madrigal, Chad Gebo, Benjamin D. McElvany, Menelaos Symeonides, Sean A. Diehl, Adam T. Waickman
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 是登革热的病原体,登革热是一种蚊媒疾病,给全世界带来了巨大且日益严重的公共卫生负担。登革热的一个独特的病理生理学特征是免疫介导的增强,其中由原发感染引起的预先存在的免疫力可以增强异源DENV血清型的后续感染的严重性。这种现象的一个主要机制解释是 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),其中亚中和浓度的 DENV 特异性 IgG 抗体有利于 DENV 进入表达 FcγR 的细胞。因此,该模型假设吞噬性单核细胞是 DENV 的主要 储存宿主。然而,多个独立研究小组表明,B 细胞是急性登革热期间循环中病毒感染细胞中最大的 储存宿主,这表明我们对免疫介导的 DENV 趋向性的理解存在脱节。为了应对这种持续存在的知识差距,我们的团队之前发现了一种免疫介导增强的新机制,我们将其称为 DENV 感染的 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性增强 (BDE)。在这项研究中,我们发现 DENV 反应性 B 细胞的 DENV 感染对 BCR/DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白相互作用高度敏感。DENV 进入 B 细胞亚群是由动力介导的,需要近端 BCR 信号传导,并涉及晚期内体的定位。最后,我们证明永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞可以被活 DENV 有效感染,能够支持所有四种血清型的活跃病毒复制。我们认为,BDE 提供了一层额外的病原体特异性免疫介导的感染风险,补充了现有的 ADE 模型,并为 DENV 免疫发病机制的潜在机制提供了更多见解。重要性 这项研究提供了对 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染增强过程的机制见解。免疫介导的感染增强是登革热免疫发病机制的基石,最常归因于 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE)。DENV 感染的 BCR 依赖性增强是免疫介导增强的一种替代和补充机制,它解决了登革热领域中几个持续存在的知识空白,并可能为为什么有些人在感染后进展到 重症登革热 提供机制见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗体依赖性增强 (DENV) 是一个重大的公共卫生负担,预先存在的免疫力会增强随后异源感染的严重性,这种现象通常归因于 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE)。这项研究解决了有关免疫介导的 DENV 趋向性机制的知识空白,特别研究了 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性增强 (BDE),因为 B 细胞是急性登革热期间感染细胞中最大的 储存宿主。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有报告具体的研究日期、中心或样本量。该研究利用永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞和活 DENV 来研究所有四种血清型的感染机制。
核心方法
该研究评估了 DENV 感染对 BCR 和 DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白相互作用的敏感性,并描述了进入过程。方法包括确定 DENV 反应性 B 细胞病毒感染期间对动力介导的进入、近端 BCR 信号传导以及晚期内体定位的需求。
主要结果
发现 DENV 反应性 B 细胞的 DENV 感染对 BCR/DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白相互作用高度敏感。进入该 B 细胞亚群的过程是动力介导的,需要近端 BCR 信号传导,并涉及晚期内体的定位。永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞被活 DENV 有效感染,支持所有四种血清型的活跃病毒复制。
解释与创新
作者提出,BCR 依赖性增强 (BDE) 提供了一层额外的病原体特异性免疫介导的感染风险,补充了现有的 ADE 模型。这种机制为 DENV 免疫发病机制提供了新的见解,并解释了为什么一些个体在感染后进展为 重症登革热。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了 B 细胞受体依赖性 DENV 感染增强的机制见解,这可能解释了一些患者进展为 重症登革热 的情况。该研究表明,BDE 补充了 ADE 模型,有可能为未来对登革热免疫发病机制的理解提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告有关样本量、对照或普遍性的具体限制。证据强度被评估为中等,因为结果来自永生化细胞系而不是原代细胞或体内模型。

Molecular and biophysical requirements for B cell receptor-dependent enhancement of dengue virus infection

Authors: Gaby Madrigal, Chad Gebo, Benjamin D. McElvany, Menelaos Symeonides, Sean A. Diehl, Adam T. Waickman
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus (DENV) is the causative agent of dengue, a mosquito-borne disease that represents a significant and growing public health burden around the world. A unique pathophysiological feature of dengue is immune-mediated enhancement, wherein preexisting immunity elicited by a primary infection can enhance the severity of a subsequent infection by a heterologous DENV serotype. A leading mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon is antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), where sub-neutralizing concentrations of DENV-specific IgG antibodies facilitate the entry of DENV into FcγR-expressing cells. Accordingly, this model posits that phagocytic mononuclear cells are the primary reservoir of DENV. However, multiple independent groups have shown that B cells are the largest reservoir of virally infected cells in circulation during acute dengue, representing a disconnect in our understanding of immune-mediated DENV tropism. In response to this persistent knowledge gap, our team has previously identified a novel mechanism of immune-mediated enhancement we have termed B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement (BDE) of DENV infection. In this study, we show that DENV infection of DENV-reactive B cells is highly sensitive to BCR/DENV envelope (E) protein interactions. DENV entry into this subset of B cells is dynamin-mediated, requires proximal BCR signaling, and involves localization to late endosomes. Finally, we show that immortalized DENV-reactive B cells are productively infected by live DENV, capable of supporting active viral replication of all four serotypes. We propose that BDE provides an additional layer of pathogen-specific immune-mediated infection risk that complements existing models of ADE and offers additional insight into potential mechanisms of DENV immunopathogenesis. IMPORTANCE This study provides mechanistic insight into the process of B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement of dengue virus (DENV) infection. Immune-mediated enhancement of infection is a cornerstone of dengue immunopathogenesis and is most often attributed to antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). BCR-dependent enhancement of DENV infection is an alternative and complementary mechanism for immune-mediated enhancement that addresses several persistent knowledge gaps in the dengue field and may offer mechanistic insight into why some people progress to severe dengue after infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is a significant public health burden where preexisting immunity can enhance the severity of subsequent heterologous infections, a phenomenon often attributed to antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). This study addresses a knowledge gap regarding the mechanism of immune-mediated DENV tropism, specifically investigating B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement (BDE) as B cells are the largest reservoir of infected cells during acute dengue.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not report specific study dates, centers, or sample sizes. The study utilized immortalized DENV-reactive B cells and live DENV to investigate infection mechanisms across all four serotypes.
Core methods
The study assessed the sensitivity of DENV infection to BCR and DENV envelope (E) protein interactions and characterized the entry process. Methods included determining the requirement for dynamin-mediated entry, proximal BCR signaling, and localization to late endosomes during viral infection of DENV-reactive B cells.
Main results
DENV infection of DENV-reactive B cells was found to be highly sensitive to BCR/DENV envelope (E) protein interactions. The entry process into this B cell subset is dynamin-mediated, requires proximal BCR signaling, and involves localization to late endosomes. Immortalized DENV-reactive B cells were productively infected by live DENV, supporting active viral replication of all four serotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors propose that BCR-dependent enhancement (BDE) provides an additional layer of pathogen-specific immune-mediated infection risk that complements existing models of ADE. This mechanism offers novel insight into DENV immunopathogenesis and addresses why some individuals progress to severe dengue after infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings offer mechanistic insight into B cell receptor-dependent enhancement of DENV infection, which may explain the progression to severe dengue in some patients. The study suggests that BDE complements ADE models, potentially informing future understanding of dengue immunopathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations regarding sample size, controls, or generalizability. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate because the results are derived from immortalized cell lines rather than primary cells or in vivo models.

超越中和:评估减毒活四价登革热 疫苗 诱导体液免疫的综合框架

Beyond neutralization: A comprehensive framework for evaluating humoral immunity induced by a live-attenuated tetravalent dengue vaccine
作者： Raphael Gottardo, Hansi Dean, Eduardo J.M. Nascimento, Vianney Tricou, Mayuri Sharma
摘要中文翻译目标:登革热 发病率 在全球范围内不断增加。TAK-003 是一种减毒活四价登革热 疫苗,已在多个国家/地区获得许可。其安全性、有效性和免疫原性已得到广泛研究,并为建议和实施提供了路线图。根据 WHO 指南,登革热 疫苗 临床试验的主要免疫原性终点是中和抗体 (NAb) 滴度。然而,保护的免疫学相关性尚未确定,登革病毒 (DENV) 会引发广泛的抗体反应。本研究评估了统计和实验方法,以评估对 TAK-003 的各种抗体反应,作为评估可能与预防登革热感染和/或疾病相关的 疫苗 免疫学特征的框架。方法:通过以下免疫测定评估反应:微量中和测定、DENV 总结合 IgG ELISA、抗登革热补体抗体测定、抗登革热非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) IgG ELISA 和抗登革热 IgG 亲合力测定。对来自多项试验的 TAK-003 疫苗接种/安慰剂参与者的样本进行了测试。比较结果的测定性能以及基线血清状态、血清型和年龄对 疫苗 引发的反应的影响。相关分析评估变量之间的关系。主成分分析 (PCA) 评估数据复杂性。结果:疫苗接种 在每次检测中均引发对所有四种 DENV 血清型的反应。在基线血清阳性参与者中,疫苗 诱导的反应程度较高,但在基线血清阴性参与者中,疫苗 效应更强。基线血清阴性参与者在所有血清型中具有相当的 DENV 总结合 IgG 浓度、抗登革热 IgG 亲合力和抗登革热补体抗体滴度反应。MNT50 NAb 和抗登革热 NS1 IgG 反应因血清型而异。参数之间的中等到低相关性表明不同的测定测量非冗余 疫苗 诱导的体液 免疫应答。反应变异的主要来源是 疫苗接种 后的时间,其次是血清型。结论:TAK-003 疫苗接种 引发多个非重叠、独特的体液 免疫应答。数据支持包括除 NAb 之外的参数,以更好地评估 疫苗 诱导的体液反应。临床试验:政府注册:NCT01511250、NCT02302066、NCT02747927、NCT03423173。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 发病率 在全球范围内不断增加,虽然 TAK-003(一种减毒活四价登革热 疫苗)已在多个国家获得许可,但尚未确定保护的免疫学相关性,并且 登革病毒 会引发广泛的抗体反应。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了统计和实验方法,使用来自多项试验的疫苗接种者和安慰剂参与者的样本来评估对 TAK-003 的各种抗体反应。
核心方法
通过微量中和测定、DENV 总结合 IgG ELISA、抗登革热补体抗体测定、抗登革热非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) IgG ELISA 和抗登革热 IgG 亲和力测定评估反应,比较测定性能以及基线血清状态、血清型和年龄对 疫苗 引发反应的影响。
主要结果
疫苗接种 在每次测定中均引发对所有四种 DENV 血清型的反应,基线血清阳性参与者的反应幅度较高,但基线血清阴性参与者的 疫苗 效应更强;参数之间的中等至低相关性表明非冗余 疫苗 诱导的体液 免疫应答。
解释与创新
作者解释说,TAK-003 疫苗接种 引发了多种非重叠、独特的体液 免疫应答,并且数据支持包括除了中和抗体之外的参数,以更好地评估 疫苗 诱导的体液反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为评估登革热 疫苗 诱导的体液免疫的综合框架的开发提供了信息,有可能改善对 疫苗 功效和保护的评估。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于具有不同设计的多个试验的观察数据,并且局限性包括潜在的混杂因素,例如 疫苗接种 后时间和血清型变异性。

Beyond neutralization: A comprehensive framework for evaluating humoral immunity induced by a live-attenuated tetravalent dengue vaccine

Authors: Raphael Gottardo, Hansi Dean, Eduardo J.M. Nascimento, Vianney Tricou, Mayuri Sharma
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Dengue incidence is increasing worldwide. TAK-003, a live-attenuated tetravalent dengue vaccine, is licensed in several countries. Its safety, efficacy, and immunogenicity have been extensively studied and provided the roadmap for recommendations and implementation. Per WHO guidelines, the primary immunogenicity endpoint in dengue vaccine clinical trials is neutralizing antibody (NAb) titers. However, there is no established immunological correlate of protection, and dengue virus (DENV) elicits a broad range of antibody responses. This study assessed statistical and experimental methods to evaluate a wide range of antibody responses to TAK-003 as a framework for evaluating vaccine immunological signatures that may correlate with protection from dengue infection and/or disease. METHODS: Responses were assessed by the following immunoassays: microneutralization assay, DENV total binding IgG ELISA, anti-dengue complement antibody assay, anti-dengue non-structural protein 1 (NS1) IgG ELISA, and anti-dengue IgG avidity assay. Samples from TAK-003 vaccinated/placebo participants from several trials were tested. Results were compared for assay performance and the impact of baseline serostatus, serotype, and age on vaccine-elicited responses. Correlation analyses assessed relationships among variables. Principal component analyses (PCA) evaluated data complexity. RESULTS: Vaccination elicited responses to all four DENV serotypes in each assay. The magnitude of vaccine-induced responses was higher in baseline seropositive participants, but vaccine effect was stronger in baseline seronegative participants. Baseline seronegative participants had comparable DENV total binding IgG concentrations, anti-dengue IgG avidity, and anti-dengue complement antibody titer responses across all serotypes. MNT50 NAb and anti-dengue NS1 IgG responses varied by serotype. Modest to low correlations across parameters suggest that the different assays measure non-redundant vaccine-induced humoral immune responses. The major source of response variability was post-vaccination time, followed by serotype. CONCLUSION: TAK-003 vaccination elicited multiple non-overlapping, unique humoral immune responses. Data support including parameters in addition to NAbs to better assess vaccine-induced humoral responses. CLINICALTRIALS: govregistration:NCT01511250, NCT02302066, NCT02747927, NCT03423173.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue incidence is increasing worldwide, and while TAK-003, a live-attenuated tetravalent dengue vaccine, is licensed in several countries, there is no established immunological correlate of protection, and dengue virus elicits a broad range of antibody responses.
Design and population
This study assessed statistical and experimental methods to evaluate a wide range of antibody responses to TAK-003 using samples from vaccinated and placebo participants from several trials.
Core methods
Responses were assessed by microneutralization assay, DENV total binding IgG ELISA, anti-dengue complement antibody assay, anti-dengue non-structural protein 1 (NS1) IgG ELISA, and anti-dengue IgG avidity assay, with results compared for assay performance and the impact of baseline serostatus, serotype, and age on vaccine-elicited responses.
Main results
Vaccination elicited responses to all four DENV serotypes in each assay, with higher magnitude in baseline seropositive participants but stronger vaccine effect in baseline seronegative participants; modest to low correlations across parameters suggest non-redundant vaccine-induced humoral immune responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that TAK-003 vaccination elicited multiple non-overlapping, unique humoral immune responses, and data support including parameters in addition to neutralizing antibodies to better assess vaccine-induced humoral responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of a comprehensive framework for evaluating humoral immunity induced by dengue vaccines, potentially improving the assessment of vaccine efficacy and protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on observational data from multiple trials with varying designs, and limitations include potential confounding factors such as post-vaccination time and serotype variability.

在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜的埃及伊蚊中检测到 登革病毒

Detection of Dengue Virus in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes, Dubai, United Arab Emirates
作者： Norbert Nowotny, Noushad Karuvantevida, Elmahi El Tilib Gubran, Naseem Mohammed Rafee, Adel Abdulla AlKarrani, Ghanim Al Hammadi, Nasna Nassir, Dia Advani, Mohammed J Uddin, Jeremy V. Camp
摘要中文翻译继 2024 年 登革病毒 暴发 后,我们在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜进行了为期 1 年的蚊子调查。我们收集了 1,598 只埃及伊蚊,并在 5 个蚊子数量少于 20 只的池中检测到了 登革病毒。我们的研究结果强调了持续的 媒介 监测 对于 登革病毒 在干旱城市环境中的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
据报告,2024 年 4 月降雨量增加后,阿拉伯联合酋长国的登革热活动有所增加,包括本土病例。为了监测当地蚊子种类并识别传播的蚊媒病毒,我们在阿联酋迪拜进行了蚊子调查。
设计与对象
我们于2024年6月20日开始,在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜进行了为期1年的蚊虫调查。我们使用结合了CO2诱饵的BG-Sentinel蚊虫陷阱进行机会性诱捕,采集了1,598只埃及伊蚊。该调查针对的城市区域包括劳工营地、建筑工地、住宅社区和绿地。
核心方法
我们利用分子技术评估了被捕获的蚊子是否存在蚊媒人类病原体。诱捕蚊子是迪拜市公共卫生服务部蚊子 监测 计划的一部分。我们在 5 个池中检测到 登革病毒,蚊子数量少于 20 只。
主要结果
我们收集了 1,598 只埃及伊蚊,并在 5 个池中检测到了 登革病毒,其中蚊子数量少于 20 只。提供的证据没有报告具体的感染率、血清型分布或统计比较。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果强调了持续的 媒介 监测 对于 登革病毒 在干旱城市环境中的重要性。在埃及伊蚊中检测到 登革病毒 证实了继 2024 年 暴发 后当地 传播 的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了需要持续实施 媒介 监测 来管理迪拜等干旱城市环境中的 登革病毒 风险。公共卫生工作应重点监测劳改营、建筑工地和绿地的蚊子数量,以预防未来的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的研究局限性或证据强度的详细评估。机会主义捕获方法可能会限制研究结果对迪拜所有城市地区的推广。

Detection of Dengue Virus in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes, Dubai, United Arab Emirates

Authors: Norbert Nowotny, Noushad Karuvantevida, Elmahi El Tilib Gubran, Naseem Mohammed Rafee, Adel Abdulla AlKarrani, Ghanim Al Hammadi, Nasna Nassir, Dia Advani, Mohammed J Uddin, Jeremy V. Camp
Original abstract (English)
We performed a 1-year mosquito survey in Dubai, United Arab Emirates, after a dengue virus outbreak in 2024. We collected 1,598 Aedes aegypti mosquitoes and detected dengue virus in 5 pools of < 20 mosquitoes. Our findings underscore the importance of sustained vector surveillance for dengue virus in arid urban environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Increased dengue activity, including autochthonous cases, was reported in the United Arab Emirates after periods of increased rainfall in April 2024. To monitor local mosquito species and identify circulating mosquitoborne viruses, we conducted a mosquito survey in Dubai, UAE.
Design and population
We performed a 1-year mosquito survey in Dubai, United Arab Emirates, starting on June 20, 2024. We collected 1,598 Aedes aegypti mosquitoes using opportunistic trapping with BG-Sentinel mosquito traps combined with CO2 lures. The survey targeted urban areas including labor camps, construction sites, residential communities, and green spaces.
Core methods
We evaluated the trapped mosquitoes for the presence of mosquitoborne human pathogens by using molecular techniques. Mosquito trapping was part of an enhanced Dubai Municipality Public Health Services Department mosquito surveillance program. We detected dengue virus in 5 pools of < 20 mosquitoes.
Main results
We collected 1,598 Aedes aegypti mosquitoes and detected dengue virus in 5 pools of < 20 mosquitoes. The supplied evidence does not report specific infection rates, serotype distributions, or statistical comparisons.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings underscore the importance of sustained vector surveillance for dengue virus in arid urban environments. The detection of dengue virus in Aedes aegypti mosquitoes confirms local transmission potential following the 2024 outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for ongoing vector surveillance to manage dengue virus risks in arid urban settings like Dubai. Public health efforts should focus on monitoring mosquito populations in labor camps, construction sites, and green spaces to prevent future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific study limitations or a detailed assessment of evidence strength. The opportunistic trapping method may limit the generalizability of the findings to all urban areas in Dubai.

伊朗的 登革热 暴发:紧急警告和解决方案

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions
作者： Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
摘要中文翻译自COVID-19大流行以来,最重要的蚊媒病毒性疾病之一登革热近年来已成为全球许多国家面临的严重公共卫生挑战。这种疾病由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播,在热带和亚热带地区流行。伊朗尽管地处干旱和半干旱气候地区,但也面临传播这种疾病的风险。最近有当地病例报告霍尔木兹甘省,特别是伦盖市的登革热为该国的卫生系统敲响了警钟。登革热是伊朗公共卫生面临的一个新威胁,需要采取紧急和协调的行动。控制这种疾病需要社会各界的参与,包括政府、卫生机构和公众。通过实施科学和可持续的战略并与政府机构协调,可以防止伊朗成为该病的流行地区。登革热。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管登革热在热带和亚热带地区存在,但登革热已成为包括伊朗在内的许多国家的严重公共卫生挑战。该研究强调迫切需要采取协调行动,以防止伊朗成为 登革热 的流行区。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注霍尔木兹甘省的 登革热 暴发,特别是伊朗境内的班达尔伦盖市 (Bandar Lengeh)。它报告了最近的当地病例,为该国的卫生系统敲响了警钟。
核心方法
该研究没有提供数据收集或分析的详细方法或程序。它依赖于报告的病例和 暴发 的一般观察。
主要结果
该研究报告了霍尔木兹甘省最近发生的 登革热 当地病例,特别是在班达尔伦盖市,这表明伊朗的公共卫生面临新的威胁。
解释与创新
作者将最近的当地病例解读为对伊朗卫生系统的重大警告,强调需要采取紧急和协调行动,以防止该疾病流行。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在伊朗实施科学和可持续战略来控制 登革热 的重要性,特别是在霍尔木兹甘省等已报告当地 传播 的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有提供详细的方法或统计分析,这限制了其研究结果的可信度。然而,证据是基于最近的当地案例,这些案例被明确报告并支持新出现威胁的结论。

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions

Authors: Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
Original abstract (English)
Following the COVID-19 pandemic, dengue fever, one of the most important mosquito-borne viral diseases, has become a serious public health challenge in many countries around the world in recent years.Transmitted by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes, this disease is prevalent in tropical and subtropical regions.Iran is also at risk of spreading this disease despite being located in a region with a predominantly arid and semi-arid climate.Recent reports of local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, are a wake-up call for the country's health system.Dengue fever is an emerging threat to public health in Iran that requires urgent and coordinated action.Controlling this disease requires the participation of all sectors of society, including the government, health institutions, and the public.By implementing scientific and sustainable strategies and in coordination with government agencies, Iran can be prevented from becoming an endemic area for dengue fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever has become a serious public health challenge in many countries, including Iran, despite its prevalence in tropical and subtropical regions. The study highlights the urgent need for coordinated action to prevent Iran from becoming an endemic area for dengue fever.
Design and population
The study focuses on the dengue fever outbreak in Hormozgan province, particularly in Bandar Lengeh city, within Iran. It reports recent local cases as a wake-up call for the country's health system.
Core methods
The study does not provide detailed methods or procedures for data collection or analysis. It relies on reported cases and general observations of the outbreak.
Main results
The study reports recent local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, indicating an emerging threat to public health in Iran.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the recent local cases as a significant warning for Iran's health system, emphasizing the need for urgent and coordinated action to prevent the disease from becoming endemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of implementing scientific and sustainable strategies to control dengue fever in Iran, particularly in regions like Hormozgan province where local transmission has been reported.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not provide detailed methods or statistical analysis, which limits the confidence in its findings. However, the evidence is based on recent local cases, which are explicitly reported and support the conclusion of an emerging threat.

登革热 尽管 PCR 呈阴性,但仍并发急性非结石性胆囊炎和噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增多症。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Acute Acalculous Cholecystitis and Hemophagocytic Lymphohistiocytosis Despite Negative PCR.
作者： Takako Kobayashi, Masayuki Oki, Hideki Ozawa
摘要中文翻译目的:报告一例 登革热 并发急性非结石胆囊炎 (AAC) 和噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增多症 (HLH) 的病例,尽管最初聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 结果呈阴性。方法:我们描述了一名 40 岁女性的临床病程,她从印度尼西亚返回,出现持续发烧和皮疹。诊断是通过临床评估、血清学检测和腹部影像学建立的。该研究符合《赫尔辛基宣言》中概述的原则。结果:虽然最初的登革热PCR结果呈阴性,但随后的NS1和IgM抗体检测结果呈阳性。患者出现了严重的并发症,包括以胆囊壁增厚为特征的 AAC 和以明显发热、脾肿大、低纤维蛋白原血症、高铁蛋白血症和高可溶性 IL-2 受体水平为特征的 HLH。尽管有这些症状,患者仍接受保守治疗。由于超声检查观察到自发性改善,计划的经皮肝穿刺胆囊抽吸术被取消。患者在未输注皮质类固醇或血小板的情况下完全康复,并于第 14 天出院。结论:鉴于登革热急性期 PCR 结果可能出现假阴性,多模式血清学检测至关重要。该病例表明,严重的非典型并发症,如 AAC 和 HLH,可以仅通过支持性护理来解决,避免侵入性手术、免疫抑制治疗和血小板输注。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
报告一例 登革热 并发急性非结石性胆囊炎 (AAC) 和噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增多症 (HLH),尽管最初的聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 结果呈阴性。
设计与对象
我们描述了一名 40 岁女性的临床病程,她从印度尼西亚返回,出现持续发烧和皮疹。
核心方法
诊断是通过临床评估、血清学检测和腹部影像学建立的。该研究符合《赫尔辛基宣言》中概述的原则。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 登革热 综合诊断方法的重要性,特别是当 PCR 结果为阴性时,并支持对严重并发症的保守治疗,以避免不必要的干预。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于单个 病例报告,限制了普遍性。然而,详细的临床过程和多模式诊断方法为报告的结果和管理策略提供了适度的信心。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Acute Acalculous Cholecystitis and Hemophagocytic Lymphohistiocytosis Despite Negative PCR.

Authors: Takako Kobayashi, Masayuki Oki, Hideki Ozawa
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To report a case of dengue fever complicated by acute acalculous cholecystitis (AAC) and hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis (HLH), despite initial negative polymerase chain reaction (PCR) results. METHODS: We describe the clinical course of a 40-year-old woman who returned from Indonesia with a persistent fever and rash. The diagnosis was established through clinical evaluation, serological testing, and abdominal imaging. The study conformed to the principles outlined in the Declaration of Helsinki. RESULTS: Although the initial dengue PCR result was negative, subsequent NS1 and IgM antibody test results were positive. The patient developed severe complications, including AAC, characterized by gallbladder wall thickening, and HLH, indicated by marked fever, splenomegaly, hypofibrinogenemia, hyperferritinemia, and high soluble IL-2 receptor levels. Despite these symptoms, the patient was managed conservatively. Planned percutaneous transhepatic gallbladder aspiration was canceled because of spontaneous improvement observed on ultrasonography. The patient recovered fully without corticosteroid or platelet transfusions and was discharged on day 14. CONCLUSION: Given the potential for false-negative PCR results during the acute phase of dengue, multimodal serological testing is essential. This case demonstrates that severe atypical complications, such as AAC and HLH, can be resolved with supportive care alone, avoiding invasive procedures, immunosuppressive therapy, and platelet transfusion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To report a case of dengue fever complicated by acute acalculous cholecystitis (AAC) and hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis (HLH), despite initial negative polymerase chain reaction (PCR) results.
Design and population
We describe the clinical course of a 40-year-old woman who returned from Indonesia with a persistent fever and rash.
Core methods
The diagnosis was established through clinical evaluation, serological testing, and abdominal imaging. The study conformed to the principles outlined in the Declaration of Helsinki.
Main results
Although the initial dengue PCR result was negative, subsequent NS1 and IgM antibody test results were positive. The patient developed severe complications, including AAC, characterized by gallbladder wall thickening, and HLH, indicated by marked fever, splenomegaly, hypofibrinogenemia, hyperferritinemia, and high soluble IL-2 receptor levels. Despite these symptoms, the patient was managed conservatively and recovered fully without corticosteroid or platelet transfusions, being discharged on day 14.
Interpretation and novelty
Given the potential for false-negative PCR results during the acute phase of dengue, multimodal serological testing is essential. This case demonstrates that severe atypical complications, such as AAC and HLH, can be resolved with supportive care alone, avoiding invasive procedures, immunosuppressive therapy, and platelet transfusion.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of comprehensive diagnostic approaches for dengue fever, especially when PCR results are negative, and support conservative management for severe complications to avoid unnecessary interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case report, limiting generalizability. However, the detailed clinical course and multimodal diagnostic approach provide moderate confidence in the reported outcomes and management strategy.

重症登革热 中意识障碍、急性呼吸窘迫综合征 (ARDS) 和多器官功能障碍综合征 (MODS) 作为 死亡率 的三联征:来自 100 例连续 ICU 入院的证据

Impaired Consciousness, Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS), and Multiorgan Dysfunction Syndrome (MODS) As the Triad of Mortality in Severe Dengue: Evidence From 100 Consecutive ICU Admissions
作者： Akanksha Gupta, Sunita Gupta, Divyam Goyal, Neel S Dave
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重症登革热 是热带地区重症监护病房 (ICU) 入院和死亡的主要原因,尽早识别与不良结局相关的因素对于指导 重症登革热 和为危重患者分配资源至关重要。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 纳入了 100 名连续 2025 年 3 月至 2026 年 1 月期间入住哈里亚纳邦穆拉纳 Maharishi Markandeshwar 医学科学研究所 (MMIMSR) 的 ICU 和急诊科的 WHO 2009 定义的 重症登革热 患者。
核心方法
使用适当的统计检验、95% CI 的相对风险 (RR) 和 ROC 分析,记录并比较幸存者和非幸存者的临床、实验室和严重程度评分数据(急性生理学和慢性健康评估 II (APACHE II)、序贯器官衰竭评估 (SOFA))。
主要结果
ICU 死亡率 为 21.0% (21/100),血小板减少症未显示 死亡率 关联 (p = 0.678)。意识障碍(RR 103.20)、急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS;RR 7.55)和多器官功能障碍综合征(MODS;RR 6.08)与 死亡率 的相关性最强,而 APACHE II(AUC 0.935)和 SOFA(AUC 0.938)的辨别准确性最好。
解释与创新
意识障碍、ARDS 和 MODS 强烈预测了 重症登革热 队列研究 中的 死亡率,形成了早期风险分层的临床重要簇,这些单中心研究结果质疑以血小板减少症为中心的范式。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持从入住 ICU 起更密切地关注神经状态和器官功能障碍,以指导危重患者的 治疗 和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
由于单中心设计和 100 名连续患者的样本量有限,证据强度中等,可能会影响普适性。

Impaired Consciousness, Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS), and Multiorgan Dysfunction Syndrome (MODS) As the Triad of Mortality in Severe Dengue: Evidence From 100 Consecutive ICU Admissions

Authors: Akanksha Gupta, Sunita Gupta, Divyam Goyal, Neel S Dave
Original abstract (English)
Background Severe dengue is a leading contributor to intensive care unit (ICU) admissions and death in tropical regions. Identifying factors associated with poor outcomes early is critical for guiding treatment and allocating resources in critically ill patients. Methods This prospective observational cohort enrolled 100 consecutive patients with WHO 2009-defined severe dengue admitted to the ICU and Emergency Department of Maharishi Markandeshwar Institute of Medical Science and Research (MMIMSR), Mullana, Haryana, between March 2025 and January 2026. Clinical, laboratory, and severity-score data (Acute Physiology and Chronic Health Evaluation II (APACHE II), Sequential Organ Failure Assessment (SOFA)) were recorded and compared between survivors and non-survivors using appropriate statistical tests, relative risks (RR) with 95% CI, and ROC analysis. Results The mean age was 47.2 ± 17.5 years; 58% were male. ICU mortality was 21.0% (21/100). Thrombocytopenia showed no mortality link (p = 0.678). Higher pulse rate, respiratory rate, body temperature, total leucocyte count, creatinine, bilirubin, and lactate, together with lower blood pressure, Glasgow Coma scale (GCS) score, arterial pH, and PaO2, were each associated with ICU mortality (all p 2 cm was significant (50.0% vs. 17.0% mortality; RR 2.93, 95% CI 1.41-6.09; p = 0.024). Impaired consciousness (72.4% vs. 0% mortality; RR 103.20, 95% CI 6.46-1649.18; p < 0.001), acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS; 100% vs. 12.2%; RR 7.55, 95% CI 4.33-13.16; p < 0.001), and multiorgan dysfunction syndrome (MODS; 76.9% vs. 12.6%; RR 6.08, 95% CI 3.25-11.39; p = 0.002) showed the strongest associations (φ = 0.807, 0.647, 0.531 respectively). APACHE II (AUC 0.935) and SOFA (AUC 0.938) had the best discriminative accuracy, followed by lactate (AUC 0.835) and arterial pH (AUC 0.824). Conclusions Impaired consciousness, ARDS, and MODS strongly predicted mortality in this severe dengue cohort, forming a clinically vital cluster for early risk stratification. These single-center findings question the thrombocytopenia-centric paradigm and support closer attention to neurological status and organ dysfunction from ICU admission onward.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe dengue is a leading contributor to intensive care unit (ICU) admissions and death in tropical regions, and identifying factors associated with poor outcomes early is critical for guiding treatment and allocating resources in critically ill patients.
Design and population
This prospective observational cohort enrolled 100 consecutive patients with WHO 2009-defined severe dengue admitted to the ICU and Emergency Department of Maharishi Markandeshwar Institute of Medical Science and Research (MMIMSR), Mullana, Haryana, between March 2025 and January 2026.
Core methods
Clinical, laboratory, and severity-score data (Acute Physiology and Chronic Health Evaluation II (APACHE II), Sequential Organ Failure Assessment (SOFA)) were recorded and compared between survivors and non-survivors using appropriate statistical tests, relative risks (RR) with 95% CI, and ROC analysis.
Main results
ICU mortality was 21.0% (21/100), and thrombocytopenia showed no mortality link (p = 0.678). Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support closer attention to neurological status and organ dysfunction from ICU admission onward to guide treatment and resource allocation in critically ill patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the single-center design and limited sample size of 100 consecutive patients, which may affect generalisability.

使用 LSTM 网络进行深度学习用于登革热疾病预测和控制

Deep learning with LSTM networks for dengue disease forecasting and control
作者： Tahmid Nowsher, Shaiful Islam Arafat, Md. Kamrujjaman
摘要中文翻译摘要 准确预测传染病动态对于有效的公共卫生规划和早期 暴发 响应至关重要。传统的统计模型和现代深度学习方法都被广泛用于流行病学时间序列预测。登革热 仍然是孟加拉国的一个主要公共卫生问题,季节性 暴发 经常会产生急剧且不可预测的感染高峰。因此,可靠的预测模型对于预测感染趋势和支持早期干预策略非常重要。本研究使用 2010 年至 2022 年的 监测 数据调查了长短期记忆 (LSTM) 网络在预测孟加拉国每日登革热感染计数方面的性能。通过改变 LSTM 单元数量、回溯窗口大小和数据预处理策略,开发了多种 LSTM 架构。使用基于固定时间的数据分割,训练数据截至 2021 年 9 月 30 日,并对后续观察结果进行测试,以避免信息泄漏。将所提出的 LSTM 模型与几种基线预测方法进行了比较,包括 Naive、Seasonal-Naive 和 ARIMA 模型。使用标准预测指标评估模型性能,包括平均绝对误差 (MAE)、均方根误差 (RMSE) 和对称平均绝对百分比误差 (SMAPE)。实验结果表明,经典统计模型仍然是登革热预测的强有力基准。在所有评估的模型中,ARIMA(2,1,2)模型在测试数据集上取得了最好的预测性能,MAE为8.40,RMSE为14.72。性能最佳的 LSTM 配置产生了具有竞争力的结果,MAE 为 8.92,RMSE 为 15.87,优于朴素和季节朴素模型等简单基线方法。这些发现表明,虽然深度学习模型能够捕获复杂的时间模式,但对于该数据集,不能保证它们相对于经过良好调整的统计模型的优势。结果强调了在预测流行病学数据时将深度学习模型与经典时间序列方法进行基准测试的重要性。虽然 LSTM 网络在非线性时间动力学建模方面表现出良好的性能,但 ARIMA 模型为所研究的登革热数据集提供了最准确的预测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确预测传染病动态对于有效的公共卫生规划和早期 暴发 应对至关重要。登革热 仍然是孟加拉国的一个主要公共卫生问题,季节性 暴发 经常会产生急剧且不可预测的感染高峰。
设计与对象
本研究使用 2010 年至 2022 年的 监测 数据调查了长短期记忆 (LSTM) 网络在预测孟加拉国每日登革热感染计数方面的性能。通过改变 LSTM 单元数量、回溯窗口大小和数据预处理策略,开发了多种 LSTM 架构。
核心方法
使用标准预测指标评估模型性能,包括平均绝对误差 (MAE)、均方根误差 (RMSE) 和对称平均绝对百分比误差 (SMAPE)。使用基于固定时间的数据分割,训练数据截至 2021 年 9 月 30 日,并对后续观察结果进行测试,以避免信息泄漏。
主要结果
将所提出的 LSTM 模型与几种基线预测方法进行了比较,包括 Naive、Seasonal-Naive 和 ARIMA 模型。性能最佳的 LSTM 配置产生了具有竞争力的结果,MAE 为 8.92,RMSE 为 15.87,优于朴素和季节朴素模型等简单基线方法。ARIMA(2,1,2) 模型在测试数据集上取得了最佳预测性能,MAE 为 8.40,RMSE 为 14.72。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,虽然深度学习模型能够捕获复杂的时间模式,但对于该数据集,不能保证它们相对于经过良好调整的统计模型的优势。结果强调了在预测流行病学数据时将深度学习模型与经典时间序列方法进行基准测试的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果让我们深入了解 LSTM 网络在登革热预测中对非线性时间动态进行建模的潜力。研究结果强调需要仔细选择和验证模型,以支持季节性登革热 暴发 地区的早期干预策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。证据强度中等,因为该研究将 LSTM 模型与经典统计模型进行了比较,但没有提供有关潜在偏差或混杂因素的详细信息。

Deep learning with LSTM networks for dengue disease forecasting and control

Authors: Tahmid Nowsher, Shaiful Islam Arafat, Md. Kamrujjaman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Accurate forecasting of infectious disease dynamics is essential for effective public health planning and early outbreak response. Traditional statistical models and modern deep learning approaches have both been widely used for epidemiological time–series forecasting. Dengue fever remains a major public health concern in Bangladesh, where seasonal outbreaks often produce sharp and unpredictable infection spikes. Reliable forecasting models are therefore important for anticipating infection trends and supporting early intervention strategies. This study investigates the performance of Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for predicting daily dengue infection counts in Bangladesh using surveillance data from 2010 to 2022. Multiple LSTM architectures were developed by varying the number of LSTM units, lookback window size, and data preprocessing strategies. A fixed time-based data split was used, with training data up to 30 September 2021 and testing on subsequent observations to avoid information leakage. The proposed LSTM models were compared with several baseline forecasting approaches, including Naive, Seasonal-Naive, and ARIMA models. Model performance was evaluated using standard forecasting metrics including Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), and Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE). Experimental results indicate that classical statistical models remain strong benchmarks for dengue forecasting. Among all evaluated models, the ARIMA(2,1,2) model achieved the best predictive performance on the test dataset with MAE of 8.40 and RMSE of 14.72. The best-performing LSTM configuration produced competitive results with MAE of 8.92 and RMSE of 15.87, outperforming simple baseline approaches such as the Naive and Seasonal-Naive models. These findings suggest that while deep learning models are capable of capturing complex temporal patterns, their advantage over well-tuned statistical models is not guaranteed for this dataset. The results highlight the importance of benchmarking deep learning models against classical time-series approaches when forecasting epidemiological data. While LSTM networks demonstrate promising performance in modeling nonlinear temporal dynamics, the ARIMA model provided the most accurate predictions for the studied dengue dataset.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate forecasting of infectious disease dynamics is essential for effective public health planning and early outbreak response. Dengue fever remains a major public health concern in Bangladesh, where seasonal outbreaks often produce sharp and unpredictable infection spikes.
Design and population
This study investigates the performance of Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for predicting daily dengue infection counts in Bangladesh using surveillance data from 2010 to 2022. Multiple LSTM architectures were developed by varying the number of LSTM units, lookback window size, and data preprocessing strategies.
Core methods
Model performance was evaluated using standard forecasting metrics including Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), and Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE). A fixed time-based data split was used, with training data up to 30 September 2021 and testing on subsequent observations to avoid information leakage.
Main results
The proposed LSTM models were compared with several baseline forecasting approaches, including Naive, Seasonal-Naive, and ARIMA models. The best-performing LSTM configuration produced competitive results with MAE of 8.92 and RMSE of 15.87, outperforming simple baseline approaches such as the Naive and Seasonal-Naive models. The ARIMA(2,1,2) model achieved the best predictive performance on the test dataset with MAE of 8.40 and RMSE of 14.72.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that while deep learning models are capable of capturing complex temporal patterns, their advantage over well-tuned statistical models is not guaranteed for this dataset. The results highlight the importance of benchmarking deep learning models against classical time-series approaches when forecasting epidemiological data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the potential of LSTM networks for modeling nonlinear temporal dynamics in dengue forecasting. The findings emphasize the need for careful model selection and validation to support early intervention strategies in regions with seasonal dengue outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study compares LSTM models with classical statistical models but does not provide detailed information on potential biases or confounding factors.

孟加拉国三级护理医院收治的登革热患者的临床概况和 治疗 模式:一项以医院为基础的观察性研究

Clinical Profile and Treatment Patterns of Dengue Patients Admitted to a Tertiary Care Hospital in Bangladesh: a Hospital-Based Observational Study
作者： Deepankar Sutradhar, Shaila Kabir
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热在孟加拉国造成严重的公共卫生负担,通常需要住院接受支持性护理,但详细的临床和 治疗 数据仍然有限。本研究旨在描述住院登革热患者的临床表现、治疗 模式和社会人口学特征,为当地管理和控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
孟加拉国达卡的一家三级护理医院进行了一项以医院为基础的观察性研究,招募了研究期间入院的 205 名实验室确诊的登革热患者。该研究描述了这些患者的社会人口学特征、临床特征和住院趋势。
核心方法
该研究回顾性分析了医疗记录,以提取人口统计数据、临床症状、疾病严重程度分类和 治疗 方案。使用线性回归(R2 = 0.2505)根据人口变量对住院时间进行建模,以评估影响住院时间的因素。
主要结果
205例患者中,登革热占86.82%,登革出血热占10.24%,登革休克综合征占2.93%。最常见的症状是高烧(82.93%)、恶心呕吐(45.37%)、头痛(40%)、出血(21.95%)和休克(8.78%)。液体疗法是主要疗法,45.36% 的患者接受生理盐水加葡萄糖,32.68% 的患者接受 5% DNS。
解释与创新
该研究强调了住院病例中年轻人的主导地位,并强调了及时支持治疗(特别是液体复苏)在管理登革热方面的重要性。它提供了有关临床概况和 治疗 模式的当地相关数据,可以指导孟加拉国的登革热控制工作。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为基于医院的登革热管理方案提供信息,优先分配重症病例的资源,并支持有针对性的公共卫生干预措施,以减轻孟加拉国的住院负担。
局限与证据强度
作为一项单中心、回顾性观察研究,研究结果可能无法推广到其他环境,并且会受到选择和信息偏差的影响。回归模型的中等解释力(R2 = 0.2505)进一步限制了因果推断,需要谨慎解释。

Clinical Profile and Treatment Patterns of Dengue Patients Admitted to a Tertiary Care Hospital in Bangladesh: a Hospital-Based Observational Study

Authors: Deepankar Sutradhar, Shaila Kabir
Original abstract (English)
In Bangladesh, dengue is a serious public health issue that spreads quickly through mosquitoes and usually necessitates hospitalization for supportive care. The purpose of this hospital-based observational study was to assess the clinical presentation, treatment methods, hospitalization trends and sociodemographic profile of 205 dengue patients who were admitted to a tertiary care hospital in Dhaka. Young adults, aged 21 to 30 years, accounted for the highest percentage of cases, indicating their significant affliction. Dengue hemorrhagic fever (10.24%) and dengue shock syndrome (2.93%) were the next most common causes of hospitalization, after dengue fever (86.82%). Patients from outside of Dhaka constituted a larger percentage of severe cases and had longer hospital stays; part of the variation in hospitalization duration was explained by demographic characteristics (R2 = 0.2505). High fever (82.93%), nausea and vomiting (45.37%), headache (40%), bleeding (21.95%), and shock (8.78%) were the most frequent clinical symptoms. The mainstay of treatment was fluid therapy, with 45.36% of patients receiving normal saline along with dextrose preparations and 32.68% receiving 5% DNS. When clinically necessary, antihistamines, antibiotics and antiemetics were taken in addition to paracetamol (82.93%), multivitamin supplements (90.73%) and Carica papaya leaf extract (75.6%). To enhance outcomes and direct dengue control methods in Bangladesh, the data from this research highlights current hospitalization trends and stresses the significance of prompt, supportive care in the clinical management of dengue cases. Bangladesh Pharmaceutical Journal 29(2): 215-227, 2026 (July)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue poses a serious public health burden in Bangladesh, often requiring hospitalization for supportive care, yet detailed clinical and treatment data remain limited. This study aimed to characterize the clinical presentation, treatment patterns, and sociodemographic profile of hospitalized dengue patients to inform local management and control strategies.
Design and population
A hospital-based observational study was conducted at a tertiary care hospital in Dhaka, Bangladesh, enrolling 205 laboratory-confirmed dengue patients admitted during the study period. The study described sociodemographic characteristics, clinical features, and hospitalization trends among these patients.
Core methods
The study retrospectively analyzed medical records to extract demographic data, clinical symptoms, disease severity classifications, and treatment regimens. Hospitalization duration was modeled against demographic variables using linear regression (R2 = 0.2505) to assess factors influencing length of stay.
Main results
Among 205 patients, dengue fever accounted for 86.82%, dengue hemorrhagic fever for 10.24%, and dengue shock syndrome for 2.93%. The most frequent symptoms were high fever (82.93%), nausea and vomiting (45.37%), headache (40%), bleeding (21.95%), and shock (8.78%). Fluid therapy was the mainstay, with 45.36% receiving normal saline with dextrose and 32.68% receiving 5% DNS.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the predominance of young adults among hospitalized cases and underscores the importance of prompt supportive care, particularly fluid resuscitation, in managing dengue. It provides locally relevant data on clinical profiles and treatment patterns that can guide dengue control efforts in Bangladesh.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform hospital-based dengue management protocols, prioritize resource allocation for severe cases, and support targeted public health interventions to reduce hospitalization burden in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single-center, retrospective observational study, the findings may not be generalizable to other settings and are subject to selection and information biases. The moderate explanatory power of the regression model (R2 = 0.2505) further limits causal inference, warranting cautious interpretation.

用于登革热 疫苗 开发的基于树枝状聚合物的多抗原肽 (MAP) 方法:在计算机和体内对安全性和有效性的见解

A Dendrimer-Based Multiple Antigenic Peptide (MAP) Approach for Dengue Vaccine Development: In Silico and In Vivo Insights on Safety and Effectiveness
作者： Amtul Wadood Wajeeha, Najam us Sahar Sadaf Zaidi, Deeba Amraiz, Naseeha Bibi, Mamuna Mukhtar, Sobia Asghar, Muhammad Tahir
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种迅速传播的蚊媒病毒性疾病,造成了重大的全球健康问题,特别是在热带和亚热带地区。由于缺乏针对所有四种 疫苗 血清型的普遍有效的 疫苗,因此需要继续探索新的 疫苗 策略。本研究在 BALB/c 小鼠中评估了 PrM 衍生的多抗原肽 (MAP) 疫苗 候选物。分子对接分析预测所选 PrM 衍生的 B 细胞表位与人类(人 BCR Fab 区 PDB ID:5IFH)和小鼠(小鼠 BCR PDB ID:8EMA)B 细胞受体之间存在良好的相互作用,支持其抗原潜力。ChemSketch 用于开发 PrM–MAP 疫苗 结构,然后进行商业合成。小鼠接受单次、双倍和三倍加强剂量的 疫苗,而对照组则接受 PBS 或单独的佐剂。通过ELISA测定体液免疫应答,并通过HEK293细胞的体外细胞毒性测定和体内主要器官的组织病理学检查评价安全性。与随后的加强剂量相比,用单次加强剂量免疫的小鼠产生了显着水平的 PrM 特异性抗体,这表明所引起的体液反应存在差异。ELISA 中的最佳涂层浓度证明了抗原浓度对测定灵敏度的重要性。安全性评估表明 HEK293 细胞具有高细胞活力,PrM-MAP 高达 150 μg/mL,并且在治疗动物中不存在明显的组织病理学损伤。PrM-MAP 疫苗 构建体引发了可检测的体液 免疫应答,同时在体外和体内表现出高安全性。这些发现表明基于 MAP 的 疫苗 平台作为针对 登革病毒 进一步开发的有希望的候选者的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 构成了一个重大的全球健康问题,并且缺乏针对所有四种 登革病毒 血清型的普遍有效的 疫苗,因此需要继续探索新的 疫苗 策略。
设计与对象
这项研究在 BALB/c 小鼠中评估了 PrM 衍生的多抗原肽 (MAP) 疫苗 候选药物,这些小鼠接受单次、双倍和三倍加强剂量,而对照组接受 PBS 或单独的佐剂。
核心方法
ChemSketch 用于开发 PrM–MAP 疫苗 构建体,该构建体是商业合成的,并通过与人和小鼠 B 细胞受体的分子对接分析进行评估。通过ELISA测定体液免疫应答,并通过HEK293细胞的体外细胞毒性测定和体内主要器官的组织病理学检查评价安全性。
主要结果
与随后的加强剂量相比,用单次加强剂量免疫的小鼠产生了显着水平的 PrM 特异性抗体,这表明体液反应存在差异。安全性评估表明 HEK293 细胞具有高细胞活力,PrM-MAP 高达 150 μg/mL,并且在治疗动物中不存在明显的组织病理学损伤。
解释与创新
PrM-MAP 疫苗 构建体引发了可检测的体液 免疫应答,同时在体外和体内表现出高安全性,表明基于 MAP 的 疫苗 平台作为针对 登革病毒 进一步开发的有希望的候选者的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,基于 MAP 的 疫苗 平台是针对 登革病毒 进一步开发的有希望的候选平台,为安全有效的免疫提供了潜在的策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、偏见、混杂因素或对研究动物模型之外的结果的普遍性的详细评估。

A Dendrimer-Based Multiple Antigenic Peptide (MAP) Approach for Dengue Vaccine Development: In Silico and In Vivo Insights on Safety and Effectiveness

Authors: Amtul Wadood Wajeeha, Najam us Sahar Sadaf Zaidi, Deeba Amraiz, Naseeha Bibi, Mamuna Mukhtar, Sobia Asghar, Muhammad Tahir
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever, a rapidly spreading mosquito-borne viral disease, poses a major global health issue, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions. The absence of a universally effective vaccine against all four dengue virus serotypes necessitates continued exploration of novel vaccine strategies. This study evaluated a PrM-derived multiple antigenic peptide (MAP) vaccine candidate in BALB/c mice. Molecular docking analyses predicted favourable interactions of the selected PrM-derived B-cell epitope with both human (human BCR Fab region PDB ID: 5IFH) and mouse (mouse BCR PDB ID: 8EMA) B-cell receptors, supporting its antigenic potential. ChemSketch was used to develop the PrM–MAP vaccine construct, which was then commercially synthesized. Mice received single, double, and triple booster doses of the vaccine, while control groups received either PBS or adjuvant alone. Humoral immune responses were measured by ELISA, and safety was evaluated through in vitro cytotoxicity assays on HEK293 cells and the in vivo histopathological examination of major organs. Mice immunized with a single booster dose produced significant levels of PrM-specific antibodies compared to subsequent booster doses, suggesting variability in the humoral response elicited. Optimal coating concentrations in ELISA demonstrated the importance of antigen concentrations in the sensitivity of the assay. The safety evaluation indicated high cell viability, up to 150 μg/mL of PrM–MAP, in HEK293 cells, and an absence of significant histopathological damage in treated animals. The PrM–MAP vaccine construct elicited a detectable humoral immune response while demonstrating a high safety profile in vitro and in vivo. These findings indicate the potential of MAP-based vaccine platforms as promising candidates for further development against the dengue virus.
View research seven-element analysis

Design and population

Core methods

Humoral immune responses were measured by ELISA, and safety was evaluated through in vitro cytotoxicity assays on HEK293 cells and in vivo histopathological examination of major organs.
Main results
Mice immunized with a single booster dose produced significant levels of PrM-specific antibodies compared to subsequent booster doses, suggesting variability in the humoral response. The safety evaluation indicated high cell viability, up to 150 μg/mL of PrM–MAP, in HEK293 cells, and an absence of significant histopathological damage in treated animals.
Interpretation and novelty
The PrM–MAP vaccine construct elicited a detectable humoral immune response while demonstrating a high safety profile in vitro and in vivo, indicating the potential of MAP-based vaccine platforms as promising candidates for further development against the dengue virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that MAP-based vaccine platforms are promising candidates for further development against the dengue virus, offering a potential strategy for safe and effective immunization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, confounding, or a detailed assessment of the generalizability of the results beyond the studied animal model.

孟加拉国登革热风险的时空动态:基于 GIS 的方法。

Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Dengue Risk in Bangladesh: A GIS Based Approach.
作者： Nusrat Zahan Jarin, Abinash Bhattachan, Obaidur Rahman
摘要中文翻译登革热俗称断骨热,自2000年以来在孟加拉国流行。季风条件和温暖的气温为媒介埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊创造了适宜的繁殖环境。尽管登革热 暴发 在季风后几个月(9 月和 10 月)达到峰值,但 2023 年出现了不寻常的转变,病例最早在 6 月达到峰值。2023年的病死率(0.54%)是过去二十年来最高的。本研究使用 2019 年至 2024 年的每日登革热病例数据,对整个孟加拉国的登革热 发病率 集群的时空动态和复合风险进行了详尽的检查。空间自相关分析(Local Moran's I)显示,中部和南部沿海地区,特别是达卡、马尼克甘杰和巴里萨尔是 暴发 高峰年(2019 年,2019 年)的主要登革热热点地区。2023 年和 2024 年)。杰卡德相似指数 (JSI) 分析进一步表明,登革热热点存在强烈的季节性和年际重组,特别是在季风后季节,2020 年之后沿海地区的热点集中度不断增加。为了评估危害、脆弱性和风险,我们应用了基于 GIS 的多标准决策模型 (MCDM) 框架,并比较了加权(基于 AHP)和未加权方法。在预测登革热风险区方面,加权 AHP 模型 (AUC = 0.75) 比未加权模型 (AUC = 0.55) 更准确。负二项混合效应回归显示,滞后的温度、降水和人口密度增加了登革热 发病率,而较高的归一化差异累积指数与较低的 发病率 相关。这些发现强调需要针对高风险地区和平等的医疗保健机会制定综合登革热控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2000 年以来,登革热在孟加拉国流行,本研究旨在研究全国范围内登革热 发病率 集群和复合风险的时空动态。
设计与对象
该研究使用孟加拉国 2019 年至 2024 年的每日登革热病例数据来分析时空动态和风险因素。
核心方法
应用空间自相关分析 (Local Moran's I)、杰卡德相似指数 (JSI)、基于 GIS 的多标准决策模型 (MCDM) 和负二项式混合效应回归来评估登革热风险和相关因素。
主要结果
登革热 暴发 在 9 月和 10 月达到高峰,但在 2023 年,病例最早在 6 月就达到高峰;2023年病死率为0.54%,为二十年来最高;中部和南部沿海地区,特别是达卡、马尼克甘杰和巴里萨尔,是主要热点地区;加权 AHP 模型 (AUC = 0.75) 在预测风险区域方面比未加权模型 (AUC = 0.55) 更准确。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 2020 年后登革热 暴发 的异常变化以及沿海地区热点集中度的增加,强调需要采取有针对性的控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为针对高风险地区和确保平等的医疗服务的综合登革热控制策略提供了见解。
局限与证据强度
尽管全面的时空分析和经过验证的模型支持可靠的结论,但证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于观测数据和模型。

Spatiotemporal Dynamics of Dengue Risk in Bangladesh: A GIS Based Approach.

Authors: Nusrat Zahan Jarin, Abinash Bhattachan, Obaidur Rahman
Original abstract (English)
Dengue, commonly known as breakbone fever, has been prevalent in Bangladesh since 2000. Monsoon conditions and warmer temperatures create suitable breeding environment for the vectors Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. Although dengue outbreaks peak during the post-monsoon months (September and October), 2023 showed an unusual shift, with cases peaking as early as June. The case fatality rate (0.54%) in 2023 was the highest in the past two decades. This study offers an exhaustive examination of the spatiotemporal dynamics of dengue incidence clusters and composite risk across Bangladesh using daily dengue case data from 2019 to 2024. Spatial autocorrelation analysis (Local Moran's I) revealed that the central and south coastal districts, especially Dhaka, Manikganj, and Barisal were the major dengue hotspots during the peak outbreak years (2019, 2023, and 2024). Jaccard Similarity Index (JSI) analysis further indicated strong seasonal and interannual reorganization of dengue hotspots, especially during the post-monsoon season, with increasing hotspot concentration in coastal districts after 2020. To assess hazard, vulnerability and risk, we applied a GIS-based multi-criteria decision-making model (MCDM) framework and compared both weighted (AHP-based) and unweighted approaches. The weighted AHP model was more accurate (AUC = 0.75) than the unweighted model (AUC = 0.55) in predicting dengue risk zones. A negative binomial mixed-effects regression showed that lagged temperature, precipitation and population density increased dengue incidence, while higher normalized difference built-up index was associated with lower incidence. These findings emphasize the need for integrated dengue control strategies targeting high-risk areas and equal healthcare access.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue has been prevalent in Bangladesh since 2000, and this study aimed to examine the spatiotemporal dynamics of dengue incidence clusters and composite risk across the country.
Design and population
The study used daily dengue case data from 2019 to 2024 across Bangladesh to analyze spatiotemporal dynamics and risk factors.
Core methods
Spatial autocorrelation analysis (Local Moran's I), Jaccard Similarity Index (JSI), a GIS-based multi-criteria decision-making model (MCDM), and a negative binomial mixed-effects regression were applied to assess dengue risk and associated factors.
Main results
Dengue outbreaks peaked in September and October, but in 2023, cases peaked as early as June; the case fatality rate in 2023 was 0.54%, the highest in two decades; central and south coastal districts, especially Dhaka, Manikganj, and Barisal, were major hotspots; the weighted AHP model (AUC = 0.75) was more accurate than the unweighted model (AUC = 0.55) in predicting risk zones.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight unusual shifts in dengue outbreaks and increasing hotspot concentration in coastal districts after 2020, emphasizing the need for targeted control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights for integrated dengue control strategies targeting high-risk areas and ensuring equal healthcare access.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on observational data and models, though the comprehensive spatiotemporal analysis and validated models support robust conclusions.

与登革热感染相关的延迟血浆泄漏:病例报告

Delayed Plasma Leakage Associated with Dengue Infection: A Case Report
作者： Chamara Sarathchandra, Kosala Weerakoon, Sisira Siribaddana
摘要中文翻译登革出血热 (DHF) 的特点是疾病关键阶段的血浆渗漏,通常发生在疾病第 4 天和第 7 天之间退热期间。在此期间之后的延迟血浆渗漏很少被描述。我们报告了一例诊断上具有挑战性的登革热病例,患者来自登革热流行地区,一名先前健康的年轻女性,在患病第 13 天检测到血浆渗漏,增加了关键期延迟的可能性,但不能排除随后出现登革热感染的连续发热性疾病。患者最初出现高烧、呕吐、关节痛和肌痛,三天后消退。经过三天的短暂临床改善后,出现严重头痛、腹痛和反复呕吐,但没有再次发烧。她于第 12 天因血小板减少症和明显的转氨炎入院。发病第13天,超声检查显示肝肾袋和右侧胸膜腔内有游离液体,与血浆渗漏一致,而登革热IgM和IgG抗体呈阳性。该患者接受了 DHF 治疗,并进行了仔细的液体监测,并顺利康复。该病例强调了在流行地区患者中考虑登革热的重要性,即使症状不典型、无发热或暂时延迟。尽管关键期可能会延迟,但由于无法获得早期病毒学确认,因此不能排除随后出现登革热感染的连续发热性疾病。即使对于明显康复的患者,仔细监测血小板减少和血浆渗漏仍然至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热的特点是血浆渗漏,通常发生在患病第 4 天至第 7 天之间,但很少描述超过该时间段的延迟血浆渗漏。
设计与对象
这是一位来自登革热流行地区、之前健康的年轻女性的 病例报告,她出现了诊断上具有挑战性的登革热感染。
核心方法
使用临床评估、超声检查检测游离液体以及登革热 IgM 和 IgG 抗体的血清学检测对患者进行评估。
主要结果
发病第13天,超声检查显示肝肾袋和右侧胸膜腔内有游离液体,与血浆渗漏一致,登革热IgM和IgG抗体呈阳性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为登革热可能延迟的关键阶段,尽管他们指出,由于缺乏早期病毒学确认,不能排除登革热感染后发生的连续发热性疾病。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了在流行地区患者中考虑登革热的重要性,即使症状不典型、无发热或暂时延迟,并强调需要仔细监测血小板减少和血浆渗漏。
局限与证据强度
由于无法获得早期病毒学确认,因此证据强度较低,因此无法排除继发登革热感染而导致发热性疾病的可能性。

Delayed Plasma Leakage Associated with Dengue Infection: A Case Report

Authors: Chamara Sarathchandra, Kosala Weerakoon, Sisira Siribaddana
Original abstract (English)
Dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF) is characterized by plasma leakage during the critical phase of illness, typically occurring around defervescence between illness days 4 and 7. Delayed plasma leakage beyond this period is rarely described. We report a diagnostically challenging case of dengue in a previously healthy young woman from a dengue-endemic region, in whom plasma leakage was detected on illness day 13, raising the possibility of a delayed critical phase, though a sequential febrile illness followed by dengue infection could not be excluded. The patient initially developed high-grade fever, vomiting, arthralgia, and myalgia, which resolved after three days. After a three-day period of transient clinical improvement, severe headache, abdominal pain, and recurrent vomiting developed without recurrence of fever. She was admitted on day 12 with thrombocytopenia and marked transaminitis. On day 13 of illness, ultrasonography demonstrated free fluid in the hepatorenal pouch and right pleural cavity, consistent with plasma leakage, while dengue IgM and IgG antibodies were positive. The patient was managed as DHF with careful fluid monitoring and recovered uneventfully. This case highlights the importance of considering dengue in patients from endemic settings even when presentation is atypical, afebrile, or temporally delayed. Although a delayed critical phase is possible, sequential febrile illnesses with subsequent dengue infection could not be excluded because early virological confirmation was unavailable. Careful monitoring for thrombocytopenia and plasma leakage remains essential even in apparently recovering patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue haemorrhagic fever is characterized by plasma leakage typically occurring between illness days 4 and 7, but delayed plasma leakage beyond this period is rarely described.
Design and population
This is a case report of a previously healthy young woman from a dengue-endemic region who presented with a diagnostically challenging dengue infection.
Core methods
The patient was assessed using clinical evaluation, ultrasonography to detect free fluid, and serological testing for dengue IgM and IgG antibodies.
Main results
On illness day 13, ultrasonography showed free fluid in the hepatorenal pouch and right pleural cavity consistent with plasma leakage, while dengue IgM and IgG antibodies were positive.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a possible delayed critical phase of dengue, though they noted that sequential febrile illnesses followed by dengue infection could not be excluded due to the lack of early virological confirmation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case highlights the importance of considering dengue in patients from endemic settings even when presentation is atypical, afebrile, or temporally delayed, and emphasizes the need for careful monitoring for thrombocytopenia and plasma leakage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low because early virological confirmation was unavailable, making it impossible to exclude the possibility of sequential febrile illnesses with subsequent dengue infection.
