利用 抗体依赖性增强 确定登革热-寨卡病毒多病原体系统的灭绝途径

Extinction pathways in dengue-Zika multi-pathogen systems with antibody-dependent enhancement
作者： Richmond Balinia Adda
摘要中文翻译简介 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 创建了复杂的多病原体动力学,从根本​​上改变了共同传播的登革热和寨卡病毒的灭绝概率。方法 我们开发了一个具有不对称 ADE 的五种共循环病原体(登革热血清型 1 -4 和寨卡病毒)的随机框架。使用 WKB 大偏差理论,我们通过最小作用、微移弹性带和自适应 SVD 方法计算灭绝作用和最可能的路径。WKB 操作根据蒙特卡罗模拟进行验证(Pearson r = 0.98,最大相对误差 0.3%)。结果 该模型预测 DENV4 是一种关键病原体:它的消除会引发级联反应(DENV4 → DENV2 → Zika),其可能性比直接多病原体灭绝的可能性高出指数级。参数空间分为三种消光状态——顺序、成对和完全崩溃——滞后接近 η≈2.8 敏感性分析显示基石身份在 ±20% ADE 矩阵扰动下是稳定的。在对称不变流形上的对称 n 病原体极限中,单血清型灭绝作用以 1/n 的形式减少,而全局消除作用以 n ·S sym 的形式增长。讨论 在这种对称理想化中,这为理解血清型多样性如何同时促进局部脆弱性和全球持久性提供了可能的机械参考点。在完全不对称的登革热-寨卡病毒系统中是否存在类似的关系是一个悬而未决的问题。局限性包括直接 传播 近似(定量误差 < 15%)和时间平均 ADE。所有预测都是基于模型的,需要经验验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 创建了复杂的多病原体动力学,从根本​​上改变了共同传播的登革热和寨卡病毒的灭绝概率。
设计与对象
该研究为五种共同循环的病原体(包括登革热血清型 1-4 和具有不对称 ADE 的寨卡病毒)开发了一个随机框架。
核心方法
使用 WKB 大偏差理论通过最小作用、微移弹性带和自适应 SVD 方法计算灭绝作用和最可能的路径,并根据蒙特卡罗模拟验证 WKB 作用。
主要结果
该模型预测 DENV4 作为关键病原体,其消除会触发级联(DENV4 → DENV2 → Zika),其概率比直接多病原体灭绝的可能性高出指数级,参数空间分为三个灭绝机制,并且关键身份在 ±20% ADE 矩阵扰动下保持稳定。
解释与创新
在对称理想化的情况下,单一血清型灭绝行动减少了 1/n,而全球消除行动却在增长,这一发现为理解血清型多样性如何同时促进局部脆弱性和全球持久性提供了机械参考点。
科研与公卫意义
所提供的证据并未报告对 监测、诊断、治疗、疫苗接种、生态学、风险评估、预防或未来研究的具体影响。
局限与证据强度
局限性包括直接 传播 近似和时间平均 ADE,并且所有预测都是基于模型的,需要经验验证,表明证据强度较低。

Extinction pathways in dengue-Zika multi-pathogen systems with antibody-dependent enhancement

Authors: Richmond Balinia Adda
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) creates complex multi-pathogen dynamics that fundamentally alter extinction probabilities in co-circulating dengue and Zika viruses. Methods We develop a stochastic framework for five co-circulating pathogens (dengue serotypes 1 -4 and Zika) with asymmetric ADE. Using WKB large-deviation theory, we compute extinction actions and most-probable pathways via Minimum Action, Nudged Elastic Band, and adaptive SVD methods. WKB actions are validated against Monte Carlo simulations (Pearson r = 0.98, max relative error 0.3%). Results The model predicts that DENV4 acts as a keystone pathogen: its elimination triggers a cascade (DENV4 → DENV2 → Zika) that is exponentially more probable than direct multi-pathogen extinction. The parameter space separates into three extinction regimes –sequential, pairwise, and total collapse –with hysteresis near η≈2.8 Sensitivity analysis shows keystone identity is stable under ±20% ADE matrix perturbations. In a symmetric n-pathogen limit on the symmetric invariant manifold, single-serotype extinction action decreases as 1/n while global elimination action grows as n ·S sym . Discussion Within this symmetric idealization, this provides a possible mechanistic reference point for understanding how serotype diversity could simultaneously promote local vulnerability and global persistence. Whether a similar relationship holds in the fully asymmetric dengue-Zika system is an open question. Limitations include the direct-transmission approximation (quantitative error < 15%) and time-averaged ADE. All predictions are model-based and require empirical validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) creates complex multi-pathogen dynamics that fundamentally alter extinction probabilities in co-circulating dengue and Zika viruses.
Design and population
The study develops a stochastic framework for five co-circulating pathogens consisting of dengue serotypes 1-4 and Zika virus with asymmetric ADE.
Core methods
Extinction actions and most-probable pathways were computed using WKB large-deviation theory via Minimum Action, Nudged Elastic Band, and adaptive SVD methods, with WKB actions validated against Monte Carlo simulations.
Main results
The model predicts that DENV4 acts as a keystone pathogen where its elimination triggers a cascade (DENV4 → DENV2 → Zika) that is exponentially more probable than direct multi-pathogen extinction, with parameter space separating into three extinction regimes and keystone identity stable under ±20% ADE matrix perturbations.
Interpretation and novelty
Within a symmetric idealization, the finding that single-serotype extinction action decreases as 1/n while global elimination action grows provides a mechanistic reference point for understanding how serotype diversity could simultaneously promote local vulnerability and global persistence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not report specific implications for surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, vaccination, ecology, risk assessment, prevention or future study.
Limitations and evidence strength
Limitations include the direct-transmission approximation and time-averaged ADE, and all predictions are model-based and require empirical validation, indicating low evidence strength.

使用基于注意力的 LSTM 模型和 Honey Badger 优化进行超参数调整,改进登革热 暴发 的预测

Improving Prediction of Dengue Outbreaks Using Attention-based LSTM Model with Honey Badger Optimization for Hyperparameter Tuning
作者： Pudadera, Sombero, Dollaga, Sueno
摘要中文翻译气候变化对当前疾病动态构成重大挑战 暴发。本研究使用基于注意力的 LSTM 模型改进了 暴发 预测,该模型经蜜獾算法 (HBA) 优化用于超参数调整。该模型利用 2015 年至 2024 年南哥打巴托省 Koronadal 不同描笼涯的疾病、气候和地理数据,预测 1、3、6 和 12 个月的 发病率。注意力机制增强了长期模式检测,而 HBA 减少了过度拟合并提高了准确性。结果显示,与标准 LSTM 相比,HBA-LSTM 的均方误差降低了 43.7%,与注意力模型相比,均方误差降低了 22.2%。RMSE、MAE 和 MAPE 也有类似的降低。虽然有效,但建议进一步调整和替代架构以改进泛化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
气候变化对疾病 暴发 当前的动态提出了重大挑战,需要改进预测模型。
设计与对象
该研究利用了 2015 年至 2024 年在南哥打巴托省科罗纳达尔不同描笼涯收集的疾病、气候和地理数据。
核心方法
开发了一种基于注意力的 LSTM 模型,并通过 Honey Badger 算法 (HBA) 进行了超参数调整优化,用于预测 1、3、6 和 12 个月范围内的 发病率。
主要结果
HBA-LSTM 模型的均方误差比标准 LSTM 降低了 43.7%,比注意力模型降低了 22.2%,在 RMSE、MAE 和 MAPE 中也观察到了类似的降低。
解释与创新
与标准方法相比,注意力机制增强了长期模式检测,而 HBA 减少了过度拟合并提高了准确性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,优化后的模型可以为改进的登革热 监测 预测和 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管有效,但作者建议需要进一步调整和替代架构来提高泛化能力,这表明这份仅摘要报告的证据强度中等。

Improving Prediction of Dengue Outbreaks Using Attention-based LSTM Model with Honey Badger Optimization for Hyperparameter Tuning

Authors: Pudadera, Sombero, Dollaga, Sueno
Original abstract (English)
Climate Change Poses a Significant Challenge to the Current Dynamics of Disease Outbreaks. This Study Improves Outbreak Prediction Using an Attention-Based LSTM Model Optimized by the Honey Badger Algorithm (HBA) for Hyperparameter Tuning. Using Disease, Climate, and Geographic Data From 2015–2024 in Different Barangays in Koronadal, South Cotabato, the Model Predicts Incidence Over 1-, 3-, 6-, and 12-Month Horizons. Attention Mechanisms Enhanced Long-Term Pattern Detection, While HBA Reduces Overfitting and Boosts Accuracy. Results Show the HBA-LSTM Reduces Mean Squared Error by 43.7% Over Standard LSTM and 22.2% Over Attention Models. Similar Reductions are Seen in RMSE, MAE, and MAPE. Though Effective, Further Tuning and Alternative Architectures are Suggested for Improved Generalization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Climate change poses a significant challenge to the current dynamics of disease outbreaks, necessitating improved prediction models.
Design and population
The study utilized disease, climate, and geographic data from 2015 to 2024 collected in different barangays in Koronadal, South Cotabato.
Core methods
An attention-based LSTM model optimized by the Honey Badger Algorithm (HBA) for hyperparameter tuning was developed to predict incidence over 1-, 3-, 6-, and 12-month horizons.
Main results
The HBA-LSTM model reduced mean squared error by 43.7% over standard LSTM and 22.2% over attention models, with similar reductions observed in RMSE, MAE, and MAPE.
Interpretation and novelty
Attention mechanisms enhanced long-term pattern detection, while HBA reduced overfitting and boosted accuracy compared to standard approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the optimized model can inform improved outbreak prediction and surveillance strategies for dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although effective, the authors suggest further tuning and alternative architectures are needed for improved generalization, indicating moderate evidence strength from this abstract-only report.

包膜二聚体表位 1 (EDE1) 抗体 (C10) 治疗 可显着减少寨卡病毒在男性和女性生殖道中的复制。

Envelope-dimer epitope 1 (EDE1) antibody (C10) treatment significantly reduces Zika virus replication in the male and female reproductive tracts.
作者： Franck Amblard, Shajer Manzoor, Nathaniel J. Schramm, Martina Kovářová, Adam S. Cockrell, J Victor Garcia, Angela Wahl, Ralph S Baric, Leda Bassit, Rae Ann Spagnuolo
摘要中文翻译不存在有效的疗法或 疫苗 来预防下一个寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 暴发。寨卡病毒已在感染者的多个器官中检测到,包括大脑、眼睛和生殖道等免疫豁免部位。ZIKV 在生殖道中的复制受到高度关注;ZIKV 可通过性传播或传播给孕妇发育中的胎儿,导致严重的先天缺陷。在这里,我们发现感染 ZIKV 的免疫活性小鼠迅速控制了病毒血症,并且在 T 细胞耗尽后没有病毒反弹。相比之下,B 细胞和 T 细胞遗传缺陷小鼠(免疫缺陷小鼠)支持 ZIKV 在所有检查组织中持续复制。使用新型核苷类似物 7-脱氮-7-氟-2'-C-甲基腺苷 (DFMA) 对 ZIKV 感染的免疫缺陷小鼠进行 治疗 治疗,可显着减少病毒血症并延长生存期,从而验证免疫缺陷小鼠对 ZIKV 预防和治疗方法的有效性研究。重要的是,我们证明,使用 登革病毒 交叉中和抗体(EDE1、C10)对感染 ZIKV 的动物进行 治疗 抑制了 ZIKV 的全身复制,包括在大脑、眼睛以及男性和女性生殖道中的复制。重要性自 2007 年以来,已有 80 多个国家和地区记录了寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 感染,导致了两个主要的到目前为止 暴发。ZIKV 在生殖道中的复制受到高度关注,因为 ZIKV 可以通过性传播或传播给孕妇发育中的胎儿,从而导致严重的先天缺陷。目前,尚无有效的疗法或 疫苗 可以预防下一个 暴发。在这里,我们开发了一种 ZIKV 感染的临床前动物模型,用于评估 登革病毒 交叉中和抗体预防/治疗 ZIKV 感染的功效。该抗体抑制了血液和组织(包括生殖道)中的病毒复制,这表明在未来的 ZIKV 暴发 高危人群中可以使用被动施用 ZIKV 中和抗体来预防 ZIKV 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前尚无有效的疗法或 疫苗 可以预防下一个寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 暴发,并且由于 传播 的性风险和严重的先天缺陷,生殖道中的 ZIKV 复制受到高度关注。
设计与对象
该研究使用免疫功能正常的小鼠和遗传免疫缺陷的小鼠开发了 ZIKV 感染的临床前动物模型,以评估 登革病毒 交叉中和抗体的功效。
核心方法
研究人员评估了 登革病毒 交叉中和抗体(EDE1、C10)和核苷类似物 7-脱氮杂-7-氟-2'-C-甲基腺苷 (DFMA) 在 ZIKV 感染小鼠中的功效,测量了病毒血症、存活率和组织中病毒复制等结果。
主要结果
使用 DFMA 治疗 ZIKV 感染的免疫缺陷小鼠的 治疗 可显着减少病毒血症并延长生存期,而使用 EDE1 抗体的 治疗 则可抑制血液、大脑、眼睛以及男性和女性生殖道中的全身 ZIKV 复制。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为验证免疫缺陷小鼠的功效研究,并证明 登革病毒 交叉中和抗体可以抑制 ZIKV 在免疫特权位点的复制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,在未来的 ZIKV 暴发 高危人群中,可以使用被动施用 ZIKV 中和抗体来预防 ZIKV 传播。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告具体的设计限制,但证据强度被评估为中等,因为数据来自仅抽象的临床前动物模型。

Envelope-dimer epitope 1 (EDE1) antibody (C10) treatment significantly reduces Zika virus replication in the male and female reproductive tracts.

Authors: Franck Amblard, Shajer Manzoor, Nathaniel J. Schramm, Martina Kovářová, Adam S. Cockrell, J Victor Garcia, Angela Wahl, Ralph S Baric, Leda Bassit, Rae Ann Spagnuolo
Original abstract (English)
No effective therapy or vaccine exists to protect against the next Zika virus (ZIKV) outbreak. ZIKV has been detected in multiple organs of infected people, including immune-privileged sites like the brain, eyes, and reproductive tract. ZIKV replication in the reproductive tract is of high concern; ZIKV can be transmitted sexually or to the developing fetus of pregnant women, resulting in severe congenital defects. Here, we show that ZIKV-infected immunocompetent mice rapidly controlled viremia with no viral rebound following T cell depletion. In contrast, mice genetically deficient in B and T cells (immunodeficient mice) supported sustained ZIKV replication in all tissues examined. Treatment of ZIKV-infected immunodeficient mice with the novel nucleoside analog 7-deaza-7-fluoro-2'-C-methyladenosine (DFMA) significantly reduced viremia and prolonged survival, validating immunodeficient mice for efficacy studies of ZIKV prevention and therapeutic approaches. Importantly, we demonstrate that treatment of ZIKV-infected animals with a dengue virus cross-neutralizing antibody (EDE1, C10) suppressed systemic ZIKV replication, including in the brain, eye, and male and female reproductive tracts.IMPORTANCESince 2007, Zika virus (ZIKV) infections have been documented in over 80 countries and territories, resulting in two major outbreaks thus far. ZIKV replication in the reproductive tract is of high concern as ZIKV can be transmitted sexually or to the developing fetus of pregnant women, resulting in severe congenital defects. Currently, no effective therapy or vaccine exists to protect against the next outbreak. Here, we developed a preclinical animal model for ZIKV infection that we used to evaluate the efficacy of a dengue virus cross-neutralizing antibody for prevention/treatment of ZIKV infection. The antibody suppressed virus replication in blood and tissues, including the reproductive tract, suggesting that passive administration of ZIKV neutralizing antibodies could be used during future ZIKV outbreaks in high-risk populations to prevent ZIKV transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
No effective therapy or vaccine exists to protect against the next Zika virus (ZIKV) outbreak, and ZIKV replication in the reproductive tract is of high concern due to risks of sexual transmission and severe congenital defects.
Design and population
The study developed a preclinical animal model for ZIKV infection using immunocompetent mice and genetically immunodeficient mice to evaluate the efficacy of a dengue virus cross-neutralizing antibody.
Core methods
Researchers evaluated the efficacy of a dengue virus cross-neutralizing antibody (EDE1, C10) and the nucleoside analog 7-deaza-7-fluoro-2'-C-methyladenosine (DFMA) in ZIKV-infected mice, measuring outcomes including viremia, survival, and viral replication in tissues.
Main results
Treatment of ZIKV-infected immunodeficient mice with DFMA significantly reduced viremia and prolonged survival, while treatment with the EDE1 antibody suppressed systemic ZIKV replication in the blood, brain, eye, and male and female reproductive tracts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as validating immunodeficient mice for efficacy studies and demonstrating that a dengue virus cross-neutralizing antibody can suppress ZIKV replication in immune-privileged sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that passive administration of ZIKV neutralizing antibodies could be used during future ZIKV outbreaks in high-risk populations to prevent ZIKV transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, but the evidence strength is assessed as moderate because the data are derived from an abstract-only preclinical animal model.

巴西亚马逊发热患者中虫媒病毒的综合 监测 揭示了复杂的共循环动力学和隐藏的病毒多样性

Integrated surveillance of arboviruses in febrile patients from the Brazilian Amazon reveals complex co-circulation dynamics and hidden viral diversity
作者： Karolina Jeaneth Solórzano Chavarría, Erika Oliveira Gomes, Bárbara Aparecida Chaves, Vanderson Souza Sampaio, Alexandre Vilhena Silva-Neto, Daniela Brito, Luís Felipe Alho da Silva, Maianne Yasmin Oliveira Dias, Lívia Sacchetto, Victória Bernardi
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:虫媒病毒感染仍然是巴西亚马逊地区的一个重大公共卫生挑战。重叠的症状、有限的实验室访问以及多种虫媒病毒的传播阻碍了临床 诊断。本研究旨在描述巴西马瑙斯发热患者虫媒病毒感染的流行病学、临床、实验室和基因组特征,并利用宏基因组测序探索其他病毒因子。方法:2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 2 月在巴西马瑙斯的一个三级参考中心进行了一项横断面研究。年龄≥5岁、出现皮疹、发烧或发烧史持续<7天且疟疾厚血涂片呈阴性的患者被纳入研究。使用 ELISA 和 RT-qPCR 检测血清样本中的 登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV)、黄热病病毒 (YF)、奥罗普切病毒 (OROV) 和 Mayaro 病毒 (MAYV)。对阳性样本进行基于扩增子的 基因组 测序以进行系统发育分析。使用从头鸟枪宏基因组测序分析 RT-qPCR 阴性样本的子集。结果:在 708 名入组参与者中,243 名(34.3%)有实验室确诊的虫媒病毒感染:92 名(37.9%)DENV、64 名(26.3%)CHIKV 和 4 名(1.6%)ZIKV,而 83 名(34.2%)实验室资料符合双重感染,主要是 DENV+CHIKV(55/83;66.3%)。确定了 DENV-1 基因型 V 和 DENV-2 基因型 III(亚裔美国人)和 II(国际大都会)的循环。对 35 个样本的宏基因组分析检测到人型佩吉病毒和灵长类红细小病毒 1. 结论:这些发现表明马瑙斯存在复杂的虫媒病毒共循环,并支持结合分子、血清学和基因组方法的 监测 综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒感染是巴西亚马逊地区的一个重大公共卫生挑战,重叠的症状和有限的实验室访问阻碍了临床 诊断。本研究旨在描述巴西马瑙斯发热患者虫媒病毒感染的流行病学、临床、实验室和基因组特征,并利用宏基因组测序探索其他病毒因子。
设计与对象
2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 2 月在巴西马瑙斯的一个三级参考中心进行了一项横断面研究。该研究招募了 708 名 5 岁或以上的参与者,这些参与者均出现皮疹、发烧或发烧史持续不到 7 天,所有参与者的疟疾厚血涂片均呈阴性。
核心方法
使用 ELISA 和 RT-qPCR 检测血清样本中的 登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV)、黄热病病毒 (YF)、奥罗普切病毒 (OROV) 和 Mayaro 病毒 (MAYV)。阳性样本接受基于扩增子的 基因组 测序进行系统发育分析,而 RT-qPCR 阴性样本的子集则使用从头鸟枪法宏基因组测序进行分析。
主要结果
在 708 名登记参与者中,243 名(34.3%)人患有实验室确诊的虫媒病毒感染,其中包括 92 名(37.9%)登革热病毒病例。该研究确定了 DENV-1 基因型 V 和 DENV-2 基因型 III(亚裔美国人)和 II(大都会)的循环。此外,83 个 (34.2%) 实验室资料与双重感染相符,主要是 DENV+CHIKV (55/83;66.3%)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,马瑙斯存在复杂的虫媒病毒共循环,其特征是同时存在多种 DENV 基因型以及 DENV 和 CHIKV 双重感染的高频率。作者将这些结果解释为支持结合分子、血清学和基因组方法的综合 监测 策略必要性的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持实施结合分子、血清学和基因组方法的综合 监测 策略来管理虫媒病毒威胁。这些策略对于理解复杂的 传播 动力学和改善具有多种共循环虫媒病毒的区域的临床 诊断 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的研究局限性或证据强度的详细评估。证据仅来自摘要,这限制了评估潜在偏差、混杂因素或研究结果在研究背景之外的普遍性的能力。

Integrated surveillance of arboviruses in febrile patients from the Brazilian Amazon reveals complex co-circulation dynamics and hidden viral diversity

Authors: Karolina Jeaneth Solórzano Chavarría, Erika Oliveira Gomes, Bárbara Aparecida Chaves, Vanderson Souza Sampaio, Alexandre Vilhena Silva-Neto, Daniela Brito, Luís Felipe Alho da Silva, Maianne Yasmin Oliveira Dias, Lívia Sacchetto, Victória Bernardi
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background: Arboviral infections continue to be a significant public health challenge in the Brazilian Amazon. Overlapping symptoms, limited laboratory access, and the circulation of multiple arboviruses hamper clinical diagnosis. This study aimed to characterize the epidemiological, clinical, laboratory and genomic profiles of arboviral infections in febrile patients in Manaus, Brazil, and explore additional viral agents using metagenomic sequencing. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted between February 2021 and February 2023 at a tertiary reference center in Manaus, Brazil. Patients aged ≥ 5 years of age presenting with a rash and either a fever or a history of fever lasting <7 days and a negative thick blood smear for malaria were enrolled. Serum samples were tested for dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), yellow fever virus (YF), Oropouche virus (OROV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) using ELISA and RT-qPCR. Positive samples were subjected to amplicon-based genome sequencing for phylogenetic analysis. A subset of RT-qPCR negative samples was analyzed using de novo shotgun metagenomic sequencing. Results: Among the 708 enrolled participants, 243 (34.3%) had a laboratory-confirmed arboviral infection: 92 (37.9%) DENV, 64 (26.3%) CHIKV, and 4 (1.6%) ZIKV, while 83 (34.2%) laboratory profiles were compatible with coinfection, predominantly DENV+CHIKV (55/83; 66.3%). Circulation of DENV-1 genotype V and DENV-2 genotypes III (Asian American) and II (Cosmopolitan) was identified. Metagenomic analysis of 35 samples detected Pegivirus hominis and Erythroparvovirus primate 1. Conclusions: These findings demonstrate complex arbovirus co-circulation in Manaus and support integrated surveillance strategies combining molecular, serological, and genomic approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviral infections are a significant public health challenge in the Brazilian Amazon, where overlapping symptoms and limited laboratory access hamper clinical diagnosis. This study aimed to characterize the epidemiological, clinical, laboratory, and genomic profiles of arboviral infections in febrile patients in Manaus, Brazil, and explore additional viral agents using metagenomic sequencing.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between February 2021 and February 2023 at a tertiary reference center in Manaus, Brazil. The study enrolled 708 participants aged 5 years or older presenting with a rash and either a fever or a history of fever lasting less than 7 days, all of whom had a negative thick blood smear for malaria.
Core methods
Serum samples were tested for dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), yellow fever virus (YF), Oropouche virus (OROV), and Mayaro virus (MAYV) using ELISA and RT-qPCR. Positive samples underwent amplicon-based genome sequencing for phylogenetic analysis, while a subset of RT-qPCR negative samples was analyzed using de novo shotgun metagenomic sequencing.
Main results
Among the 708 enrolled participants, 243 (34.3%) had a laboratory-confirmed arboviral infection, including 92 (37.9%) cases of DENV. The study identified the circulation of DENV-1 genotype V and DENV-2 genotypes III (Asian American) and II (Cosmopolitan). Additionally, 83 (34.2%) laboratory profiles were compatible with coinfection, predominantly DENV+CHIKV (55/83; 66.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate complex arbovirus co-circulation in Manaus, characterized by the simultaneous presence of multiple DENV genotypes and a high frequency of DENV and CHIKV coinfections. The authors interpret these results as evidence supporting the necessity of integrated surveillance strategies that combine molecular, serological, and genomic approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support the implementation of integrated surveillance strategies combining molecular, serological, and genomic approaches to manage arboviral threats. Such strategies are crucial for understanding complex transmission dynamics and improving clinical diagnosis in regions with multiple co-circulating arboviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific study limitations or a detailed assessment of evidence strength. The evidence is derived solely from an abstract, which restricts the ability to evaluate potential biases, confounding factors, or the generalizability of the findings beyond the study setting.

弥合登革热中的 监测 差距:集成混合数据源的分层模型,用于 传播 估计和 疫苗 定位

Bridging surveillance gaps in dengue: a hierarchical model integrating mixed data sources for transmission estimation and vaccine targeting
作者： Bimandra A Djaafara, Iqbal Elyazar, Prima Yosephine, Asik Surya, Fadjar SM Silalahi, Agus Handito, Burhannudin Thohir, Desfalina Aryani, Dyana Gunawan, Anzala Khoirun Nisa
摘要中文翻译摘要 估计登革热感染力 (FOI) 对于了解 监测 动态和制定干预计划至关重要,但估计所需的流行环境中的 监测 数据通常不完整,且格式各异。我们开发了一个贝叶斯分层催化模型,该模型在单一框架内联合拟合年龄分层病例数据、汇总病例数据和 血清流行率 调查,并结合外部协变量以提高参数可识别性。综合验证表明,即使大多数地区仅提供汇总数据,仅协变量也能恢复准确的 FOI 点估计,但这样做的不确定性校准不佳;有必要通过一次 血清流行率 调查来锚定模型,以使可信的区间覆盖范围接近名义值。该模型应用于印度尼西亚爪哇岛和巴厘岛的 128 个地区(2016-2024 年),揭示了 FOI 和报告率的显着空间异质性。爪哇的许多地区超出了世界卫生组织建议的 疫苗 引入阈值,但在使用报告的 发病率 作为优先标准时被归类为低优先级,特别是在 监测 较弱的地区。基于模型的 血清流行率 估计,集成了多个数据源,为识别 疫苗 引入的高优先级地区提供了更一致的基础,并且比报告的 发病率 更不易受到 监测 偏差的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
估计登革热感染力对于了解 监测 动态和制定干预计划至关重要,但估计所需的流行环境中的 监测 数据通常不完整,且格式各异。
设计与对象
该研究将贝叶斯分层催化模型应用于印度尼西亚爪哇和巴厘岛的 128 个地区,涵盖 2016 年至 2024 年期间。
核心方法
该模型将年龄分层病例数据、汇总病例数据和 血清流行率 调查联合拟合在一个框架内,并结合外部协变量以提高参数可识别性。
主要结果
综合验证表明,即使大多数地区仅提供汇总数据,仅协变量也能恢复准确的感染点估计强度,但这样做的不确定性校准不佳;有必要通过一次 血清流行率 调查来锚定模型,以使可信的区间覆盖范围接近名义值。该模型揭示了感染力和报告率的显着空间异质性。爪哇的许多地区超出了世界卫生组织建议的 疫苗 引入阈值,但在使用报告的 发病率 作为优先标准时被归类为低优先级,特别是在 监测 较弱的地区。
解释与创新
基于模型的 血清流行率 估计,集成了多个数据源,为识别 疫苗 引入的高优先级地区提供了更一致的基础,并且比报告的 发病率 更不易受到 监测 偏差的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,与单独使用报告的 发病率 相比,整合多个数据源为确定登革热 疫苗 引入的高优先级地区提供了更一致的基础。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制或超出有关不确定性校准的综合验证结果的证据强度的详细评估。

Bridging surveillance gaps in dengue: a hierarchical model integrating mixed data sources for transmission estimation and vaccine targeting

Authors: Bimandra A Djaafara, Iqbal Elyazar, Prima Yosephine, Asik Surya, Fadjar SM Silalahi, Agus Handito, Burhannudin Thohir, Desfalina Aryani, Dyana Gunawan, Anzala Khoirun Nisa
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Estimating dengue force of infection (FOI) is essential for understanding transmission dynamics and targeting intervention programmes, yet surveillance data in endemic settings required for estimations are often incomplete, with varying formats. We developed a Bayesian hierarchical catalytic model that jointly fits age-stratified case data, aggregate case data, and seroprevalence surveys within a single framework, incorporating external covariates to improve parameter identifiability. Synthetic validation showed that covariates alone recovered accurate FOI point estimates even when most districts contributed only aggregate data, but did so with poorly calibrated uncertainty; anchoring the model with a single seroprevalence survey was necessary to bring credible interval coverage close to nominal. Applied to 128 districts across Java and Bali, Indonesia (2016–2024), the model revealed substantial spatial heterogeneity in FOI and reporting rates. Many districts in Java exceeded the WHO-suggested seroprevalence threshold for vaccine introduction, yet were classified as low-priority when using reported incidence as prioritisation criterion, particularly in areas with weak surveillance. Model-based seroprevalence estimation, integrating multiple data sources, offers a more consistent basis for identifying high-priority districts for vaccine introduction, and is less susceptible to surveillance bias than reported incidence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Estimating dengue force of infection is essential for understanding transmission dynamics and targeting intervention programmes, yet surveillance data in endemic settings required for estimations are often incomplete, with varying formats.
Design and population
The study applied a Bayesian hierarchical catalytic model to 128 districts across Java and Bali, Indonesia, covering the period from 2016 to 2024.
Core methods
The model jointly fits age-stratified case data, aggregate case data, and seroprevalence surveys within a single framework, incorporating external covariates to improve parameter identifiability.
Main results
Synthetic validation showed that covariates alone recovered accurate force of infection point estimates even when most districts contributed only aggregate data, but did so with poorly calibrated uncertainty; anchoring the model with a single seroprevalence survey was necessary to bring credible interval coverage close to nominal. The model revealed substantial spatial heterogeneity in force of infection and reporting rates. Many districts in Java exceeded the WHO-suggested seroprevalence threshold for vaccine introduction, yet were classified as low-priority when using reported incidence as prioritisation criterion, particularly in areas with weak surveillance.
Interpretation and novelty
Model-based seroprevalence estimation, integrating multiple data sources, offers a more consistent basis for identifying high-priority districts for vaccine introduction, and is less susceptible to surveillance bias than reported incidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating multiple data sources provides a more consistent basis for identifying high-priority districts for dengue vaccine introduction compared to using reported incidence alone.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or a detailed assessment of evidence strength beyond the synthetic validation results regarding uncertainty calibration.

Pasar Rebo地区医院登革热继发感染中血小板计数与血细胞比容值的关系

Hubungan Jumlah Trombosit Dengan Nilai Hematokrit Pada Infeksi Sekunder Dengue Di RSUD Pasar Rebo
作者： Rachman Apriansyah, Suparlan Hadi, Enny Khotimah
摘要中文翻译Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) 是印度尼西亚预防登革热、预防登革热、预防登革热的主要手段。实验室中的血细胞比容和血细胞比容是由实验室指示器、哈西尔笔尖实验室和菜单系统变量提供的。在 RSUD Pasar Rebo 的登革热治疗期间,请注意预防登革热。Penelitian 的数据存储在桌面和横断面的设计方法中。数据显示 诊断 感染登革热并确认血清学 IgG 和 IgM 阳性。Pengumpulan 数据可以通过有目的的采样和双变量的双变量分析来进行有目的的采样。Pada penelitian ini ditemukan bahwa 79,3% pa​​sien dengan infeksi dengue sekunder mengalami trombositopenia tanpa disertai hemokonsentrasi.请注意预防登革热的血细胞比容。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热 (DHF) 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个严重的公共卫生问题,患病率 较高,特别是在继发感染病例中,有可能导致更严重的症状。先前的研究表明,血小板计数与血细胞比容值之间的关系存在差异,而血细胞比容值通常用作实验室指标。
设计与对象
本研究利用描述性定量方法和横截面方法的二手数据。这些数据来自于 RSUD Pasar Rebo 诊断为继发性登革热感染的住院患者的医疗记录。
核心方法
使用有目的的抽样技术进行数据收集。使用具有 Spearman 相关性的单变量和双变量检验进行分析。
主要结果
研究发现,79.3%的继发登革热感染患者在没有血液浓缩的情况下出现血小板减少。结论是继发性登革热感染中血小板计数与血细胞比容值之间没有关系。
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为表明该特定患者群体中血小板计数与血细胞比容水平之间缺乏相关性。这与这些指标的共同使用形成鲜明对比,并强调了所研究环境中继发性登革热感染的特定血液学特征。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明继发性登革热病例中血小板减少症的发生可能与血液浓缩无关,从而为临床监测和预后提供信息。这对于登革热患者管理中实验室参数的解释具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
由于横截面设计和对来自单个中心的二手数据的依赖,证据强度中等。使用有目的抽样可能会限制研究结果对更广泛人群的推广。

Hubungan Jumlah Trombosit Dengan Nilai Hematokrit Pada Infeksi Sekunder Dengue Di RSUD Pasar Rebo

Authors: Rachman Apriansyah, Suparlan Hadi, Enny Khotimah
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat serius di Indonesia dengan prevalensi tinggi, khususnya pada kasus infeksi sekunder yang beresiko menimbulkan manifestasi lebih berat. Perubahan jumlah trombosit dan nilai hematokrit sering digunakan sebagai indikator laboratorium, namun hasil penelitian sebelumnya menunjukkan variasi temuan terkait hubungan keduanya. Tujuan penelitian ini untuk mengetahui hubungan antara jumlah trombosit dengan nilai hematokrit pada pasien infeksi sekunder dengue di RSUD Pasar Rebo. Penelitian ini menggunakan data sekunder dengan menggunakan metode kuantitatif deskriptif dan pendekatan cross sectional. Data diperoleh dari rekam medis pasien rawat inap dengan diagnosis infeksi sekunder dengue yang dikonfirmasi melalui pemeriksaan serologi IgG dan IgM positif. Pengumpulan data dilakukan melalui teknik purposive sampling dan dianalisis menggunakan uji univariat serta bivariat dengan korelasi Spearman. Pada penelitian ini ditemukan bahwa 79,3% pasien dengan infeksi dengue sekunder mengalami trombositopenia tanpa disertai hemokonsentrasi. Kesimpulan yang didapat adalah tidak terdapat hubungan jumlah trombosit dengan nilai hematokrit pada infeksi sekunder dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a serious public health problem in Indonesia with a high prevalence, particularly in secondary infection cases which risk causing more severe manifestations. Previous research has shown variations in findings regarding the relationship between platelet count and hematocrit values, which are often used as laboratory indicators.
Design and population
This study utilized secondary data with a descriptive quantitative method and a cross-sectional approach. The data were obtained from medical records of inpatients diagnosed with secondary dengue infection at RSUD Pasar Rebo.
Core methods
Data collection was performed using purposive sampling techniques. The analysis was conducted using univariate and bivariate tests with the Spearman correlation.
Main results
The study found that 79.3% of patients with secondary dengue infection experienced thrombocytopenia without hemoconcentration. The conclusion was that there was no relationship between platelet count and hematocrit values in secondary dengue infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a lack of correlation between platelet count and hematocrit levels in this specific patient population. This contrasts with the common use of these indicators together and highlights the specific hematological profile of secondary dengue infections in the studied setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform clinical monitoring and prognosis by suggesting that thrombocytopenia may occur independently of hemoconcentration in secondary dengue cases. This has implications for the interpretation of laboratory parameters in the management of dengue patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and reliance on secondary data from a single center. The use of purposive sampling may limit the generalizability of the findings to broader populations.

暴发 中继发登革热感染的频率增加(2016-2024)——临床影响和诊断挑战

Increasing Frequency of Secondary Dengue Infections in Sequential Outbreaks (2016–2024)—Clinical Impact and Diagnostic Challenges
作者： Sonia L. Espindola, Matías Javier Pereson, José Martín Lema, Analía Kachuk, Graciela M. Carballo, Natalia Aloisi, María Noel Badano, Marcos Miretti, Federico A. Di Lello, Patricia Baré
摘要中文翻译连续的 登革病毒 (DENV) 暴发 逐步重塑群体免疫力,影响疾病表达和诊断性能。本研究评估了连续 暴发 继发感染对疾病严重程度标志物、血清型动态以及常规使用的直接诊断方法之间的一致性的影响。这项回顾性研究分析了来自阿根廷米西奥内斯省三个 暴发 的 976 份血清样本。使用 318 个样本的子集(包括 107 个 RT-PCR/NS1 阴性样本)来比较 NS1 抗原快速诊断测试 (NS1 Ag) 和 RT-PCR。评估了病毒血清型以及临床和实验室严重程度标志物。继发感染从 2016 年的 31.1% 增加到 2019 年的 43.2% 和 2024 年的 53.1% (p < 0.001)。血清型分布从2016年的DENV-1为主(95.1%)转变为2019年的DENV-1/DENV-4共循环(60.7%/39.3%)和2024年的DENV-2为主(97.6%)。继发感染与实验室严重程度标志物频率增加相关,特别是在 2024 年,包括较高的血细胞比容、肝酶水平以及较低的白细胞和血小板计数。NS1 Ag 和 RT-PCR 之间的一致性在继发感染中较低(κ = 0.457 vs. 继发感染频率的增加可能会影响与登革热严重程度相关的实验室标志物,并降低直接检测方法之间的诊断一致性,突出表明在登革热 监测 期间需要优化 监测 和诊断策略 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估连续 暴发 的继发性登革热感染如何影响疾病严重程度标志物、血清型动态以及直接方法之间的诊断一致性。
设计与对象
一项回顾性研究分析了 2016 年至 2024 年间来自阿根廷米西奥内斯省三例登革热 暴发 的 976 份血清样本,重点研究继发感染对临床和实验室结果的影响。
核心方法
该研究使用 318 个样本的子集(包括 107 个 RT-PCR/NS1 阴性样本)比较了 NS1 抗原快速诊断测试 (NS1 Ag) 和 RT-PCR,以评估诊断一致性并评估血清型分布和严重程度标记。
主要结果
继发感染从 2016 年的 31.1% 增加到 2019 年的 43.2% 和 2024 年的 53.1% (p < 0.001)。血清型分布从2016年的DENV-1为主(95.1%)转变为2019年的DENV-1/DENV-4共循环(60.7%/39.3%)和2024年的DENV-2为主(97.6%)。
解释与创新
该研究强调,继发感染频率的增加与血清型分布的变化以及实验室严重程度标志物的相关性有关,特别是在 2024 年。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,继发性登革热感染频率的上升可能会影响疾病的严重程度和诊断准确性,强调需要改进 监测 和 监测 期间的诊断策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于血清样本,这可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力,尽管由于观察到明显的趋势,证据强度适中。

Increasing Frequency of Secondary Dengue Infections in Sequential Outbreaks (2016–2024)—Clinical Impact and Diagnostic Challenges

Authors: Sonia L. Espindola, Matías Javier Pereson, José Martín Lema, Analía Kachuk, Graciela M. Carballo, Natalia Aloisi, María Noel Badano, Marcos Miretti, Federico A. Di Lello, Patricia Baré
Original abstract (English)
Successive dengue virus (DENV) outbreaks progressively reshape population immunity, influencing disease expression and diagnostic performance. This study evaluated the impact of secondary infections across sequential outbreaks on disease severity markers, serotype dynamics, and the agreement between routinely used direct diagnostic methods. This retrospective study analyzed 976 serum samples from three outbreaks in Misiones, Argentina. A subset of 318 samples, including 107 RT-PCR/NS1-negative samples, was used to compare NS1 antigen rapid diagnostic tests (NS1 Ag) and RT-PCR. Viral serotypes and clinical and laboratory severity markers were evaluated. Secondary infections increased from 31.1% in 2016 to 43.2% in 2019 and 53.1% in 2024 (p < 0.001). Serotype distribution shifted from DENV-1 predominance in 2016 (95.1%) to DENV-1/DENV-4 co-circulation in 2019 (60.7%/39.3%) and DENV-2 predominance in 2024 (97.6%). Secondary infections were associated with increased frequency of laboratory severity markers, particularly in 2024, including higher hematocrit, liver enzyme levels and lower leukocyte and platelet counts. Concordance between NS1 Ag and RT-PCR was lower in secondary infections (κ = 0.457 vs. The increasing frequency of secondary infections may influence laboratory markers associated with dengue severity and reduce the diagnostic concordance between direct detection methods, highlighting the need for optimized surveillance and diagnostic strategies during dengue outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate how secondary dengue infections across sequential outbreaks affect disease severity markers, serotype dynamics, and diagnostic agreement between direct methods.
Design and population
A retrospective study analyzed 976 serum samples from three dengue outbreaks in Misiones, Argentina, between 2016 and 2024, focusing on the impact of secondary infections on clinical and laboratory outcomes.
Core methods
The study compared NS1 antigen rapid diagnostic tests (NS1 Ag) and RT-PCR using a subset of 318 samples, including 107 RT-PCR/NS1-negative samples, to assess diagnostic concordance and evaluate serotype distribution and severity markers.
Main results
Secondary infections increased from 31.1% in 2016 to 43.2% in 2019 and 53.1% in 2024 (p < 0.001). Serotype distribution shifted from DENV-1 predominance in 2016 (95.1%) to DENV-1/DENV-4 co-circulation in 2019 (60.7%/39.3%) and DENV-2 predominance in 2024 (97.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that the increasing frequency of secondary infections is linked to changes in serotype distribution and an association with laboratory severity markers, particularly in 2024.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the rising frequency of secondary dengue infections may influence disease severity and diagnostic accuracy, emphasizing the need for improved surveillance and diagnostic strategies during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on serum samples, which may introduce biases and limit the ability to establish causality, though the evidence is moderate in strength due to the clear trends observed.

登革热、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅热:百万人口的入侵,预防措施

Dengue, Zika, chikungunya : les moustiques invasifs coûtent des milliards, la prévention reste à la traîne
作者： David Roiz, Frédéric Simard, Jean-Michel Salles
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
入侵性伊蚊对公共卫生构成重大威胁,并造成重大经济损失,但与 暴发 应对成本相比,预防支出仍然不足。这项研究旨在量化包括登革热在内的伊蚊传播疾病的全球经济负担,并强调反应性支出与预防性投资之间的差距。
设计与对象
作者整理了国际 InvaCost 数据库的数据,以评估 1975 年至 2020 年 45 年间与入侵物种相关的经济成本。分析重点是受影响国家与伊蚊及其传播的疾病(包括登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒)相关的全球成本。
核心方法
该团队从科学和机构文献中确定并汇总了记录的成本,将数字转换为 2022 美元。分析包括直接医疗费用、生产力损失、公共 媒介 控制支出和间接经济影响,同时指出,由于少报,估计数可能只是最低值。
主要结果
The study estimated a minimum cumulative cost of 94.7 billion US dollars (approximately 105 billion in 2026 dollars) attributable to Aedes mosquitoes and their associated diseases between 1975 and 2020. Annual costs averaged over 3 billion dollars, with a peak exceeding 20 billion dollars in 2013, and high-end estimates including long-term sequelae suggested a total cost exceeding 300 billion dollars.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对经济影响的结构性低估和集体选择严重不平衡的证据,并指出修复损害的支出大约是预防支出的十倍。这些成本的累积性和重复性使它们有别于卡特里娜飓风等单一自然灾害。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了将资源从反应转向预期的迫切需要,强调对综合 媒介 管理、监测、城市基础设施和创新控制方法的投资。此类预防措施对于减轻伊蚊传播的流行病未来的健康、经济和社会后果至关重要。
局限与证据强度
The evidence strength is moderate because the 94.7 billion dollar figure relies solely on documented and published costs, which likely represents a significant underestimation, particularly in regions with less robust surveillance systems like South-East Asia, Sub-Saharan Africa, and Latin America.

Dengue, Zika, chikungunya : les moustiques invasifs coûtent des milliards, la prévention reste à la traîne

Authors: David Roiz, Frédéric Simard, Jean-Michel Salles
Original abstract (English)
Les moustiques invasifs ne constituent pas seulement une menace sanitaire : ils représentent aussi un coût économique majeur. Une étude internationale estime qu’entre 1975 et 2020, les maladies transmises par les moustiques Aedes ont engendré au moins 94,7 milliards de dollars de pertes dans le monde. Plus frappant encore, les dépenses consacrées à la prévention restent très inférieures aux coûts supportés une fois les épidémies déclarées. Ils sont petits, à peine visibles, et pourtant leur impact économique atteint une ampleur comparable à celle de certaines crises sanitaires majeures. Les moustiques invasifs du genre Aedes – notamment Aedes albopictus (moustique tigre) et Aedes aegypti (son cousin, moins connu en Europe, le moustique de la fièvre jaune)- ne se contentent pas de troubler nos soirées estivales, ils nous transmettent les virus de la dengue, de la fièvre jaune, chikungunya ou encore Zika et, ce faisant, ils engendrent également des coûts considérables pour les systèmes de santé, les collectivités et les ménages du monde entier. Dans une étude publiée en 2024 dans Science of The Total Environment on estime qu’entre 1975 et 2020, les coûts économiques liés à ces moustiques et aux maladies qu’ils transmettent, atteignent au minimum 94,7 milliards (en dollars de 2022). Les estimations hautes suggèrent elles un coût total supérieur à 300 milliards lorsque les séquelles à long terme sont prises en compte, soit un montant comparable au coût total de l’ouragan Katrina, l’une des catastrophes naturelles les plus coûteuses de l’histoire contemporaine, et supérieur à ceux de la catastrophe pétrolière de Deepwater Horizon en 2010 dans le golfe du Mexique. Derrière ce chiffre impressionnant, on trouve une réalité plus préoccupante encore : cette évaluation est sans d...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Invasive Aedes mosquitoes pose a major public health threat and cause significant economic losses, yet prevention spending remains insufficient compared to outbreak response costs. This study aimed to quantify the global economic burden of Aedes-transmitted diseases, including dengue, and to highlight the disparity between reactive expenditures and preventive investments.
Design and population
The authors compiled data from the international InvaCost database to assess economic costs associated with invasive species over a 45-year period from 1975 to 2020. The analysis focused on global costs related to Aedes mosquitoes and the diseases they transmit, including dengue, chikungunya, and Zika, across affected countries.
Core methods
The team identified and aggregated documented costs from scientific and institutional literature, converting figures to 2022 US dollars. The analysis included direct medical costs, lost productivity, public vector control expenditures, and indirect economic impacts, while noting that estimates likely represent a minimum due to underreporting.
Main results
The study estimated a minimum cumulative cost of 94.7 billion US dollars (approximately 105 billion in 2026 dollars) attributable to Aedes mosquitoes and their associated diseases between 1975 and 2020. Annual costs averaged over 3 billion dollars, with a peak exceeding 20 billion dollars in 2013, and high-end estimates including long-term sequelae suggested a total cost exceeding 300 billion dollars.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of a structural underestimation of economic impacts and a major imbalance in collective choices, noting that spending on damage repair is approximately ten times higher than on prevention. The cumulative, recurring nature of these costs distinguishes them from singular natural disasters like Hurricane Katrina.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the critical need to shift resources from reaction to anticipation, emphasizing investments in integrated vector management, surveillance, urban infrastructure, and innovative control methods. Such preventive measures are essential to mitigate the future health, economic, and social consequences of Aedes-borne epidemics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate because the 94.7 billion dollar figure relies solely on documented and published costs, which likely represents a significant underestimation, particularly in regions with less robust surveillance systems like South-East Asia, Sub-Saharan Africa, and Latin America.

评估越南洪水与登革热 发病率 之间的关联:时间序列研究

Assessing the association between floods and dengue incidence in Vietnam: A time series study
作者： Tu Nguyen Tien Tue, Xerxes Seposo, Hong Tam Nguyen, Hung Nguyen Viet, Nhung Hong Ha, Nghia Ngu Duy, Lina Madaniyazi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
近几十年来,登革热 发病率 显着增加,特别是在快速城市化和频繁洪水泛滥的地区。洪水可以通过创造有利的蚊子繁殖地来促进登革热 传播,但这种关联的时间和强度仍不清楚,特别是在越南,那里的洪水多发地区与登革热高负担重叠。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了越南各地和胡志明市 (HCMC) 这个人口稠密、洪水多发的城市中心,洪水暴露与登革热 发病率 之间的关系。使用 63 个省份(2011-2019 年)的月度数据和胡志明市 24 个地区的周度数据进行时间序列分析。
核心方法
使用具有负二项式分布的广义加性混合模型,结合分布式滞后线性函数来估计累积和延迟效应。分析按季节分层(雨季与雨季)
主要结果
At the national level, flooding was associated with a 17% higher dengue incidence (relative risk [RR] = 1.17; 95% confidence interval [CI] = 0.94, 1.47), with the strongest effect observed 2 months after flooding (RR = 1.11; 95% CI = 1.03, 1.20). Associations were more pronounced during the rainy season (RR = 1.65; 95% CI = 1.17, 2.32). In HCMC, floods were linked to a 10.4% increase in dengue cases (RR = 1.10; 95% CI = 1.03, 1.18), with effects peaking at week 9 (RR = 1.03; 95% CI = 1.02, 1.04).
解释与创新
这些发现强调了与洪水相关的登革热风险的延迟性和季节性特征,并支持将洪水指标纳入早期预警系统和气候知情的 媒介 控制策略中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持将洪水指标纳入早期预警系统和气候知情的 媒介 控制策略,以减轻洪水易发地区的登革热风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对时间序列数据和潜在混杂因素的依赖而受到限制,但由于使用了广义加性混合模型和分层季节性分析,证据充足。

Assessing the association between floods and dengue incidence in Vietnam: A time series study

Authors: Tu Nguyen Tien Tue, Xerxes Seposo, Hong Tam Nguyen, Hung Nguyen Viet, Nhung Hong Ha, Nghia Ngu Duy, Lina Madaniyazi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue incidence has increased significantly in recent decades, especially in areas undergoing rapid urbanization and frequent flooding. Floods can promote dengue transmission by creating favorable mosquito breeding sites, yet the timing and strength of this association remain unclear, particularly in Vietnam, where flood-prone areas overlap with a high dengue burden. Methods: This study assessed the relationship between flood exposure and dengue incidence across Vietnam and in Ho Chi Minh City (HCMC), a densely populated, flood-prone urban center. Time-series analyses were conducted using monthly data from 63 provinces (2011–2019) and weekly data from 24 districts in HCMC. Generalized additive mixed models with a negative binomial distribution were used, incorporating distributed lag linear functions to estimate cumulative and delayed effects. Analyses were stratified by season (rainy vs. Results: At the national level, flooding was associated with a 17% higher dengue incidence (relative risk [RR] = 1.17; 95% confidence interval [CI] = 0.94, 1.47), with the strongest effect observed 2 months after flooding (RR = 1.11; 95% CI = 1.03, 1.20). Associations were more pronounced during the rainy season (RR = 1.65; 95% CI = 1.17, 2.32). In HCMC, floods were linked to a 10.4% increase in dengue cases (RR = 1.10; 95% CI = 1.03, 1.18), with effects peaking at week 9 (RR = 1.03; 95% CI = 1.02, 1.04). Seasonal analysis revealed short-term declines in risk during the rainy season and delayed increases during the dry season, peaking at week 9 (RR = 1.03; 95% CI = 1.01, 1.04). Conclusions: These findings highlight the delayed and seasonal nature of flood-related dengue risk and support the integration of flood metrics into early warning systems and climate-informed vector control strategies.

Question and background
Dengue incidence has increased significantly in recent decades, especially in areas undergoing rapid urbanization and frequent flooding. 气候变化和登革热动态:印度尼西亚泗水的路径分析

Climate Variability and Dengue Dynamics: A Path Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia
作者： Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Heny Arwati, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Rahmat Dapari, Fariani Syahrul, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热 是一种气候敏感的 媒介 传播疾病。虽然许多研究已经记录了气候变量与登革热 发病率 之间的关联,但大多数研究都依赖于传统的回归方法,这些方法无法区分直接和间接的气候路径,限制了对环境健康的机制理解和效用。本研究旨在阐明气候变化对泗水登革热 发病率 的相互影响。这项研究整合了季节时间序列分解和路径分析,以区分直接和间接的气候路径、日照时长和风速。方法:采用回顾性生态设计,将2020年至2024年每月登革热发病率数据与气象变量(包括降雨量、温度、湿度、日照时长和风速)相结合。应用季节时间序列分解来表征时间动态,然后在结构方程模型框架内进行路径分析,以量化直接和间接的气候影响。结果和讨论:登革热 发病率 具有明显的季节性模式,在雨季达到高峰。最高温度、相对湿度、日照时长和最小风速对登革热发病率有显着的直接影响(p < 0.05)。降雨量没有显示出直接关联,但却是影响其他气候变量的关键上游驱动因素。该结构模型解释了登革热 发病率 中 52.1% 的变异,凸显了相互关联的气候过程的贡献。结论:这些发现表明,热带大都市环境中的登革热动态受复杂的气候介导途径而非孤立的气候预测因子的控制。这项研究提供了新的环境流行病学证据,以支持气候变化和城市环境变化下的气候信息登革热早期预警系统和自适应 媒介 控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是一种对气候敏感的 媒介 传播疾病,虽然许多研究已经记录了气候变量与登革热 发病率 之间的关联,但大多数研究都依赖于无法区分直接和间接气候途径的传统回归方法。本研究旨在阐明气候变化对泗水登革热 发病率 的相互影响。
设计与对象
使用 2020 年至 2024 年从泗水收集的每月登革热 发病率 数据进行回顾性生态设计。
核心方法
该研究整合了降雨量、温度、湿度、日照时长和风速等气象变量,应用季节时间序列分解来表征时间动态。结构方程模型框架内的路径分析用于量化直接和间接的气候影响。
主要结果
登革热 发病率 具有明显的季节性模式,在雨季达到峰值,最高温度、相对湿度、日照持续时间和最小风速对登革热 发病率 产生显着的直接影响 (p < 0.05)。降雨量没有显示出直接关联,但作为影响其他气候变量的关键上游驱动因素,结构模型解释了登革热 发病率 中 52.1% 的方差。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,热带大都市环境中的登革热动态是由复杂的气候介导途径控制的,而不是孤立的气候预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了新的环境流行病学证据,以支持气候变化和城市环境变化下的气候信息登革热早期预警系统和自适应 媒介 控制策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性或超出所报告的方差解释的证据强度。

Climate Variability and Dengue Dynamics: A Path Analysis in Surabaya, Indonesia

Authors: Khuliyah Candraning Diyanah, Ririh Yudhastuti, Lilis Sulistyorini, Heny Arwati, Hari Basuki Notobroto, Rahmat Dapari, Fariani Syahrul, Muhammad Rasyid Ridha, Babucarr Jassey, Aditya Sukma Pawitra
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Dengue fever is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease. While many studies have documented associations between climate variables and dengue incidence, most rely on conventional regression approaches that fail to separate direct and indirect climatic pathways, limiting mechanistic understanding and utility for environmental health. This study aims to elucidate the interconnected effects of climatic variability on dengue incidence in Surabaya. This study integrates seasonal time-series decomposition and path analysis to distinguish direct and indirect climatic pathways, sunlight duration and wind speed. Methods: A retrospective ecological design, monthly dengue incidence data from 2020 to 2024 were integrated with meteorological variables, including rainfall, temperature, humidity, sunlight duration, and wind speed. Seasonal time-series decomposition was applied to characterize temporal dynamics, followed by path analysis within a structural equation modeling framework to quantify direct and indirect climatic effects. Results and Discussion: A pronounced seasonal pattern in dengue incidence, with peaks during the rainy season. Maximum temperature, relative humidity, sunlight duration, and minimum wind speed exerted significant direct effects on dengue incidence (p < 0.05). Rainfall showed no direct association but acted as a key upstream driver influencing other climatic variables. The structural model explained 52.1% of the variance in dengue incidence, highlighting the contribution of interconnected climatic processes. Conclusion: These findings demonstrate that dengue dynamics in tropical metropolitan environments are governed by complex climate-mediated pathways rather than isolated climatic predictors. This study novel environmental epidemiological evidence to support climate-informed dengue early warning systems and adaptive vector control strategies under climate variability and urban environmental change.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease, and while many studies have documented associations between climate variables and dengue incidence, most rely on conventional regression approaches that fail to separate direct and indirect climatic pathways. This study aims to elucidate the interconnected effects of climatic variability on dengue incidence in Surabaya.
Design and population
A retrospective ecological design was employed using monthly dengue incidence data from Surabaya collected from 2020 to 2024.
Core methods
The study integrated meteorological variables including rainfall, temperature, humidity, sunlight duration, and wind speed, applying seasonal time-series decomposition to characterize temporal dynamics. Path analysis within a structural equation modeling framework was used to quantify direct and indirect climatic effects.
Main results
A pronounced seasonal pattern in dengue incidence was observed with peaks during the rainy season, and maximum temperature, relative humidity, sunlight duration, and minimum wind speed exerted significant direct effects on dengue incidence (p < 0.05). Rainfall showed no direct association but acted as a key upstream driver influencing other climatic variables, with the structural model explaining 52.1% of the variance in dengue incidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that dengue dynamics in tropical metropolitan environments are governed by complex climate-mediated pathways rather than isolated climatic predictors.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides novel environmental epidemiological evidence to support climate-informed dengue early warning systems and adaptive vector control strategies under climate variability and urban environmental change.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence beyond the reported variance explained.

IMPLEMENTASI KEBIJAKAN PENANGGULANGAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD) DI DESA KARANGNANAS KECAMATAN SOKARAJA

Authors: Muhammad Faiz Ananta Zain, Chamid Sutikno, Ariesta Amanda, Indah Ayu Permana Pribadi, Andi Zaelani
Original abstract (English)
Implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) merupakan upaya penting dalam menekan tingginya angka kasus DBD di Indonesia. Desa Karangnanas, Kecamatan Sokaraja, Kabupaten Banyumas tercatat sebagai desa dengan jumlah kasus DBD tertinggi di Kecamatan Sokaraja pada tahun 2024. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas. Penelitian menggunakan metode kualitatif dengan pendekatan deskriptif. Pengumpulan data dilakukan melalui observasi, wawancara, dan dokumentasi. Informan penelitian terdiri atas pemerintah desa, petugas puskesmas, kader kesehatan, dan masyarakat yang dipilih menggunakan teknik purposive sampling. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD di Desa Karangnanas secara umum telah berjalan cukup baik ditinjau dari aspek komunikasi, sumber daya, disposisi, dan struktur birokrasi menurut teori George C. Komunikasi dilakukan melalui penyuluhan kesehatan, kegiatan Pemberantasan Sarang Nyamuk (PSN), serta koordinasi antar pelaksana. Sumber daya didukung oleh tenaga kesehatan, kader, fasilitas, dan anggaran, meskipun masih terdapat keterbatasan sarana dan partisipasi masyarakat. Disposisi pelaksana dilaksanakan melalui komitmen dan tanggung jawab dalam pelaksanaan program, sedangkan struktur birokrasi didukung oleh prosedur operasional standar dan pembagian tugas yang jelas. Faktor pendukung implementasi meliputi koordinasi lintas sektor, dukungan pemerintah desa, keterlibatan kader kesehatan, dan media edukasi masyarakat. Faktor penghambat tersebut meliputi rendahnya partisipasi sebagian masyarakat, luasnya wilayah pengawasan, keterbatasan tenaga pelaksana, serta tingginya beban kerja kader kesehatan. Kata kunci: Implementasi Kebijakan, Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD), Pemberantasan Sarang Nyamuk (PSN), Partisipasi Masyarakat
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) merupakan upaya penting dalam menekan tingginya angka kasus DBD di Indonesia, dan Desa Karangnanas tercatat sebagai desa dengan jumlah kasus DBD tertinggi di Kecamatan Sokaraja pada tahun 2024.
Design and population
Penelitian ini menggunakan metode kualitatif dengan pendekatan deskriptif di Desa Karangnanas, Kecamatan Sokaraja, Kabupaten Banyumas, dengan informan yang terdiri atas pemerintah desa, petugas puskesmas, kader kesehatan, dan masyarakat yang dipilih menggunakan teknik purposive sampling.
Core methods
Pengumpulan data dilakukan melalui observasi, wawancara, dan dokumentasi, dengan analisis berfokus pada aspek komunikasi, sumber daya, disposisi, dan struktur birokrasi menurut teori George C.
Main results
Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa implementasi kebijakan penanggulangan DBD secara umum telah berjalan cukup baik, dengan komunikasi melalui penyuluhan kesehatan dan Pemberantasan Sarang Nyamuk (PSN), serta dukungan sumber daya berupa tenaga kesehatan, kader, fasilitas, dan anggaran meskipun masih terdapat keterbatasan sarana dan partisipasi masyarakat.
Interpretation and novelty
Penelitian ini menginterpretasikan bahwa implementasi kebijakan berjalan cukup baik dengan didukungnya disposisi pelaksana melalui komitmen dan struktur birokrasi yang jelas, namun mengidentifikasi adanya faktor penghambat seperti rendahnya partisipasi sebagian masyarakat dan keterbatasan tenaga.
Scientific and public-health significance
Temuan ini menginformasikan pentingnya koordinasi lintas sektor, dukungan pemerintah desa, dan keterlibatan kader kesehatan serta media edukasi masyarakat untuk meningkatkan efektivitas penanggulangan DBD di tingkat desa.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or a formal assessment of evidence strength, though the study notes constraints such as limited facilities, low community participation, and a wide surveillance area.

媒介 传播的 Enfermedades:恰加斯病毒、登革热、疟疾和汉坦病毒

Enfermedades transmitidas por vectores: chagas, dengue, malaria y hantavirus
作者： Nailin Inga, Luis Miguel Tejada
摘要中文翻译原发性预防的策略:家庭的个人医疗和初始急救的基本护理、家庭护理和医院急救的预防。Alta sospecha clínica: el diagnóstico inicial se groundamenta en vinulous un síndrome febril-gico claro con un nexo epidemiológico claro (viajes recientes,picaduras o exposición a roedores/brotes cerrados)。“红旗”标识:优先侦察警报信号(粘膜疼痛、腹部疼痛、呼吸困难或心脏失代偿)以保证立即住院并有机会。社区行动:媒介 控制教育和流行病学通知不可转让,并与健康中心整合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在强调初级保健在 登革病毒 相关疾病的早期发现和管理中的作用,解决及时干预以预防严重并发症的需要。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 登革病毒 及其相关感染,检查不同地区初级保健机构的临床和流行病学实践。
核心方法
这些方法包括识别与 登革病毒 相关的临床综合征、评估流行病学联系以及评估识别特定警告信号以立即转诊医院的重要性。
主要结果
结果强调了初级保健在初步筛查、管理轻症病例以及通过早期识别 重症登革热 症状来防止医院超负荷方面的关键作用。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对初级保健在 登革病毒 管理中的战略重要性的新颖见解,特别是在识别及时转诊医院的警告信号方面。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强化 登革病毒 监测 和早期干预方案中初级保健整合的需求,为公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据基于一般观察,不包括详细的统计分析或具体的实验数据,导致可信度评估适中。

Enfermedades transmitidas por vectores: chagas, dengue, malaria y hantavirus

Original abstract (English)
Rol estratégico de la Atención Primaria: el personal médico de familia es fundamental para el cribado inicial, el manejo ambulatorio de los casos leves y la prevención del colapso hospitalario. Alta sospecha clínica: el diagnóstico inicial se fundamenta en vincular un síndrome febril inespecífico con un nexo epidemiológico claro (viajes recientes, picaduras o exposición a roedores/brotes cerrados). Identificación de «banderas rojas»: es prioritario reconocer los signos de alarma (como sangrado de mucosas, dolor abdominal intenso, compromiso respiratorio o descompensación cardíaca) para garantizar una derivación hospitalaria inmediata y oportuna. Acción comunitaria: la educación para el control del vector y la notificación epidemiológica son pilares indelegables en el abordaje integral desde el centro de salud.

Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the role of primary care in the early detection and management of dengue virus-related diseases, addressing the need for timely intervention to prevent severe complications.
Design and population
The study focused on dengue virus and its associated infections, examining the clinical and epidemiological practices in primary care settings across various regions.
Core methods
The methods included identifying clinical syndromes linked to dengue virus, assessing epidemiological connections, and evaluating the importance of recognizing specific warning signs for prompt hospital referral.
Main results
The results emphasized the critical role of primary care in initial screening, managing mild cases, and preventing hospital overload through early recognition of severe dengue symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as novel insights into the strategic importance of primary care in dengue virus management, particularly in identifying warning signs for timely hospital referral.

寨卡病毒非结构蛋白 1 的成熟和分泌需要脯氨酰羟基化

Prolyl hydroxylation is required for maturation and secretion of Zika virus non-structural protein 1
作者： Bodine Bezemer, Gijs J. Overheul, Milou Stevens, C. G. Spruijt, M. Vermeulen, Pascal Miesen, Ronald P. van Rij
摘要中文翻译蚊媒正黄病毒,如寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 具有很高的流行潜力,并与多种可能的结果相关,从无症状感染到严重并发症。正黄病毒非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 可以是膜结合的,也可以从细胞中分泌,在病毒复制、免疫逃避和 发病机制 中发挥重要作用。为了描述 ZIKV NS1 的相互作用组,我们用表达 FLAG 标签 NS1 的重组 ZIKV 感染 Huh7 和神经祖细胞,并通过定量质谱法鉴定相互作用蛋白。在这两种细胞类型中,多种脯氨酰羟化酶、糖基化酶、ER 相关降解蛋白和参与免疫的蛋白质被鉴定为 NS1 相互作用蛋白。质谱数据显示NS1含有潜在的羟脯氨酸残基,表明脯氨酰羟化酶参与了NS1的翻译后修饰。脯氨酰羟化酶的化学抑制会损害 NS1 的细胞内运输和质膜定位,并降低 ZIKV RNA 水平和滴度。281位脯氨酸残基的诱变损害了NS1的分泌,但病毒复制不受影响。此外,在第 267 位取代脯氨酸会严重失调 NS1 表达,并在单次传代后恢复为野生型序列,表明该 突变 会导致严重的复制缺陷。我们的结果确定脯氨酰羟基化是 ZIKV NS1 的重要翻译后修饰。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
所提供的证据并未报告 登革病毒 的具体研究问题或背景,因为该研究的重点是寨卡病毒。
设计与对象
所提供的证据并未报告与 登革病毒 相关的研究设计或群体,因为实验涉及感染重组寨卡病毒的 Huh7 和神经祖细胞。
核心方法
所提供的证据并未报告适用于 登革病毒 的方法,因为所描述的技术包括用 FLAG 标记的寨卡病毒感染、定量质谱分析、脯氨酰羟化酶的化学抑制以及寨卡病毒 NS1 的定点诱变。
主要结果
所提供的证据并未报告 登革病毒 的结果,因为研究结果涉及脯氨酰羟化酶作为寨卡病毒 NS1 相互作用物的鉴定、寨卡病毒 NS1 中羟脯氨酸残基的存在,以及脯氨酰羟化酶抑制和 NS1 诱变对寨卡病毒复制和分泌的影响。
解释与创新
所提供的证据并未报告 登革病毒 的解释或新颖性,因为作者得出结论,脯氨酰羟基化是专门针对寨卡病毒 NS1 的重要翻译后修饰。
科研与公卫意义
所提供的证据并未报告 登革病毒 的科学或公共卫生意义,因为有关病毒复制和潜在治疗靶点的影响仅限于寨卡病毒。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告 登革病毒 的局限性或证据强度,因为该研究是对寨卡病毒的仅摘要调查,没有 登革病毒 的数据。

Prolyl hydroxylation is required for maturation and secretion of Zika virus non-structural protein 1

Authors: Bodine Bezemer, Gijs J. Overheul, Milou Stevens, C. G. Spruijt, M. Vermeulen, Pascal Miesen, Ronald P. van Rij
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne orthoflaviviruses such as Zika virus (ZIKV) and dengue virus (DENV) have a high epidemic potential and are associated with a wide range of possible outcomes, from asymptomatic infections to severe complications. The orthoflavivirus non-structural protein 1 (NS1), which can be membrane-bound as well as secreted from the cell, plays important roles in viral replication, immune evasion and pathogenesis. To describe the interactome of ZIKV NS1, we infected Huh7 and neural progenitor cells with a recombinant ZIKV expressing FLAG-tagged NS1 and identified interacting proteins by quantitative mass spectrometry. In both cell types, multiple prolyl hydroxylases, glycosylation enzymes, ER-associated degradation proteins, and proteins involved in immunity were identified as NS1 interactors. Mass spectrometry data showed that NS1 contains potential hydroxyproline residues, suggesting that prolyl hydroxylase enzymes are involved in post-translational modification of NS1. Chemical inhibition of prolyl hydroxylases impaired NS1 intracellular trafficking and plasma membrane localization and reduced ZIKV RNA levels and titers. Mutagenesis of the proline residue at position 281 impaired NS1 secretion, although viral replication was unaffected. Moreover, substituting proline at position 267 strongly dysregulated NS1 expression and reverted to the wild-type sequence already after a single passage, suggesting that this mutation leads to severe replication defects. Our results identify prolyl hydroxylation as an essential post-translational modification of ZIKV NS1.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The supplied evidence does not report a specific research question or background for dengue virus, as the study focuses on Zika virus.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not report a study design or population relevant to dengue virus, as the experiments involved Huh7 and neural progenitor cells infected with recombinant Zika virus.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not report methods applicable to dengue virus, as the techniques described include infection with FLAG-tagged Zika virus, quantitative mass spectrometry, chemical inhibition of prolyl hydroxylases, and site-directed mutagenesis of Zika virus NS1.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report results for dengue virus, as the findings concern the identification of prolyl hydroxylases as interactors of Zika virus NS1, the presence of hydroxyproline residues in Zika virus NS1, and the effects of prolyl hydroxylase inhibition and NS1 mutagenesis on Zika virus replication and secretion.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not report an interpretation or novelty for dengue virus, as the authors conclude that prolyl hydroxylation is an essential post-translational modification specifically for Zika virus NS1.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not report scientific or public health significance for dengue virus, as the implications regarding viral replication and potential therapeutic targets are restricted to Zika virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for dengue virus, as the study is an abstract-only investigation of Zika virus without data on dengue virus.

农村登革热动态:哥斯达黎加气候、建筑环境和农业的相互作用

Rural dengue dynamics: the interplay of climate, built environment, and agriculture in Costa Rica
作者： Glidden, C. K., Southworth, E. K., Shragai, T., Rojas-Araya, D., Troyo, A., Chaves-Gonzalez, L. E., Marin, R., Vargas Roldan, I., Mordecai, E. A.
摘要中文翻译登革热是世界上负担最重的虫媒病毒疾病之一。尽管通常被认为是一种城市疾病,但许多地区的农村地区都面临着巨大的登革热负担。长期气候、短期天气变化、当地建筑环境和土地利用梯度对农村地区登革热动态的综合影响仍知之甚少,限制了我们预测全球变化下风险转移的能力。在这里,我们调查了哥斯达黎加的这些动态,以阐明这些相互作用的社会环境因素如何影响农村登革热 传播。我们首先使用 22 年的州级 (admin-2) 病例数据来确定农村地区的登革热病例和 发病率 始终高于城市地区。然后,利用十年来的区级 (admin-3) 每月病例数据和贝叶斯分层建模框架,我们确定了与登革热风险最密切相关的气候和土地利用特征。温度是登革热城乡分布的广泛空间模式的基础,而沿海之间的降水影响不同,反映了沿海气候区的对比,而不是城市化和水资源可用性之间的相互作用。在适宜的气候条件下,即使是适度的基础设施建设也会显着增加风险,但这种关系在建筑量较高的情况下会趋于稳定。在低海拔和中海拔农作物覆盖率较高的地区,登革热风险也较高,但在较高、较冷的海拔地区则不然。这些结果共同表明,哥斯达黎加农村登革热的高风险是由于气候适宜性与已建基础设施的基线水平一致所致,而农业可能成为农村登革热 传播 的独特驱动因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热仍然是一种高负担的虫媒病毒性疾病,尽管农村负担沉重,但其在农村地区的动态却尚未得到充分研究。该研究解决了长期气候、短期天气变化、建筑环境和土地利用梯度如何相互作用影响农村地区登革热 传播 的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用了哥斯达黎加 22 年的州级 (admin-2) 登革热病例数据和 10 年的区级 (admin-3) 每月病例数据。它采用贝叶斯分层建模框架来分析农村和城市地区登革热风险的社会环境驱动因素。
核心方法
作者将贝叶斯分层模型应用于十年来地区一级的每月登革热病例数据,整合了气候和土地利用变量。温度、降水、基础设施建设和农作物覆盖率被评估为登革热风险的预测因素。
主要结果
22 年来,农村地区的登革热病例和 发病率 始终高于城市地区。温度主要解释了城乡登革热分布的广泛空间模式,而降水效应因海岸而异。建成的基础设施将风险增加到更高的水平,而农作物覆盖率在低海拔和中海拔地区增加了风险,但在高海拔地区则没有。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,哥斯达黎加农村登革热风险源于气候适宜性与基础设施建设的基线相结合,而农业是特定海拔地区的额外驱动因素。这凸显了在登革热风险模型中考虑农村特定社会环境相互作用的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究确定了与农村登革热风险相关的关键气候和土地利用特征,为哥斯达黎加农村地区的针对性 监测 和干预策略提供了信息。它强调了将气候和土地利用数据纳入登革热控制计划的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于生态关联和建模,限制了因果推断。地区一级的数据粒度和对报告病例的依赖可能会带来偏差,而且调查结果可能无法推广到哥斯达黎加特定的社会环境背景之外。

Rural dengue dynamics: the interplay of climate, built environment, and agriculture in Costa Rica

Authors: Glidden, C. K., Southworth, E. K., Shragai, T., Rojas-Araya, D., Troyo, A., Chaves-Gonzalez, L. E., Marin, R., Vargas Roldan, I., Mordecai, E. A.
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is one of the worlds highest-burden arboviral diseases. Although classically considered an urban disease, many regions experience a substantial dengue burden in rural areas. The combined influence of long-term climate, short-term weather variation, local built environments, and land-use gradients on dengue dynamics in rural settings remains poorly understood, limiting our ability to predict shifting risk under global change. Here, we investigate these dynamics in Costa Rica to disentangle how these interacting socio-environmental factors shape rural dengue transmission. We first use 22 years of canton-level (admin-2) case data to establish that both dengue cases and incidence are consistently higher in rural than in urban districts. Then, using ten years of district-level (admin-3) monthly case data and a Bayesian hierarchical modeling framework, we identify the climatic and land-use features most strongly associated with dengue risk. Temperature underlies broad spatial patterns in dengues urban-rural distribution, while precipitation effects differ between coasts, reflecting intercoastal climate zone contrasts rather than interactions between urbanization and water availability. Given suitable climate, even modest levels of built infrastructure substantially increase risk, but the relationship plateaus at higher levels of building volume. Dengue risk is also elevated in areas with high agricultural crop cover at low and mid elevations but not at higher, cooler elevations. Together these results suggest that high risk of rural dengue in Costa Rica result from climate suitability aligning with baseline levels of built infrastructure, with agriculture potentially emerging as a distinct driver of rural dengue transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a high-burden arboviral disease, yet its dynamics in rural settings are understudied despite substantial rural burden. The study addresses a knowledge gap on how long-term climate, short-term weather variation, built environments, and land-use gradients interact to shape dengue transmission in rural areas.
Design and population
The study uses 22 years of canton-level (admin-2) dengue case data and ten years of district-level (admin-3) monthly case data from Costa Rica. It employs a Bayesian hierarchical modeling framework to analyze socio-environmental drivers of dengue risk across rural and urban districts.
Core methods
The authors applied Bayesian hierarchical modeling to monthly dengue case data at the district level over ten years, integrating climate and land-use variables. Temperature, precipitation, built infrastructure, and agricultural crop cover were evaluated as predictors of dengue risk.
Main results
Rural districts consistently showed higher dengue cases and incidence than urban districts over 22 years. Temperature primarily explained broad spatial patterns in urban-rural dengue distribution, while precipitation effects varied between coasts. Built infrastructure increased risk up to a plateau at higher volumes, and agricultural crop cover elevated risk at low and mid elevations but not at higher elevations.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that rural dengue risk in Costa Rica arises from climate suitability combined with baseline built infrastructure, with agriculture acting as an additional driver in specific elevations. This highlights the need to consider rural-specific socio-environmental interactions in dengue risk models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies key climatic and land-use features associated with rural dengue risk, informing targeted surveillance and intervention strategies in rural Costa Rica. It underscores the importance of integrating climate and land-use data into dengue control programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on ecological associations and modeling, limiting causal inference. Data granularity at the district level and reliance on reported cases may introduce biases, and findings may not generalize beyond Costa Rica's specific socio-environmental context.

巴西旅游目的地的虫媒病毒风险。

Arboviral Risk at Brazilian Tourist Destinations.
作者： Sebastian Vernal, Eloise Skinner, Beatris Mário Martin, Sheng-Hsun Lee, Expedito J A Luna, Ester C Sabino, Benn Sartorius, Luis Furuya-Kanamori
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
巴西是一个热门的旅游目的地,该国经常出现登革热、基孔肯雅热和黄热病 (YF),给居民和国际旅行者带来重大健康风险。该研究旨在确定虫媒病毒感染的高风险区域,并开发一种用户友好的、基于地图的资源,以支持旅行医学的临床决策。
设计与对象
我们分析了 2019 年至 2024 年(YF 从 2015 年开始)向巴西国家 监测 系统报告的登革热、基孔肯雅热和 YF 实验室确诊病例和死亡病例。数据汇总到 133 个中间地理区域 (IGR),排除了研究期间没有确诊病例的 IGR。
核心方法
根据每月 发病率 和 死亡率(分数 1-4)的国家四分位数,结合 (i) 传播 持续时间(每年报告病例的月数,分数 0-12)和 (ii) 疾病负担,计算每个 IGR 的综合风险分数。将年度得分相加,生成每种虫媒病毒的风险地图,并将高风险 IGR(定义为综合得分的前四分之一)与热门旅游目的地重叠,以创建直观的视觉指南。
主要结果
We analysed 4.35 million dengue, 294,921 chikungunya, and 2,312 YF cases, resulting in 7,846, 565, and 794 deaths, respectively. Dengue affected all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Midwest, including the Pantanal. Chikungunya was also identified in all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Northeast, overlapping with popular beaches such as Recife. YF was identified in 38.3% of IGRs, notably in the Southeast, including São Paulo state.
解释与创新
虫媒病毒在巴西各地构成多种风险。我们的空间集成地图为旅行医学提供了一种新颖的工具,使医疗保健从业者能够向旅行者提供量身定制的建议和旅行前 疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为前往巴西高风险地区的游客提供有针对性的 监测 和旅行前 疫苗接种 策略,特别是中西部的登革热和东北部的基孔肯雅热。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于 监测 数据,该数据可能漏报病例,风险评分方法虽然具有创新性,但尚未经过外部验证。由于国家数据集全面,证据强度中等,但受到潜在报告偏差的限制。

Arboviral Risk at Brazilian Tourist Destinations.

Authors: Sebastian Vernal, Eloise Skinner, Beatris Mário Martin, Sheng-Hsun Lee, Expedito J A Luna, Ester C Sabino, Benn Sartorius, Luis Furuya-Kanamori
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Brazil is a popular travel destination that experiences recurrent outbreaks of dengue, chikungunya, and yellow fever (YF), posing a significant health risk to both residents and international travellers. We aimed to identify high-risk regions for arboviral infections and develop a user-friendly, map-based resource to support clinical decision-making in travel medicine. METHODS: We analysed laboratory-confirmed cases and deaths from dengue, chikungunya, and YF reported to Brazil's national surveillance system from 2019 to 2024 (from 2015 for YF). Data were aggregated into 133 Intermediate Geographic Regions (IGRs). IGRs without a confirmed case during the studied period were excluded. A composite risk score was calculated for each IGR by combining (i) transmission duration (months with reported cases per year, score 0-12) and (ii) disease burden, based on national quartiles of monthly incidence and mortality (score 1-4). Annual scores were summed to produce risk maps for each arbovirus. High-risk IGRs (defined as the top quartile of composite score) were overlaid with popular tourist destinations to create an intuitive visual guide. RESULTS: We analysed 4.35 million dengue, 294,921 chikungunya, and 2,312 YF cases, resulting in 7,846, 565, and 794 deaths, respectively. Dengue affected all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Midwest, including the Pantanal. Chikungunya was also identified in all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Northeast, overlapping with popular beaches such as Recife. YF was identified in 38.3% of IGRs, notably in the Southeast, including São Paulo state. CONCLUSIONS: Arboviruses pose a diverse risk across Brazil. Our spatially integrated map provides a novel tool for travel medicine, enabling healthcare practitioners to offer tailored advice and pre-travel vaccinations to travellers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Brazil is a popular travel destination that experiences recurrent outbreaks of dengue, chikungunya, and yellow fever (YF), posing a significant health risk to both residents and international travellers. The study aimed to identify high-risk regions for arboviral infections and develop a user-friendly, map-based resource to support clinical decision-making in travel medicine.
Design and population
We analysed laboratory-confirmed cases and deaths from dengue, chikungunya, and YF reported to Brazil's national surveillance system from 2019 to 2024 (from 2015 for YF). Data were aggregated into 133 Intermediate Geographic Regions (IGRs), with IGRs without a confirmed case during the studied period excluded.
Core methods
A composite risk score was calculated for each IGR by combining (i) transmission duration (months with reported cases per year, score 0-12) and (ii) disease burden, based on national quartiles of monthly incidence and mortality (score 1-4). Annual scores were summed to produce risk maps for each arbovirus, and high-risk IGRs (defined as the top quartile of composite score) were overlaid with popular tourist destinations to create an intuitive visual guide.
Main results
We analysed 4.35 million dengue, 294,921 chikungunya, and 2,312 YF cases, resulting in 7,846, 565, and 794 deaths, respectively. Dengue affected all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Midwest, including the Pantanal. Chikungunya was also identified in all IGRs, with high-risk IGRs in the Northeast, overlapping with popular beaches such as Recife. YF was identified in 38.3% of IGRs, notably in the Southeast, including São Paulo state.
Interpretation and novelty
Arboviruses pose a diverse risk across Brazil. Our spatially integrated map provides a novel tool for travel medicine, enabling healthcare practitioners to offer tailored advice and pre-travel vaccinations to travellers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and pre-travel vaccination strategies for tourists visiting high-risk regions in Brazil, particularly for dengue in the Midwest and chikungunya in the Northeast.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on surveillance data, which may underreport cases, and the risk score methodology, while innovative, has not been externally validated. The evidence strength is moderate due to the comprehensive national dataset but limited by potential reporting biases.

一种预测对 宿主 人群进行干预以降低 媒介 传播感染的 暴发 程度的效果的新方法

A New Method to Predict the Effect of an Intervention in the Host Population to Reduce the Magnitude of an Outbreak of a Vector-Borne Infection
作者： Francisco Antônio Bezerra Coutinho, Marcos Amaku, Kallas EG, Eduardo Massad, Esper G. Kallás, Coutinho, F. A. B., Kallas, E. G.
摘要中文翻译在本文中,我们提出了一种新模型来估计 媒介 传播的感染(例如每年发生不同程度的登革热)对人类 媒介 的影响。该模型适用于这些 暴发,并且实际上与它们的强度无关,也就是说,它不需要稳态假设。该模型将官方报告的与年龄相关的 媒介 传播感染病例数作为输入。它是确定性的,不考虑随机性。我们的目标是估计干预措施的影响(功效),并且我们依赖于观察到的事实,即通过同一 媒介 传播的感染病例比例的年龄分布与 媒介 的强度和研究的地理区域无关,至少对于巴西地区而言。这一发现在正文中得到了强调,并构成了我们计算的基础。使用登革热 疫苗 模拟假设干预,从而可以确定 疫苗接种 活动的最佳策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种新模型,以评估对人类 媒介 传播的感染(如登革热)进行干预的影响,登革热每年都会发生不同程度的感染。
设计与对象
该模型适用于 媒介 传播的感染的 暴发,并且与其强度无关,不需要稳态假设。
核心方法
确定性模型使用官方报告的年龄相关病例数作为输入,并模拟假设的干预措施(例如登革热 疫苗),以确定最佳 疫苗接种 策略。
主要结果
该模型不考虑随机性,而是依赖于观察到的病例年龄分布与巴西地区 传播 强度和地理区域的独立性。
解释与创新
作者将该模型解释为一种估计干预效果的新工具,利用不同 传播 强度和区域的病例的一致年龄分布。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对登革热等 媒介 传播感染的有针对性的 疫苗接种 活动的设计提供信息,从而有可能优化 暴发 控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该模型的确定性性质限制了其解释随机变异性的能力,并且由于依赖观察数据且缺乏随机建模,证据强度中等。

A New Method to Predict the Effect of an Intervention in the Host Population to Reduce the Magnitude of an Outbreak of a Vector-Borne Infection

Authors: Francisco Antônio Bezerra Coutinho, Marcos Amaku, Kallas EG, Eduardo Massad, Esper G. Kallás, Coutinho, F. A. B., Kallas, E. G.
Original abstract (English)
In this paper, we propose a new model to estimate the impact of an intervention on human hosts of a vector-borne infection, such as dengue, which occurs in yearly outbreaks of different magnitudes. The model applies to these outbreaks and, in fact, is independent of their intensity, that is, it does not require the steady-state assumption. The model takes as input the officially reported age-dependent number of cases of a vector-borne infection. It is deterministic and does not account for stochasticity. Our objective is to estimate the impact of the intervention (the efficacy), and we rely on the observed fact that the age distribution of the proportion of cases of the infections transmitted by the same vector is independent of both the intensity of transmission and the geographic area studied, at least for Brazilian regions. This finding is highlighted in the main text and forms the basis of our calculations. A hypothetical intervention is simulated using a dengue vaccine, which allows the determination of the optimal strategy for a vaccination campaign.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a new model to estimate the impact of an intervention on human hosts of vector-borne infections like dengue, which occur in yearly outbreaks of varying magnitudes.
Design and population
The model applies to outbreaks of vector-borne infections and is independent of their intensity, not requiring steady-state assumptions.
Core methods
The deterministic model uses officially reported age-dependent case numbers as input and simulates hypothetical interventions, such as a dengue vaccine, to determine optimal vaccination strategies.
Main results
The model does not account for stochasticity and relies on the observed independence of age distribution of cases from transmission intensity and geographic area in Brazilian regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the model as a novel tool to estimate intervention efficacy, leveraging the consistent age distribution of cases across different transmission intensities and regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the design of targeted vaccination campaigns for vector-borne infections like dengue, potentially optimizing outbreak control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The deterministic nature of the model limits its ability to account for random variability, and the evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on observational data and lack of stochastic modeling.
