C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

使用经杀虫剂处理的屋檐和窗户屏蔽作为 媒介 控制工具进行房屋改造:来自坦桑尼亚半田系统的证据和模拟流行病学影响。

House modifications using insecticide-treated screening of eave and window as a vector control tool: evidence from a semi-field system in Tanzania and simulated epidemiological impact.
作者： Olukayode G Odufuwa, Richard J Sheppard, Safina Ngonyani, Ahmadi Bakari Mpelepele, Dickson Kobe, Agathus Njohole, Jason Moore, Jastin Lusoli Lusoli, Joseph B Muganga, Rune Bosselmann
摘要中文翻译背景:未改进的房屋结构中的缝隙,尤其是屋檐和窗户中的缝隙,允许蚊子进入,增加室内 媒介 传播的疾病 传播。对此类房屋进行简单改造可以减少人类接触,杀虫剂 治疗 可以杀死蚊子,使所有社区成员受益。本研究评估了经杀虫剂处理的屋檐和窗户纱网 (ITS),并与坦桑尼亚经氯菊酯和 PBO 处理的蚊帐进行了比较,并与溴氰菊酯和胡椒基丁醚 (PBO) 进行了比较。方法:在大型网笼内的四个实验小屋​​中采用随机拉丁方设计(4 × 4),以便在小屋内外重新捕获蚊子。评估了四个 治疗:(1)新的(存储 12 个月的)ITS;(2) 12个月自然老化的ITS;(3) 12 个月现场使用的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 蚊帐(坦桑尼亚的护理标准),以及 (4) 无 治疗。该研究进行了 32 个晚上,每间小屋每晚使用 30 只蚊子。使用四种实验室饲养的菌株:疟疾 媒介(阿拉伯按蚊和按蚊)、登革热 媒介(埃及伊蚊)和滋扰库蚊(Culex quinquefasciatus)。重新捕获的蚊子在 72 小时、吸血和进入小屋时进行 死亡率 评估。使用修改后的机制模型进行模拟,跟踪恶性疟原虫疟疾,以说明这些产品的潜在流行病学影响。结果:与经过 12 个月老化的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 处理的蚊帐相比,针对所有蚊子种类,新 ITS 诱导更高的 死亡率 [优势比:2.25(95% 置信区间:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001],老化 ITS 相似 [OR:0.80(95% CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]。新旧 ITS 均显着 (p < 0.0001) 减少蚊子吸血 [新 OR:0.02(95% CI:0.01-0.03);旧OR:0.09(95%CI:0.05-0.14)]和小屋入口[新IRR:0.10(95%CI:0.08-0.13);老化IRR:0.25(95%CI:0.21-0.31)]。传播 模型估计表明,ITS 的流行病学影响可能会取代拟除虫菊酯 - PBO 处理的蚊帐在人口水平上的影响。模型结果表明影响效力取决于假设的干预覆盖率、持久性和蚊子生物学特性。结论:本研究引入了标准化半场生物测定法来评估 ITS,生成昆虫学数据,首次能够对其对疟疾 传播 的潜在影响进行建模。ITS 是公共卫生的有效工具,因为它可以杀死大部分疟疾和登革热 媒介,并减少令人讨厌的叮咬。鉴于其简单性,应将其视为筛选未改良房屋的附加或独立工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
未改进的房屋结构中的间隙,特别是屋檐和窗户中的间隙,允许蚊子进入,增加室内 媒介 传播的疾病 传播。对此类房屋进行简单改造可以减少人类接触,杀虫剂 治疗 可以杀死蚊子,使所有社区成员受益。
设计与对象
在大型网笼内的四个实验小屋​​中使用了随机拉丁方设计(4×4),以便在小屋内外重新捕获蚊子。该研究进行了 32 个晚上,每间小屋每晚使用 30 只蚊子。使用四种实验室饲养的菌株:疟疾 媒介(阿拉伯按蚊和按蚊)、登革热 媒介(埃及伊蚊)和滋扰库蚊(Culex quinquefasciatus)。
核心方法
本研究评估了经杀虫剂处理的屋檐和窗户纱网 (ITS),并与坦桑尼亚经氯菊酯和 PBO 处理的蚊帐进行了比较,并与溴氰菊酯和胡椒基丁醚 (PBO) 进行了比较。评估了四个 治疗:(1)新的(存储 12 个月的)ITS;(2) 12个月自然老化的ITS;(3) 12 个月现场使用的拟除虫菊酯-PBO 蚊帐(坦桑尼亚的护理标准),以及 (4) 无 治疗。重新捕获的蚊子在 72 小时、吸血和进入小屋时进行 死亡率 评估。
主要结果
对于所有蚊子种类,与 12 个月老化的拟除虫菊酯 PBO 处理的蚊帐相比,新 ITS 诱导更高的 死亡率 [比值比:2.25(95% 置信区间:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001],老化 ITS 相似 [OR:0.80(95% CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]。新旧 ITS 均显着 (p < 0.0001) 减少蚊子吸血 [新 OR:0.02(95% CI:0.01-0.03);旧OR:0.09(95%CI:0.05-0.14)]和小屋入口[新IRR:0.10(95%CI:0.08-0.13);老化IRR:0.25(95%CI:0.21-0.31)]。
解释与创新
这项研究引入了一种标准化的半场生物测定法来评估 ITS,生成昆虫学数据,首次能够对其对疟疾 传播 的潜在影响进行建模。ITS 是公共卫生的有效工具,因为它可以杀死大部分疟疾和登革热 媒介,并减少令人讨厌的叮咬。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于其简单性,ITS 应被视为筛查未改良房屋的附加或独立工具。传播 模型估计表明,ITS 的流行病学影响可能会取代拟除虫菊酯 - PBO 处理的蚊帐在人口水平上的影响。
局限与证据强度
模型结果表明影响效力取决于假设的干预覆盖率、持久性和蚊子生物学特性。由于半现场系统设计和对模拟流行病学影响的依赖,证据强度中等。

House modifications using insecticide-treated screening of eave and window as a vector control tool: evidence from a semi-field system in Tanzania and simulated epidemiological impact.

Authors: Olukayode G Odufuwa, Richard J Sheppard, Safina Ngonyani, Ahmadi Bakari Mpelepele, Dickson Kobe, Agathus Njohole, Jason Moore, Jastin Lusoli Lusoli, Joseph B Muganga, Rune Bosselmann
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Gaps in unimproved house structures, especially in eaves and windows, allow mosquito entry, increasing indoor vector-borne disease transmission. Simple modifications to such houses may reduce human exposure, and insecticide treatment may kill mosquitoes, benefiting all community members. This study evaluated insecticide-treated screening (ITS) for eaves and windows, incorporated with deltamethrin and piperonyl butoxide (PBO), compared to a permethrin and PBO-treated bednet in Tanzania. METHOD: A randomised Latin-square design (4 × 4) was used in four experimental huts within a large netting cage to allow mosquito recapture inside and outside of huts. Four treatments were evaluated: (1) new (12-month stored) ITS; (2) 12-month naturally-aged ITS; (3) 12-month field-used pyrethroid-PBO bednet (standard of care in Tanzania), and (4) no treatment. The study was performed for 32 nights using 30 mosquitoes per strain, per hut per night. Four laboratory-reared strains were used: malaria vectors (Anopheles arabiensis and An. funestus), dengue vector (Aedes aegypti), and nuisance biting (Culex quinquefasciatus). Recaptured mosquitoes were assessed for mortality at 72 h, blood-feeding, and hut entry. A simulation with a modified mechanistic model tracking Plasmodium falciparum malaria was used to illustrate potential epidemiological impact from these products. RESULTS: Against all mosquito species compared to 12-month aged pyrethroid-PBO-treated bednet, new ITS induced higher mortality [Odds Ratio:2.25(95%Confidence Interval:1.65-3.06),p < 0.0001], and aged-ITS was similar [OR:0.80(95%CI:0.59-1.08),p = 0.141]. Both new and aged ITS significantly (p < 0.0001) reduced mosquito blood-feeding [new OR:0.02(95% CI:0.01-0.03); aged OR:0.09(95%CI:0.05-0.14)] and hut entry [new IRR:0.10(95%CI:0.08-0.13); aged IRR:0.25(95%CI:0.21-0.31)]. Transmission model estimates indicate epidemiological impacts of ITS may supersede pyrethroid-PBO-treated bednets at the population level. The model results indicate impact potency depends on assumed intervention percentage cover, durability, and mosquito bionomics. CONCLUSIONS: This study introduces a standardised semi-field bioassay to evaluate ITS, generating entomological data that, for the first time, enabled modelling of its potential impact on malaria transmission. ITS is an efficacious tool for public health as it kills substantial proportions of malaria and dengue vectors, and reduces nuisance biting. Given its simplicity, it should be considered an additional or stand-alone tool for screening unimproved houses.

Question and background
Gaps in unimproved house structures, especially in eaves and windows, allow mosquito entry, increasing indoor vector-borne disease transmission. Simple modifications to such houses may reduce human exposure, and insecticide treatment may kill mosquitoes, benefiting all community members.
Design and population
A randomised Latin-square design (4 × 4) was used in four experimental huts within a large netting cage to allow mosquito recapture inside and outside of huts. The study was performed for 32 nights using 30 mosquitoes per strain, per hut per night. Four laboratory-reared strains were used: malaria vectors (Anopheles arabiensis and An. funestus), dengue vector (Aedes aegypti), and nuisance biting (Culex quinquefasciatus).
Core methods
This study evaluated insecticide-treated screening (ITS) for eaves and windows, incorporated with deltamethrin and piperonyl butoxide (PBO), compared to a permethrin and PBO-treated bednet in Tanzania. Four treatments were evaluated: (1) new (12-month stored) ITS; (2) 12-month naturally-aged ITS; (3) 12-month field-used pyrethroid-PBO bednet (standard of care in Tanzania), and (4) no treatment. Recaptured mosquitoes were assessed for mortality at 72 h, blood-feeding, and hut entry.

Interpretation and novelty
This study introduces a standardised semi-field bioassay to evaluate ITS, generating entomological data that, for the first time, enabled modelling of its potential impact on malaria transmission. ITS is an efficacious tool for public health as it kills substantial proportions of malaria and dengue vectors, and reduces nuisance biting.
Scientific and public-health significance
Given its simplicity, ITS should be considered an additional or stand-alone tool for screening unimproved houses. Transmission model estimates indicate epidemiological impacts of ITS may supersede pyrethroid-PBO-treated bednets at the population level.
Limitations and evidence strength
The model results indicate impact potency depends on assumed intervention percentage cover, durability, and mosquito bionomics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the semi-field system design and reliance on simulated epidemiological impact.

印度南部大肠杆菌来源的银纳米颗粒用于控制伊蚊 媒介 的评估。

Evaluation of Escherichia coli-derived Silver Nanoparticles for Aedes Vector Control in Southern India.
作者： Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Mortein Henri, M. Priyadharshini, Farhat Syed Kaseem, Sathya Jeevitha, Balachandar Vellingiri, Anand Setty Balakrishnan, Neena Elezebeth Philip, Mansi Malik, Sulochana Shekhar
摘要中文翻译背景 埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊是泰米尔纳德邦传播登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等主要虫媒病毒疾病的主要蚊子 媒介。埃及伊蚊是主要的 媒介,其次是白纹伊蚊。由于可预测的化学杀幼虫剂的局限性和环境问题,迫切需要替代和可持续的 媒介 控制策略。方法在这项研究中,利用大肠杆菌通过生物方法合成了环保的银纳米颗粒,该方法开发了细菌的自然还原和稳定特性。使用紫外-可见光谱和傅里叶变换红外(FTIR)光谱对合成的银纳米颗粒的尺寸、形状和稳定性进行了表征。针对印度泰米尔纳德邦蒂鲁瓦鲁尔地区的埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊幼虫,评估了银纳米粒子的杀幼虫功效。LC 50 和LC 90 值根据SPSS中的Finney法(Miller-Tainter法)使用概率分析来估计。结果生物合成的纳米银颗粒对两种蚊子均表现出较强的杀幼虫活性,根据剂量方法,在白纹伊蚊中观察到的幼虫死亡率较埃及伊蚊为中等。埃及伊蚊的半致死浓度 (LC 50 ) 估计为 270 ± 16 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的半致死浓度为 194 ± 13 mg/L,埃及伊蚊的半致死浓度 (LC 50 ) 估计为 3480 ± 490 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的 LC 90 值估计为 2510 ± 360 mg/L。研究结果表明,生物合成的银纳米颗粒是化学杀幼虫剂的有效替代品,可提高功效并减少对环境的影响。结论 当前的研究强调了利用生物资源合成纳米颗粒和实施可持续方法控制蚊媒疾病的双重优势。未来的研究应侧重于大规模生产、现场功效评估以及对非目标生物体的潜在影响的评估,以支持实际应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊是泰米尔纳德邦传播登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等主要虫媒病毒疾病的主要蚊子 媒介。由于可预测的化学杀幼虫剂的局限性和环境问题,迫切需要替代和可持续的 媒介 控制策略。
设计与对象
该研究评估了银纳米粒子对印度泰米尔纳德邦蒂鲁瓦鲁尔地区埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊幼虫的杀幼虫功效。该研究利用大肠杆菌通过生物方法合成的环保银纳米颗粒。
核心方法
使用紫外-可见光谱和傅里叶变换红外(FTIR)光谱对合成的银纳米颗粒的尺寸、形状和稳定性进行了表征。LC 50 和LC 90 值根据SPSS中的Finney法(Miller-Tainter法)使用概率分析来估计。
主要结果
生物合成的银纳米粒子对两种蚊子均表现出很强的杀幼虫活性,在白纹伊蚊中观察到的幼虫 死亡率 程度高于埃及伊蚊。埃及伊蚊的半数致死浓度 (LC 50) 估计为 270 ± 16 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的半数致死浓度为 194 ± 13 mg/L。埃及伊蚊的 LC 90 值估计为 3480 ± 490 mg/L,白纹伊蚊的 LC 90 值估计为 2510 ± 360 mg/L。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,生物合成的银纳米颗粒是化学杀幼虫剂的有效替代品,可提高功效并减少对环境的影响。目前的研究强调了利用生物资源合成纳米颗粒和实施可持续方法控制蚊媒疾病的双重优势。
科研与公卫意义
未来的研究应侧重于大规模生产、现场功效评估以及对非目标生物体的潜在影响的评估,以支持实际应用。该研究提出了与受登革热等虫媒病毒性疾病影响的地区相关的可持续 媒介 控制策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、偏差或混杂因素。证据强度被评估为中等,因为数据仅来自摘要,缺乏详细的方法描述和完整的结果。

Evaluation of Escherichia coli-derived Silver Nanoparticles for Aedes Vector Control in Southern India.

Authors: Rajalakshmi Anbalagan, Mortein Henri, M. Priyadharshini, Farhat Syed Kaseem, Sathya Jeevitha, Balachandar Vellingiri, Anand Setty Balakrishnan, Neena Elezebeth Philip, Mansi Malik, Sulochana Shekhar
Original abstract (English)
Background Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus are the principal mosquito vectors in Tamil Nadu transmitting the major arboviral diseases such as Dengue, Chikungunya, Zika and Yellow Fever. Aedes aegypti serves as the primary vector, followed by Aedes albopictus. Due to the limitations and environmental concerns associated with predictable chemical larvicides, there is an emergency need for alternative and sustainable vector control strategies. Methods In this study, environmentally friendly silver nanoparticles were synthesized using Escherichia coli through a biogenic approach that develops the bacterium's naturally reducing and stabilizing properties. The synthesized silver nanoparticles were characterized for size, shape, and stability using UV-Visible spectroscopy and Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy. The larvicidal efficacy of silver nanoparticles was evaluated against the larvae of Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus from the Thiruvarur district of Tamil Nadu, India. LC 50 and LC 90 values were estimated using probit analysis according to Finney's method (Miller-Tainter approach) in SPSS. Results The biosynthesized silver nanoparticles showed strong larvicidal activity against both mosquito species, depending on the dose approach, the moderate larval mortality was observed in Aedes albopictus than in Aedes aegypti. The median lethal concentration (LC 50 ) was estimated at 270 ± 16 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 194 ± 13 mg/L for Aedes albopictus, and LC 90 values were estimated at 3480 ± 490 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 2510 ± 360 mg/L for Aedes albopictus. The findings indicate that the biogenically synthesized silver nanoparticles are an effective alternative to chemical larvicides, offering enhanced efficacy with reduced environment impact. Conclusion The current study highlights the dual advantages of utilizing biological resources for nanoparticle synthesis and implementing sustainable approaches for the control of mosquito- borne diseases. Future research should focus on large-scale production, field-level efficacy assessments, and evaluation of potential effects on non-target organisms to support practical applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus are the principal mosquito vectors in Tamil Nadu transmitting major arboviral diseases such as Dengue, Chikungunya, Zika and Yellow Fever. Due to the limitations and environmental concerns associated with predictable chemical larvicides, there is an emergency need for alternative and sustainable vector control strategies.
Design and population
The study evaluated the larvicidal efficacy of silver nanoparticles against the larvae of Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus from the Thiruvarur district of Tamil Nadu, India. The study utilized environmentally friendly silver nanoparticles synthesized using Escherichia coli through a biogenic approach.
Core methods
The synthesized silver nanoparticles were characterized for size, shape, and stability using UV-Visible spectroscopy and Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy. LC 50 and LC 90 values were estimated using probit analysis according to Finney's method (Miller-Tainter approach) in SPSS.
Main results
The biosynthesized silver nanoparticles showed strong larvicidal activity against both mosquito species, with moderate larval mortality observed in Aedes albopictus than in Aedes aegypti. The median lethal concentration (LC 50) was estimated at 270 ± 16 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 194 ± 13 mg/L for Aedes albopictus. LC 90 values were estimated at 3480 ± 490 mg/L for Aedes aegypti and 2510 ± 360 mg/L for Aedes albopictus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the biogenically synthesized silver nanoparticles are an effective alternative to chemical larvicides, offering enhanced efficacy with reduced environment impact. The current study highlights the dual advantages of utilizing biological resources for nanoparticle synthesis and implementing sustainable approaches for the control of mosquito-borne diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
Future research should focus on large-scale production, field-level efficacy assessments, and evaluation of potential effects on non-target organisms to support practical applications. The study suggests a sustainable vector control strategy relevant to regions affected by arboviral diseases like Dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, or confounding factors. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate because the data is derived from an abstract only, lacking detailed methodological descriptions and full results.

二级保健普通医学科管理的复杂多系统医疗演示:来自印度南部的 CARE 合规病例系列

Complex Multisystem Medical Presentations Managed in a Secondary Care General Medicine Unit: A CARE-Compliant Case Series from Southern India
作者： Santhosh Kumar Tumkur Narayanappa
摘要中文翻译结构化摘要背景全科医学部门经常遇到患有重叠感染、代谢、心血管、肾脏、呼吸系统和自身免疫性疾病的患者。此类患者往往需要多学科管理和个体化的治疗策略。目的描述印度南部一家二级保健医院收治的一系列复杂医疗病例的临床特征、调查、管理方法和结果。方法利用2026年5月至2026年6月全科医学部收治的9名患者的医院记录进行回顾性观察病例系列。提取有关人口统计学、症状、合并症、诊断调查、治疗干预、多学科参与和临床结果的数据并进行描述性分析。结果病例系列包括严重支气管肺炎伴呼吸衰竭(n=2)、甲氨蝶呤诱导的全血细胞减少伴糖尿病酮症酸中毒(n=1)、病毒性发热伴炎症性多关节炎(n=1)、登革热伴血小板减少和肝功能障碍(n=1)、感染性心肌炎 (n=1)、急性肾损伤伴尿潴留 (n=1)、膀胱癌继发的梗阻性尿路病 (n=1)、尿脓毒症 (n=1) 和脓毒症坏死性筋膜炎 (n=1)。糖尿病是最常见的合并症。五名患者需要重症监护支持。多学科护理为 8 名患者带来了良好的结果,而 1 名患者需要针对恶性肿瘤相关疾病进行持续的专科随访。结论本病例系列强调了普通医学实践中遇到的广泛临床谱,并强调了早期 诊断、多学科协作和个体化管理在改善复杂医疗患者的结果方面的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
全科医学科经常遇到患有重叠感染、代谢、心血管、肾脏、呼吸和自身免疫性疾病的患者,需要多学科治疗。
设计与对象
使用 2026 年 5 月至 2026 年 6 月期间在印度南部一家二级保健医院收治的 9 名患者的医院记录进行了回顾性观察病例系列。
核心方法
提取有关人口统计、症状、合并症、诊断调查、治疗干预、多学科参与和临床结果的数据并进行描述性分析。
主要结果
该系列包括一例伴有血小板减少和肝功能障碍的 登革热 病例,以及严重支气管肺炎和甲氨蝶呤引起的全血细胞减少等其他病症;糖尿病是最常见的合并症,五名患者需要重症监护支持。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了普通医学实践中遇到的广泛临床范围,并强调了早期 诊断、多学科合作和个体化管理的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,多学科护理可以为二级护理机构中的复杂医疗患者带来有利的结果,尽管对登革热预防或控制的具体影响尚未详细说明。
局限与证据强度
由于来自单个中心的小病例系列(n = 9)的回顾性观察设计,证据强度较低,这限制了普遍性和建立因果关系的能力。

Complex Multisystem Medical Presentations Managed in a Secondary Care General Medicine Unit: A CARE-Compliant Case Series from Southern India

Authors: Santhosh Kumar Tumkur Narayanappa
Original abstract (English)
Structured AbstractBackgroundGeneral medicine departments frequently encounter patients presenting with overlapping infectious, metabolic, cardiovascular, renal, respiratory, and autoimmune disorders. Such patients often require multidisciplinary management and individualized treatment strategies.ObjectiveTo describe the clinical characteristics, investigations, management approaches, and outcomes of a series of complex medical cases admitted to a secondary care hospital in Southern India.MethodsA retrospective observational case series was conducted using hospital records of nine patients admitted between May 2026 and June 2026 under the Department of General Medicine. Data regarding demographics, presenting symptoms, comorbidities, diagnostic investigations, therapeutic interventions, multidisciplinary involvement, and clinical outcomes were extracted and analyzed descriptively.ResultsThe case series included severe bronchopneumonia with respiratory failure (n=2), methotrexate-induced pancytopenia with diabetic ketoacidosis (n=1), viral fever with inflammatory polyarthritis (n=1), dengue fever with thrombocytopenia and hepatic dysfunction (n=1), infective myocarditis (n=1), acute kidney injury with urinary retention (n=1), obstructive uropathy secondary to bladder carcinoma (n=1), urosepsis (n=1), and necrotizing fasciitis with sepsis (n=1). Diabetes mellitus was the most frequent comorbidity. Intensive care support was required in five patients. Multidisciplinary care resulted in favorable outcomes in eight patients, while one patient required continued specialist follow-up for malignancy-related disease.ConclusionThis case series highlights the broad clinical spectrum encountered in general medicine practice and emphasizes the importance of early diagnosis, multidisciplinary collaboration, and individualized management in improving outcomes in complex medical patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
General medicine departments frequently encounter patients with overlapping infectious, metabolic, cardiovascular, renal, respiratory, and autoimmune disorders who require multidisciplinary management.
Design and population
A retrospective observational case series was conducted using hospital records of nine patients admitted between May 2026 and June 2026 to a secondary care hospital in Southern India.
Core methods
Data regarding demographics, presenting symptoms, comorbidities, diagnostic investigations, therapeutic interventions, multidisciplinary involvement, and clinical outcomes were extracted and analyzed descriptively.
Main results
The series included one case of dengue fever with thrombocytopenia and hepatic dysfunction, alongside other conditions such as severe bronchopneumonia and methotrexate-induced pancytopenia; diabetes mellitus was the most frequent comorbidity and intensive care support was required in five patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as highlighting the broad clinical spectrum encountered in general medicine practice and emphasize the importance of early diagnosis, multidisciplinary collaboration, and individualized management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that multidisciplinary care can result in favorable outcomes for complex medical patients in secondary care settings, though specific implications for dengue prevention or control are not detailed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low due to the retrospective, observational design of a small case series (n=9) from a single center, which limits generalizability and the ability to establish causal relationships.

用于贝叶斯系统发育测年的分层时钟混合模型

A hierarchical clock-mixture model for Bayesian phylogenetic dating
作者： Yuan Xu, Jordan Douglas, Remco Bouckaert, Alexei J. Drummond, Drummond, A. J.
摘要中文翻译在单一分子钟模型条件下推断贝叶斯系统发育时间树,将钟的选择视为固定的,即使在合理的钟家族之间的支持是不确定的。当该假设错误时,估计的时间尺度及其不确定性可能会被扭曲。现有的做法通常通过分别拟合严格、不相关对数正态松弛(UCLN)和自相关钟并比较它们的边际似然来解决这个问题,但这需要多次计算昂贵的模型选择分析。在这里,我们介绍了一个分层钟混合框架,作为 BEAST~2 的开源 \texttt{RelaxClockAveraging} 包实现,它分离了在钟比较中经常混淆的两个问题:是否根本需要分支特异性速率变化,以及如果需要,该变化是否更好地描述为不相关或自相关。该方法在顶层在严格钟和松弛钟之间进行解析平均,然后在松弛类别内共享的分支速率 媒介 上比较 UCLN 和自相关模型,返回所有三个钟家族的后验概率以及它们之间共享参数的模型平均汇总。在分层模拟中,生成的钟家族在所有重复中都被保留在 95\% 后验模型集中,而整体替换率、根年龄和树长的模型平均估计保持准确。在 DENV-4 基准测试中,该混合模型重现了钟家族的更高努力嵌套采样排名,同时避免了独立边际似然估计的极端运行间变异性。在经验基准测试中,该方法在经典的 31 个分类单元叶绿体 \textit{rbcL} 数据集上恢复了对自相关钟的强有力支持。在一个 RSV-A G 基因数据集上,它几乎将所有后验支持集中在 UCLN 上,同时保持了既定的 RSV-A 时间尺度。在一个包含 245 个分类单元的结构化合并 H3N2 数据集上,它将大部分后验质量分配给松弛钟类别,该类别内的支持集中在自相关家族上,推断的时间尺度与已发表的估计保持一致。这些结果表明,钟模型支持和下游时间尺度敏感性是相关但不相同的。单钟定年分析可能仍然足够,但固定钟模型应由后验支持证明,而不是被视为默认假设。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在单个分子时钟模型上调节贝叶斯系统发育时间树推断的问题,即使在合理的时钟家族之间的支持不确定的情况下,该模型也将时钟选择视为固定的,这可能会扭曲估计的时间尺度及其不确定性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了一种分层时钟混合框架,作为 BEAST 2 的开源 RelaxClockAveraging 包实现,该框架分离了是否需要特定于分支的速率变化的问题,如果需要,是否最好将其描述为不相关或自相关。
核心方法
该方法在顶层对严格时钟和宽松时钟之间进行分析平均,并在共享分支速率 媒介 上比较宽松类中的不相关对数正态宽松 (UCLN) 和自相关模型,返回所有三个时钟系列的后验概率以及共享参数的模型平均摘要。
主要结果
在分层模拟中,生成时钟家族在所有重复中保留在 95% 后验模型集中,并且总体替代率、根龄和树长的模型平均估计仍然准确;在 DENV-4 基准测试中,该混合物再现了时钟系列的更高努力的嵌套采样排名,同时避免了极端的运行差异。
解释与创新
作者解释说,时钟模型支持和下游时间尺度敏感性相关但不相同,单时钟约会分析仍然足够,但修复时钟模型应该通过后验支持来证明合理,而不是被视为默认假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过提供一种计算有效的方法来解释时钟模型的不确定性,为系统发育测年实践提供信息,这可以提高病毒 进化 研究(包括 登革病毒 的研究)中时间尺度估计的准确性。
局限与证据强度
证据强度很高,因为该方法通过分层模拟和多个经验基准(包括 DENV-4 数据集)进行了验证,证明了稳健的性能以及与既定时间尺度的一致性。

A hierarchical clock-mixture model for Bayesian phylogenetic dating

Authors: Yuan Xu, Jordan Douglas, Remco Bouckaert, Alexei J. Drummond, Drummond, A. J.
Original abstract (English)
Conditioning the inference of a Bayesian phylogenetic time tree on a single molecular clock model treats clock choice as fixed, even when support among plausible clock families is uncertain. When that assumption is wrong, estimated timescales and their uncertainty can be distorted. Existing practice usually addresses this by fitting strict, uncorrelated lognormal relaxed (UCLN), and autocorrelated clocks separately and comparing their marginal likelihoods, but this requires multiple computationally expensive model selection analyses.Here we introduce a hierarchical clock-mixture framework, implemented as the open-source \texttt{RelaxClockAveraging} package for BEAST~2, that separates two questions that are often conflated in clock comparison: whether branch-specific rate variation is needed at all, and, if it is, whether that variation is better described as uncorrelated or autocorrelated. The method averages analytically between strict and relaxed clocks at the top level and then compares UCLN and autocorrelated models within the relaxed class on a shared branch-rate vector, returning posterior probabilities for all three clock families together with model-averaged summaries for parameters shared across them. In stratified simulations, the generating clock family was retained in the 95\% posterior model set in all replicates, while model-averaged estimates of the overall substitution rate, root age, and tree length remained accurate. On a DENV-4 benchmark, the mixture reproduced the higher-effort nested-sampling ranking of clock families while avoiding the extreme run-to-run variability of independent marginal-likelihood estimates. On empirical benchmarks, the method recovered strong support for the autocorrelated clock on the classical 31-taxon chloroplast \textit{rbcL} data set. On an RSV-A G-gene data set it concentrated virtually all posterior support on UCLN while preserving the established RSV-A timescale. On a 245-taxon structured-coalescent H3N2 dataset it assigned most posterior mass to the relaxed-clock class, with support within that class concentrated on the autocorrelated family, and the inferred timescale remained consistent with the published estimate. These results show that clock-model support and downstream timescale sensitivity are related but not identical. Single-clock dating analyses can still be adequate, but fixing a clock model should be justified by posterior support rather than treated as a default assumption.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the problem of conditioning Bayesian phylogenetic time tree inference on a single molecular clock model, which treats clock choice as fixed even when support among plausible clock families is uncertain, potentially distorting estimated timescales and their uncertainty.
Design and population
The study introduces a hierarchical clock-mixture framework implemented as the open-source RelaxClockAveraging package for BEAST 2, which separates the questions of whether branch-specific rate variation is needed and, if so, whether it is better described as uncorrelated or autocorrelated.
Core methods
The method analytically averages between strict and relaxed clocks at the top level and compares uncorrelated lognormal relaxed (UCLN) and autocorrelated models within the relaxed class on a shared branch-rate vector, returning posterior probabilities for all three clock families and model-averaged summaries for shared parameters.
Main results
In stratified simulations, the generating clock family was retained in the 95% posterior model set in all replicates, and model-averaged estimates of the overall substitution rate, root age, and tree length remained accurate; on a DENV-4 benchmark, the mixture reproduced the higher-effort nested-sampling ranking of clock families while avoiding extreme run-to-run variability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that clock-model support and downstream timescale sensitivity are related but not identical, and that single-clock dating analyses can still be adequate, but fixing a clock model should be justified by posterior support rather than treated as a default assumption.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform phylogenetic dating practices by providing a computationally efficient method to account for clock model uncertainty, which can improve the accuracy of timescale estimates in viral evolution studies, including those for dengue virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high, as the method was validated through stratified simulations and multiple empirical benchmarks, including a DENV-4 dataset, demonstrating robust performance and consistency with established timescales.

感染三种不同病原体的埃及伊蚊的 MicroRNA 调节:aae-miR-375 在靶向 bre-4 调节蚊子免疫中的作用。

MicroRNA Regulation of Aedes aegypti Infected with Three Different Pathogens: The Role of aae-miR-375 in Targeting bre-4 to Modulate Mosquito Immunity.
作者： Yanan Yin, Yanhui Liu, Lingling Yu, Meng Nie, Yiyang Wu, Chenghong Liao
摘要中文翻译埃及伊蚊是人类病原体的重要媒介,包括登革热和基孔肯雅病毒。我们检查了 Ae 中肠 miRNA 表达的变化。感染大肠杆菌、金黄色葡萄球菌和白僵菌后的埃及伊蚊。我们在感染大肠杆菌的四龄幼虫中肠中鉴定出 19 个差异表达的 miRNA,在感染金黄色葡萄球菌的幼虫中鉴定出 42 个差异表达的 miRNA,在成年蚊子中鉴定出 134 个差异表达的 miRNA。在感染 B 的蚊子中观察到差异表达 miRNA 的数量最多 (160)。其中,aae-miR-375 显示出最明显的变化,在 S 后下调了近 96%。使用双荧光素酶测定验证了 aae-miR-375 和 Ae-bre-4 之间的相互作用。这些结果表明不同的病原体在伊蚊中诱导不同的中肠 miRNA 表达谱。此外,我们确定 Ae-bre-4 作为 aae-miR-375 的靶标,为蚊子 免疫应答 背后的病原体-miRNA-mRNA 相互作用提供了新的见解。总的来说,这些发现提高了我们对蚊子免疫力的理解,并可能为未来的 媒介 控制策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究 microRNA 在调节蚊子免疫力以应对 登革病毒 感染中的作用,因为埃及伊蚊是登革热和其他人类病原体的关键 媒介。
设计与对象
该研究检测了埃及伊蚊感染大肠杆菌、金黄色葡萄球菌和白僵菌后中肠 miRNA 表达的变化,重点是 登革病毒 相关的 免疫应答。
核心方法
研究人员使用双荧光素酶测定来验证 aae-miR-375 和 Ae-bre-4 之间的相互作用,并分析了四龄幼虫和成年蚊子中的 miRNA 表达谱。
主要结果
该研究在感染大肠杆菌的幼虫中鉴定出 19 种差异表达的 miRNA,在感染金黄色葡萄球菌的幼虫中鉴定出 42 种差异表达的 miRNA,在成年蚊子中鉴定出 134 种差异表达的 miRNA。在感染白僵菌的蚊子中观察到差异表达 miRNA 的数量最多 (160)。其中,aae-miR-375表现出最明显的变化,在金黄色葡萄球菌感染后下调了近96%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,不同的病原体在埃及伊蚊中诱导不同的中肠 miRNA 表达谱,并且 aae-miR-375 与 Ae-bre-4 相互作用,为蚊子 免疫应答 对病原体的分子机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能通过强调 miRNA 在蚊子免疫中的作用及其作为 登革病毒 和其他病原体干预目标的潜力,为未来的 媒介 控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象水平的证据,除了双荧光素酶测定之外,没有提供详细的统计分析或实验验证,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

MicroRNA Regulation of Aedes aegypti Infected with Three Different Pathogens: The Role of aae-miR-375 in Targeting bre-4 to Modulate Mosquito Immunity.

Authors: Yanan Yin, Yanhui Liu, Lingling Yu, Meng Nie, Yiyang Wu, Chenghong Liao
Original abstract (English)
Aedes aegypti is an important vector of human pathogens, including dengue and chikungunya viruses. We examined midgut miRNA expression changes in Ae. aegypti following infection with Escherichia coli, Staphylococcus aureus, and Beauveria bassiana. We identified 19 differentially expressed miRNAs in the midgut of fourth-instar larvae infected with E. coli, 42 in larvae infected with S. aureus, and 134 in adult mosquitoes. The highest number of differentially expressed miRNAs (160) was observed in mosquitoes infected with B. Among these, aae-miR-375 showed the most pronounced change, with nearly 96% downregulation after S. The interaction between aae-miR-375 and Ae-bre-4 was validated using dual-luciferase assays. These results indicate that different pathogens induce distinct midgut miRNA expression profiles in Ae. Furthermore, we identified Ae-bre-4 as a target of aae-miR-375, providing new insights into the pathogen-miRNA-mRNA interactions that underlie mosquito immune responses. Collectively, these findings improve our understanding of mosquito immunity and may inform future vector control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the role of microRNA in modulating mosquito immunity in response to dengue virus infection, as Aedes aegypti is a key vector for dengue and other human pathogens.
Design and population
The study examined midgut miRNA expression changes in Aedes aegypti following infection with Escherichia coli, Staphylococcus aureus, and Beauveria bassiana, with a focus on dengue virus-related immune responses.
Core methods
The researchers used dual-luciferase assays to validate the interaction between aae-miR-375 and Ae-bre-4, and analyzed miRNA expression profiles in fourth-instar larvae and adult mosquitoes.
Main results
The study identified 19 differentially expressed miRNAs in larvae infected with Escherichia coli, 42 in larvae infected with Staphylococcus aureus, and 134 in adult mosquitoes. The highest number of differentially expressed miRNAs (160) was observed in mosquitoes infected with Beauveria bassiana. Among these, aae-miR-375 showed the most pronounced change, with nearly 96% downregulation after Staphylococcus aureus infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that different pathogens induce distinct midgut miRNA expression profiles in Aedes aegypti, and that aae-miR-375 interacts with Ae-bre-4, providing new insights into the molecular mechanisms of mosquito immune responses to pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future vector control strategies by highlighting the role of miRNA in mosquito immunity and its potential as a target for interventions against dengue virus and other pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and does not provide detailed statistical analyses or experimental validation beyond dual-luciferase assays, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

宿主 U2AF1和U2AF2剪接因子的利用促进了蚊媒正黄病毒跨物种感染

Exploitation of host U2AF1 and U2AF2 splicing factors facilitates mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus infection across species
作者： Changbai Huang, Yiquan Cai, Cancan Chen, Xuanfeng Zhu, Ying’an Liang, Yingrui Luo, Qinyu Peng, Yi Wang, Chao Yang, Ming Li
摘要中文翻译摘要:为了鉴定正黄病毒复制所必需的新型 宿主 因子,我们对从感染 登革病毒 或寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的细胞中分离的内质网组分进行了蛋白质组学分析。在富集的蛋白质中,剪接因子 U2AF2 及其异二聚体伴侣 U2AF1 对有效的病毒感染至关重要,在病毒蛋白质合成阶段发挥作用。U2AF1 的精氨酸/丝氨酸丰富 (RS) 结构域和第一个锌指节 (Zn1),以及除 U2AF2 的 RS 结构域之外的几乎所有结构域,对于其原病毒功能至关重要。U2AF1-U2AF2 相互作用的破坏可有效抑制 ZIKV 感染。U2AF1和U2AF2部分定位于细胞质,值得注意的是,细胞质U2AF2主要是截短的形式,足以支持病毒复制。U2AF1 和 U2AF2 均与病毒 RNA 结合,其中 U2AF1 结合依赖于 U2AF2。有趣的是,将埃及伊蚊同源物u2af38反式互补到U2AF1敲除细胞中可以恢复ZIKV复制,而在U2AF2敲除细胞中表达u2af50则不能。然而,u2af38 或 u2af50 的敲低显着抑制了蚊子中黄病毒的复制。总之,这些发现揭示黄病毒在人类细胞和蚊子中共同选择 宿主 剪接因子,强调了病毒利用的保守的跨物种机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定对正黄病毒复制至关重要的新型 宿主 因子,以解决理解病毒-宿主 相互作用的空白。
设计与对象
该研究利用从感染 登革病毒 或寨卡病毒的细胞中分离的内质网组分进行蛋白质组学分析。
核心方法
研究人员通过域作图、相互作用破坏、定位研究、RNA 结合测定以及人类和蚊子细胞中的反式互补实验,鉴定了丰富的蛋白质并评估了 U2AF1 和 U2AF2 的前病毒功能。
主要结果
U2AF1和U2AF2被发现对于有效的病毒感染至关重要,它们的功能需要特定的结构域,并破坏它们的相互作用以抑制寨卡病毒感染;蚊子同源物 u2af38 和 u2af50 的敲除抑制了黄病毒在蚊子中的复制。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为黄病毒在人类细胞和蚊子中利用 宿主 剪接因子的证据,揭示了病毒利用的保守的跨物种机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,宿主 剪接因子代表了针对蚊媒黄病毒的广谱 抗病毒药物 策略的潜在靶点。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的局限性或超出抽象水平发现的证据强度的详细评估。

Exploitation of host U2AF1 and U2AF2 splicing factors facilitates mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus infection across species

Authors: Changbai Huang, Yiquan Cai, Cancan Chen, Xuanfeng Zhu, Ying’an Liang, Yingrui Luo, Qinyu Peng, Yi Wang, Chao Yang, Ming Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract To identify novel host factors essential for orthoflavivirus replication, we performed proteomic profiling of endoplasmic reticulum fractions isolated from cells infected with Dengue virus or Zika virus (ZIKV). Among the enriched proteins, the splicing factor U2AF2 and its heterodimeric partner U2AF1 were found to be critical for efficient viral infection, functioning at the viral protein synthesis stage. The arginine/serine-rich (RS) domain and the first zinc knuckle (Zn1) of U2AF1, as well as nearly all domains except the RS domain of U2AF2, were essential for their proviral function. Disruption of the U2AF1–U2AF2 interaction potently suppressed ZIKV infection. U2AF1 and U2AF2 partially localize to the cytoplasm, and notably, the cytoplasmic U2AF2 was predominantly a truncated form that was sufficient to support viral replication. Both U2AF1 and U2AF2 bind to viral RNA, with U2AF1 binding being dependent on U2AF2. Interestingly, trans-complementation of the Aedes aegypti homolog u2af38 into U2AF1-knockout cells restored ZIKV replication, whereas expressing u2af50 in U2AF2-knockdown cells did not. However, knockdown of either u2af38 or u2af50 significantly inhibited the flavivirus replication in mosquitoes. Together, these findings reveal that flaviviruses co-opt host splicing factors in both human cells and mosquitoes, underscoring a conserved cross-species mechanism of viral exploitation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify novel host factors essential for orthoflavivirus replication to address gaps in understanding viral-host interactions.
Design and population
The study utilized proteomic profiling of endoplasmic reticulum fractions isolated from cells infected with Dengue virus or Zika virus.
Core methods
Researchers identified enriched proteins and assessed the proviral function of U2AF1 and U2AF2 through domain mapping, interaction disruption, localization studies, RNA binding assays, and trans-complementation experiments in human and mosquito cells.
Main results
U2AF1 and U2AF2 were found to be critical for efficient viral infection, with specific domains required for their function and disruption of their interaction suppressing Zika virus infection; knockdown of mosquito homologs u2af38 and u2af50 inhibited flavivirus replication in mosquitoes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that flaviviruses co-opt host splicing factors in both human cells and mosquitoes, revealing a conserved cross-species mechanism of viral exploitation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that host splicing factors represent potential targets for broad-spectrum antiviral strategies against mosquito-borne flaviviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or a detailed assessment of evidence strength beyond the abstract-level findings.

比较产卵和成虫诱捕以监测基于沃尔巴克氏体的 媒介 控制的 wMel 渗入。

Comparison of Oviposition and Adult Trapping to Monitor wMel Introgression for Wolbachia-Based Vector Control.
作者： Elisabeth Nelson, Thiago N Pereira, Erica Milena de Castro Ribeiro, Bianca Daoud Mafra E Silva, Carolina Camillo, Thiago Rodrigues da Costa, Mauro M Teixeira, Albert I Ko, Derek A T Cummings, Luciano A Moreira
摘要中文翻译沃尔巴克氏菌渗入埃及伊蚊种群是预防登革热的有效策略,目前正在接受世界卫生组织资格预审评估。目前,世界蚊子计划 (WMP) 在巴西进行的用于长期监测沃尔巴克氏体渗入的试验和部署标准方案涉及使用 BG-Sentinel 诱捕器(BG-traps;Biogents AG [雷根斯堡,德国]),这是劳动密集型且成本高昂的。更实惠的替代品,例如产卵陷阱(产卵器),已在其他环境中使用;然而,尚未对 Wolbachia 监测 的 WMP 方案进行比较。作为 EVITA 登革热试验的一部分,Ae. 2023 年 3 月至 5 月期间,从巴西贝洛奥里藏特 12 个集群的 124 个产卵器和 237 个 BG 捕获器收集埃及伊蚊卵和成虫。从产卵器收集的卵被培育成 L3 和 L4 幼虫,并通过定量聚合酶链反应测试从 BG 捕获器的幼虫和成蚊中检测到 wMel Wolbachia。混合效应模型用于比较从产卵器和 BG 陷阱数据随时间推移得出的 wMel 渗入的簇水平估计。在产卵器饲养的 3,675 只幼虫中,wMel 患病率 为 0.50(95% CI:0.48-0.51),而 BG 诱捕器饲养的 1,244 只成虫的 患病率 为 0.45(95% CI:0.42-0.48)。幼虫和成虫的簇级渗入估计值密切相关(Spearman's r = 0.70;P <0.001)。结合当前和上个月产卵器数据和蚊子丰度的多变量模型显示,基于产卵器的预测与 BG 陷阱估计值之间具有很强的一致性(rs ≥0.98)。这些发现表明,随着在高负担登革热环境中扩大干预措施,基于产卵器的 监测 为监测沃尔巴克氏体渗入提供了一种资源匮乏、高效且精确的替代方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估监测埃及伊蚊沃尔巴克氏体渗入的替代方法,这是预防登革热的关键策略。背景强调基于沃尔巴克氏体的 媒介 控制是预防登革热的有效策略,目前正在接受世界卫生组织资格预审评估。
设计与对象
该研究于 2023 年 3 月至 5 月在巴西贝洛奥里藏特进行,涉及 12 个集群中的 124 个产卵诱集器和 237 个 BG 诱集器。从这些诱捕器中收集埃及伊蚊卵和成虫以评估沃尔巴克氏体渗入。
核心方法
从产卵器收集的卵被培育成 L3 和 L4 幼虫,并使用定量聚合酶链反应测试在 BG 陷阱的幼虫和成蚊中检测到沃尔巴克氏体 (wMel)。混合效应模型用于比较从产卵器和 BG 陷阱数据随时间推移得出的 wMel 渗入的簇水平估计。
主要结果
在产卵器饲养的 3,675 只幼虫中,wMel 患病率 为 0.50(95% CI:0.48-0.51),而 BG 诱捕器饲养的 1,244 只成虫的 患病率 为 0.45(95% CI:0.42-0.48)。幼虫和成虫的簇级渗入估计值密切相关(Spearman's r = 0.70;P <0.001)。结合当前和上个月产卵器数据和蚊子丰度的多变量模型显示,基于产卵器的预测与 BG 陷阱估计值之间具有很强的一致性(rs ≥0.98)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,随着干预措施在高负担登革热环境中规模化,基于产卵器的 监测 为监测沃尔巴克氏体渗入提供了一种资源匮乏、高效且精确的替代方案。这是一个新颖的发现,因为它首次在 WMP 协议的背景下直接比较产卵陷阱和 BG 陷阱数据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更具成本效益和可扩展的 监测 方法提供信息,用于监测登革热多发地区的沃尔巴克氏体渗入,支持 媒介 控制的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于集群级别的估计。证据强度中等,因为研究结果来自单次试验,可能无法推广到所有情况。

Comparison of Oviposition and Adult Trapping to Monitor wMel Introgression for Wolbachia-Based Vector Control.

Authors: Elisabeth Nelson, Thiago N Pereira, Erica Milena de Castro Ribeiro, Bianca Daoud Mafra E Silva, Carolina Camillo, Thiago Rodrigues da Costa, Mauro M Teixeira, Albert I Ko, Derek A T Cummings, Luciano A Moreira
Original abstract (English)
Wolbachia introgression into Aedes aegypti (Ae. aegypti) mosquito populations is an effective strategy for preventing dengue and is currently under evaluation for WHO prequalification. The standard protocol for trials and deployments currently being conducted in Brazil by the World Mosquito Program (WMP) for long-term monitoring of Wolbachia introgression involves the use of BG-Sentinel traps (BG-traps; Biogents AG [Regensburg, Germany]), which are labor-intensive and costly. More affordable alternatives, such as oviposition traps (ovitraps), have been used in other settings; however, a comparison of the WMP protocol for Wolbachia surveillance has not been performed. As part of the EVITA dengue trial, Ae. aegypti eggs and adults were collected from 124 ovitraps and 237 BG-traps across 12 clusters in Belo Horizonte, Brazil, between March and May 2023. Eggs collected from ovitraps were reared to L3 and L4 larvae, and wMel Wolbachia was detected using quantitative polymerase chain reaction testing in larvae and adult mosquitoes from BG-traps. Mixed-effects models were used to compare cluster-level estimates of wMel introgression derived from ovitrap and BG-trap data over time. Among 3,675 larvae reared from ovitraps, wMel prevalence was 0.50 (95% CI: 0.48-0.51), whereas 1,244 adults from BG-traps had a prevalence of 0.45 (95% CI: 0.42-0.48). Cluster-level introgression estimates from larvae and adults were strongly correlated (Spearman's r = 0.70; P <0.001). Multivariate models incorporating current- and previous-month ovitrap data and mosquito abundance revealed strong agreement between ovitrap-based predictions and BG-trap estimates (rs ≥0.98). These findings reveal that ovitrap-based surveillance provides a low-resource, efficient, and precise alternative for monitoring Wolbachia introgression as interventions are scaled in high-burden dengue settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate alternative methods for monitoring Wolbachia introgression in Aedes aegypti mosquitoes, which is a critical strategy for preventing dengue. The background highlights that Wolbachia-based vector control is an effective strategy for dengue prevention and is currently being evaluated for WHO prequalification.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Belo Horizonte, Brazil, between March and May 2023, involving 124 ovitraps and 237 BG-traps across 12 clusters. aegypti eggs and adults were collected from these traps to assess Wolbachia introgression.
Core methods
Eggs collected from ovitraps were reared to L3 and L4 larvae, and Wolbachia (wMel) was detected using quantitative polymerase chain reaction testing in larvae and adult mosquitoes from BG-traps. Mixed-effects models were used to compare cluster-level estimates of wMel introgression derived from ovitrap and BG-trap data over time.
Main results
Among 3,675 larvae reared from ovitraps, wMel prevalence was 0.50 (95% CI: 0.48-0.51), whereas 1,244 adults from BG-traps had a prevalence of 0.45 (95% CI: 0.42-0.48). Cluster-level introgression estimates from larvae and adults were strongly correlated (Spearman's r = 0.70; P <0.001). Multivariate models incorporating current- and previous-month ovitrap data and mosquito abundance revealed strong agreement between ovitrap-based predictions and BG-trap estimates (rs ≥0.98).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that ovitrap-based surveillance provides a low-resource, efficient, and precise alternative for monitoring Wolbachia introgression as interventions are scaled in high-burden dengue settings. This is a novel finding as it directly compares ovitrap and BG-trap data for the first time in the context of the WMP protocol.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more cost-effective and scalable surveillance methods for monitoring Wolbachia introgression in dengue-prone areas, supporting public health strategies for vector control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on cluster-level estimates. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are derived from a single trial and may not be generalizable to all settings.

印度尼西亚泗水每月 DHF 病例的负二项式 tv-DINGARCH 模型(2015-2025 年)

Negative Binomial tv-DINGARCH Models for Monthly DHF Cases in Surabaya, Indonesia (2015-2025)
作者： Aminatuz Zahro, A’yunin Sofro
摘要中文翻译流行病学研究中经常会遇到计数时间序列数据,例如登革出血热 (DHF) 病例的月度报告。INGARCH 模型已广泛用于处理此类数据中的时间依赖性和过度分散。然而,标准 INGARCH 模型假设离散程度恒定,而实际上,由于季节性或 媒介 控制政策等流行病学因素,离散程度可能随时间变化。准确捕获这种随时间变化的变异性对于可靠的疾病 监测 至关重要。本研究将 tv-DINGARCH 模型应用于泗水 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月的 DHF 病例。使用负二项分布的 tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) 模型是因为它允许离散参数 \(\phi_t\) 动态演化。参数估计是使用 RStudio 中实现的条件最大似然估计方法进行的。结果表明,tv-DINGARCH 模型的 AIC = 968.43 和 BIC = 985.68,均小于标准 INGARCH 模型的结果(AIC = 1012.18,BIC = 1023.68)。似然比检验产生 LR 统计量为 47.7560,p 值小于 0.000001,强烈拒绝恒定离散。平稳性条件满足 \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\)。从公共卫生角度来看,这些发现表明 tv-DINGARCH 模型更好地捕捉了 DHF 监测 的波动动态,支持更有效的 监测 和早期预警系统。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流行病学研究中经常会遇到计数时间序列数据,例如登革出血热 (DHF) 病例的月度报告。标准 INGARCH 模型假设离散程度恒定,但实际上,由于季节性或 媒介 控制政策等流行病学因素,离散程度可能随时间变化。
设计与对象
本研究将 tv-DINGARCH 模型应用于泗水 2015 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月的 DHF 病例。使用负二项分布的 tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) 模型是因为它允许离散参数 \(\phi_t\) 动态演化。
核心方法
参数估计是使用 RStudio 中实现的条件最大似然估计方法进行的。平稳性条件满足 \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\)。
主要结果
结果表明,tv-DINGARCH 模型的 AIC = 968.43 和 BIC = 985.68,均小于标准 INGARCH 模型的结果(AIC = 1012.18,BIC = 1023.68)。似然比检验产生 LR 统计量为 47.7560,p 值小于 0.000001,强烈拒绝恒定离散。
解释与创新
tv-DINGARCH模型更好地捕捉DHF 监测的波动动态,支持更有效的监测和预警系统。这项研究证明了在疾病模型中考虑时变离散度的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
准确捕获随时间变化的变异性对于可靠的疾病 监测 至关重要。研究结果表明,tv-DINGARCH 模型为 DHF 病例预测和公共卫生规划提供了更稳健的框架。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于泗水的 DHF 病例,这可能会影响对其他地区或时间段的推广。由于统计显着的结果和严格的模型比较,证据强度很高。

Negative Binomial tv-DINGARCH Models for Monthly DHF Cases in Surabaya, Indonesia (2015-2025)

Authors: Aminatuz Zahro, A’yunin Sofro
Original abstract (English)
Count time series data are commonly encountered in epidemiological studies, such as monthly reports of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) cases. The INGARCH model has been widely used to handle temporal dependence and overdispersion in such data. However, the standard INGARCH model assumes constant dispersion, whereas in practice the dispersion level may vary over time due to epidemiological factors like seasonality or vector control policies. Accurately capturing this time-varying variability is essential for reliable disease surveillance. This study applies the tv-DINGARCH model to DHF cases in Surabaya from January 2015 to December 2025. The tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) model with Negative Binomial distribution is used because it allows the dispersion parameter \(\phi_t\) to evolve dynamically. Parameter estimation is conducted using the Conditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation method implemented in RStudio. The results show that the tv-DINGARCH model yields AIC = 968.43 and BIC = 985.68, both smaller than those of the standard INGARCH model (AIC = 1012.18, BIC = 1023.68). The likelihood ratio test produces an LR statistic of 47.7560 with a p-value less than 0.000001, strongly rejecting constant dispersion. The stationarity condition is satisfied with \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\). From a public health perspective, these findings indicate that the tv-DINGARCH model better captures the fluctuating dynamics of DHF transmission, supporting more effective surveillance and early warning systems.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Count time series data are commonly encountered in epidemiological studies, such as monthly reports of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) cases. The standard INGARCH model assumes constant dispersion, whereas in practice the dispersion level may vary over time due to epidemiological factors like seasonality or vector control policies.
Design and population
This study applies the tv-DINGARCH model to DHF cases in Surabaya from January 2015 to December 2025. The tv-DINGARCH(1,1,1,1) model with Negative Binomial distribution is used because it allows the dispersion parameter \(\phi_t\) to evolve dynamically.
Core methods
Parameter estimation is conducted using the Conditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation method implemented in RStudio. The stationarity condition is satisfied with \(\beta_2 + \alpha_2 + \beta_1 + \alpha_1 = 0.9990 1\).
Main results
The results show that the tv-DINGARCH model yields AIC = 968.43 and BIC = 985.68, both smaller than those of the standard INGARCH model (AIC = 1012.18, BIC = 1023.68). The likelihood ratio test produces an LR statistic of 47.7560 with a p-value less than 0.000001, strongly rejecting constant dispersion.
Interpretation and novelty
The tv-DINGARCH model better captures the fluctuating dynamics of DHF transmission, supporting more effective surveillance and early warning systems. This study demonstrates the importance of accounting for time-varying dispersion in disease modeling.
Scientific and public-health significance
Accurately capturing time-varying variability is essential for reliable disease surveillance. The findings indicate that the tv-DINGARCH model provides a more robust framework for DHF case forecasting and public health planning.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to DHF cases in Surabaya, which may affect generalizability to other regions or time periods. The evidence strength is high due to the statistically significant results and rigorous model comparison.

评估住院患者 D-二聚体水平升高相关的临床状况:一项横断面研究

Evaluating Elevated D-Dimer Levels Associated Clinical Conditions in Hospitalised Patients: A Cross-sectional Study
作者： Chitrawati Gargade, Prakash Jadhav, Siddhi Shirdhankar, V. Dombale
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
血浆 D-二聚体水平升高并非血栓栓塞性疾病所特有的,可能在多种非血栓性疾病中观察到,这使得资源有限的农村地区的解释变得复杂。本研究旨在调查住院患者血浆 D-二聚体水平升高相关的临床状况。
设计与对象
这项基于实验室数据库的横断面、回顾性研究于 2023 年 10 月 1 日至 2024 年 9 月 30 日在印度西马哈拉施特拉邦的一所农村三级医学院进行。该研究在排除重复测试和临床信息不足的病例后,分析了 235 名至少有一个 D-二聚体值大于 250 μg/L DDU 的住院患者。
核心方法
根据定量血浆 D-二聚体检测记录从实验室信息系统中选择患者,并从医院记录中检索人口统计详细信息、最终临床诊断和实验室参数。使用描述性统计分析数据。
主要结果
Among the 235 patients, D-dimer values ranged from 255 to 75,860 μg/L DDU with a mean of 2,604.3 μg/L. The most common cause of elevated D-dimer was dengue (133/235, 56.6%), followed by cardiovascular disorders (11.9%), sepsis (6.8%), and thromboembolic conditions (5.1%). Overall mortality was 14.9% and was predominantly seen in patients with moderate-to-severe D-dimer elevation.
解释与创新
作者解释说,在 队列研究 这家乡村医院,D-二聚体水平升高最常与感染性疾病(尤其是登革热)相关,而不是孤立的血栓栓塞。这些发现表明,在热带农村地区,D-二聚体升高通常反映了感染相关的凝血激活。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,D-二聚体结果的临床解释应以潜在疾病背景为指导,特别是在传染病流行的热带农村地区。这种针对具体情况的方法可以提高 D-二聚体检测在此类人群中的诊断效用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制、偏差或混杂因素。由于回顾性设计和对数据库记录的依赖,证据强度被评估为中等。

Evaluating Elevated D-Dimer Levels Associated Clinical Conditions in Hospitalised Patients: A Cross-sectional Study

Authors: Chitrawati Gargade, Prakash Jadhav, Siddhi Shirdhankar, V. Dombale
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: D-dimer is widely used to exclude Venous Thromboembolism (VTE) and to support the diagnosis of Disseminated Intravascular Coagulation (DIC). However, Elevated plasma D-dimer levels are not specific to thromboembolic disorders and may be observed in several non-thrombotic conditions such as infections, malignancies, inflammatory disorders, trauma, and postoperative states. This broad range of associated conditions complicates the interpretation of D-dimer results, particularly in resource-limited rural settings where the burden of infectious and inflammatory diseases is relatively high. Aim: To study the clinical conditions associated with elevated plasma D-dimer levels in hospitalised patients. Materials and Methods: The present cross-sectional, retrospective, laboratory database-based study was conducted in the Department of Pathology at a rural tertiary-care medical college in Western Maharashtra, India, from 1st October 2023 to 30th September 2024. Patients were selected from the Laboratory Information System (LIS) database based on records of quantitative plasma D-dimer testing performed during the study period. All consecutive hospitalised patients with at least one D-dimer value greater than 250 μg/L D-dimer Units (DDU), which represents the upper reference limit in the study laboratory, were included in the study. Demographic details, final clinical diagnosis, and selected laboratory parameters including Haemoglobin (Hb), Total Leukocyte Count (TLC), platelet count, Prothrombin Time/International Normalised Ratio (PT/INR), Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time (APTT), C-Reactive Protein (CRP), and other available laboratory investigations were retrieved from hospital records. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics. Results: Among 349 D-dimer tests, 247 were >250 μg/L DDU. After excluding duplicate tests (n=6) and cases with insufficient clinical information (n=6), 235 patients were analysed (139 males, 59.1%; 96 females, 40.9%). D-dimer values ranged from 255 to 75,860 μg/L DDU (mean 2,604.3 μg/L). The commonest cause was dengue (133/235, 56.6%), comprising 129 NS1 antigen-positive and 4 IgM-positive cases. Cardiovascular disorders {Myocardial infarction, Atrial Fibrillation, post Coronary artery bypass graft surgery (CABG)} constituted 28/235 (11.9%), followed by sepsis (16/235, 6.8%) and thromboembolic conditions (12/235, 5.1%). Other causes included Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS), fracture/osteomyelitis, pregnancy-related conditions, malignancy, snakebite and acute febrile illness. Overall mortality was 35/235 (14.9%) and was predominantly seen in patients with moderate-to-severe D-dimer elevation. Conclusion: In the current rural hospital cohort, elevated D-dimer levels were most frequently associated with infectious conditions particularly dengue followed by cardiovascular disorders and sepsis, whereas confirmed thromboembolic events represented a small proportion of cases. Extremely high values were observed in severe systemic illnesses such as sepsis and snakebite and were associated with poorer outcomes. These findings suggest that, in tropical rural settings, elevated D-dimer often reflects infection-related coagulation activation rather than isolated thromboembolism. Clinical interpretation should therefore be guided by the underlying disease context.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Elevated plasma D-dimer levels are not specific to thromboembolic disorders and may be observed in several non-thrombotic conditions, complicating interpretation in resource-limited rural settings. This study aimed to investigate the clinical conditions associated with elevated plasma D-dimer levels in hospitalised patients.
Design and population
This cross-sectional, retrospective, laboratory database-based study was conducted at a rural tertiary-care medical college in Western Maharashtra, India, from October 1, 2023, to September 30, 2024. The study analysed 235 hospitalised patients with at least one D-dimer value greater than 250 μg/L DDU after excluding duplicate tests and cases with insufficient clinical information.
Core methods
Patients were selected from the Laboratory Information System based on quantitative plasma D-dimer testing records, and demographic details, final clinical diagnoses, and laboratory parameters were retrieved from hospital records. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics.
Main results
Among the 235 patients, D-dimer values ranged from 255 to 75,860 μg/L DDU with a mean of 2,604.3 μg/L. The most common cause of elevated D-dimer was dengue (133/235, 56.6%), followed by cardiovascular disorders (11.9%), sepsis (6.8%), and thromboembolic conditions (5.1%). Overall mortality was 14.9% and was predominantly seen in patients with moderate-to-severe D-dimer elevation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that in this rural hospital cohort, elevated D-dimer levels were most frequently associated with infectious conditions, particularly dengue, rather than isolated thromboembolism. These findings suggest that in tropical rural settings, elevated D-dimer often reflects infection-related coagulation activation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that clinical interpretation of D-dimer results should be guided by the underlying disease context, especially in tropical rural settings where infectious diseases are prevalent. This context-specific approach can improve the diagnostic utility of D-dimer testing in such populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations, bias, or confounding factors. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to the retrospective design and reliance on database records.

哈茨肽硫磺酯双重修饰的肽硫酯和肽三唑衍生物

Peptidthioester und Peptidtriazolderivate durch Duale Modifikation von Peptidthiosulfonaten auf dem Harz
作者： Marius Werner, Agnes Bergmann, Chen-Xiang Liu, Mira A. M. Behnam, Christian D. Klein, Franziska Thomas
摘要中文翻译摘要 改性肽由于其高特异性和亲和力,特别是对于常规小分子无法治疗的药理学靶标,构成了一类重要的活性成分。因此,能够实现肽与非典型修饰高效多样化的方法非常受欢迎。生成多样化肽的一种可能策略是在树脂上的合成后期进行修饰,即在构建肽链后,有选择性地修饰一个官能团——通常在氨基酸侧链或N端——而其他官能团保持受保护状态。在此,我们提出了一种在树脂上进行双重合成后期修饰的方法。该方法包括一个反应序列,首先对树脂结合的肽硫代磺酸酯进行铜催化S-炔基化,随后进行铱催化叠氮-炔点击反应(IrAAC)。通过这种方式,生成了具有复杂非典型修饰的肽。这种双重修饰方法具有高度模块化,并与用于S-炔基化的多种炔烃以及用于IrAAC的多种叠氮化物兼容。此外,这种化学方法可用于修饰复杂的肽,例如由34个氨基酸组成的WW结构域。在针对登革病毒含三唑肽蛋白酶抑制剂的结构-活性关系研究中,我们展示了树脂上肽硫代磺酸酯的模块化双重修饰在调节肽-蛋白相互作用方面的巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了通过非规范修饰对肽进行有效多样化的需求,以靶向传统小分子无法治疗的药理结构。
设计与对象
该研究提出了一种在树脂上对肽进行双重修饰的新方法,通过针对 登革病毒 的含三唑肽蛋白酶抑制剂的结构-活性关系研究证明了这一方法。
核心方法
该方法涉及树脂结合的肽硫代磺酸盐的铜催化 S-炔基化反应序列,然后是铱催化的叠氮化物-炔点击反应 (IrAAC),从而能够生成具有复杂非规范修饰的肽。
主要结果
该方法成功生成了具有复杂非规范修饰的肽,并且高度模块化,与多种炔烃和叠氮化物兼容,并且适用于复杂肽,例如 34 个氨基酸的 WW 结构域。
解释与创新
作者将双重修饰策略解释为在调节肽-蛋白质相互作用方面具有巨大潜力,正如对 登革病毒 的含三唑肽-蛋白酶抑制剂的研究所证明的那样。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 登革病毒 新型肽疗法的开发提供了信息,有可能使设计更有效的针对病毒蛋白酶的抑制剂成为可能。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究仅限于体外演示,不包括体内验证或临床数据。

Peptidthioester und Peptidtriazolderivate durch Duale Modifikation von Peptidthiosulfonaten auf dem Harz

Authors: Marius Werner, Agnes Bergmann, Chen-Xiang Liu, Mira A. M. Behnam, Christian D. Klein, Franziska Thomas
Original abstract (English)
ZUSAMMENFASSUNG Modifizierte Peptide stellen aufgrund ihrer hohen Spezifität und Affinität insbesondere gegenüber pharmakologischen Zielstrukturen, die mit herkömmlichen kleinen Molekülen nicht behandelbar sind, eine wichtige Wirkstoffklasse dar. Daher sind Methoden, die eine effiziente Diversifizierung von Peptiden mit nicht‐kanonischen Modifikationen ermöglichen, sehr gefragt. Eine mögliche Strategie zur Erzeugung diversifizierter Peptide ist die Modifikation im späten Synthesestadium auf dem Harz, bei der eine funktionelle Gruppe – typischerweise in einer Aminosäureseitenkette oder am N‐Terminus – gezielt nach dem Aufbau der Peptidkette modifiziert wird, während die anderen funktionellen Gruppen geschützt bleiben. Hier stellen wir einen Ansatz zur dualen Modifikation im späten Synthesestadium auf dem Harz vor. Dieser Ansatz umfasst eine Reaktionssequenz aus kupferkatalysierter S‐Alkinylierung eines harzgebundenen Peptidthiosulfonats, gefolgt von einer iridiumkatalysierten Azid‐Alkin‐Click‐Reaktion (IrAAC). Auf diese Weise werden Peptide mit komplexen nicht‐kanonischen Modifikationen generiert. Der Ansatz der dualen Modifikation ist hochgradig modular und mit einer breiten Palette von Alkinen für die S‐Alkinylierung sowie von Aziden für die IrAAC kompatibel. Darüber hinaus kann diese Chemie zur Modifikation komplexer Peptide, wie beispielsweise der 34 Aminosäuren umfassenden WW‐Domäne, genutzt werden. In einer Struktur‐Wirkungs‐Beziehungsstudie zu triazolhaltigen Peptid‐Protease‐Inhibitoren des Dengue‐Virus demonstrieren wir das große Potenzial der modularen dualen Modifikation von Peptidthiosulfonaten auf dem Harz zur Modulation von Peptid‐Protein‐Wechselwirkungen.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for efficient diversification of peptides with non-canonical modifications to target pharmacological structures that are not treatable with conventional small molecules.
Design and population
The research presents a novel approach for dual modification of peptides on resin, demonstrated through a structure-activity relationship study with triazole-containing peptide-protease inhibitors targeting the Dengue virus.
Core methods
The method involves a reaction sequence of copper-catalyzed S-alkynylation of a resin-bound peptidthiosulfonate, followed by an iridium-catalyzed azide-alkyne click reaction (IrAAC), enabling the generation of peptides with complex non-canonical modifications.
Main results
The approach successfully generates peptides with complex non-canonical modifications and is highly modular, compatible with a wide range of alkynes and azides, and applicable to complex peptides such as the 34-amino acid WW-domain.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the dual modification strategy as having great potential for modulating peptide-protein interactions, as demonstrated in a study with triazole-containing peptide-protease inhibitors of the Dengue virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of novel peptide-based therapeutics for Dengue virus, potentially enabling the design of more effective inhibitors targeting viral proteases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is limited to in vitro demonstrations and does not include in vivo validation or clinical data.

儿科登革热未解决的问题:从疾病机制到预防

Unresolved questions in pediatric dengue: from disease mechanisms to prevention
作者： Eduardo Lopez-Medina, Eduardo López-Medina
摘要中文翻译综述目的:尽管最近在了解登革热感染方面取得了进展,但在其 流行病学、发病机制、诊断 和预防(包括 疫苗 的发展及其长期影响)方面仍存在巨大差距。这篇综述总结了最近的研究结果,并强调了推进该领域并减轻个人和公共卫生负担的最紧迫的研究需求。最新发现:登革热负担有所增加,特别是在儿童和青少年中,并且已扩展到以前未受影响的地区。尽管如此,由于 监测 系统不理想,当前数据可能低估了真正的负担。改善控制需要更好地了解 重症登革热 发病机制,其中仍然存在关键差距,同时在增加 疫苗 可用性的背景下重新评估诊断限制。疫苗 是一个重要的补充工具,有几个候选工具正在研究中,两个正在使用中,每个工具都有独特的优势、局限性和未解决的问题。摘要:需要更广泛的研究和投资来解决登革热日益严重的负担,包括严重的 暴发 和地理传播。与此同时,加强监管参与创新治疗和预防措施的制定和评估至关重要,特别是在负担最重的低收入和中等收入地区。关于登革热及其影响的清晰沟通和教育,以及经证明具有良好耐受性和有效性的可用预防措施,对于加强信任、提高吸收率和最大限度地提高公共卫生影响也至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 登革病毒 1-4 和登革热疾病,检查 流行病学、发病机制、诊断 和预防,包括 疫苗 的发展和长期影响,核心问题是儿科登革热中尚未解决的问题。
证据基础与方法
该综述总结了最近的发现,并强调了紧迫的研究需求,但没有报告具体的数据库、检索日期、资格标准和研究数量。
共识与结论
登革热负担有所增加,特别是在儿童和青少年中,并扩大到以前未受影响的地区;由于 监测 系统不理想,当前数据可能低估了真正的负担。
争议与缺口
重症登革热 发病机制、在增加 疫苗 可用性的背景下的诊断限制以及有关 疫苗 的优势、局限性和长期影响的未解决问题仍然存在关键差距。
科研与实践启示
需要更广泛的研究和投资来解决登革热日益严重的负担,包括严重的 暴发 和地理传播;加强监管参与创新治疗和预防措施至关重要,特别是在低收入和中等收入地区;关于登革热的明确沟通和教育以及现有的预防措施对于加强信任和提高了解至关重要。

Unresolved questions in pediatric dengue: from disease mechanisms to prevention

Authors: Eduardo Lopez-Medina, Eduardo López-Medina
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Despite recent advances in understanding dengue infection, substantial gaps remain in its epidemiology, pathogenesis, diagnosis, and prevention, including vaccine development and its long-term impact. This review summarizes recent findings and highlights the most pressing research needs to advance the field and reduce individual and public health burden. RECENT FINDINGS: Dengue burden has increased, particularly among children and adolescents, and it has expanded into previously unaffected regions. Still, current data likely underestimates the true burden due to suboptimal surveillance systems. Improving control will require a better understanding of severe dengue pathogenesis, where key gaps remain, alongside reassessment of diagnostic limitations in the context of increasing vaccine availability. Vaccines represent an important complementary tool, with several candidates under study and two currently in use, each with distinct strengths, limitations, and unresolved questions. SUMMARY: More extensive research and investment are needed to address the growing burden of dengue, including severe outbreaks and geographic spread. Meanwhile, stronger regulatory engagement in the development and evaluation of innovative therapeutic and preventive measures is essential, particularly in low-income and middle-income regions that bear the highest burden. Clear communication and education on dengue, its impact, and available preventive measures proven to be well tolerated and effective are also critical to strengthen trust, improve uptake, and maximize public health impact.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease, examining epidemiology, pathogenesis, diagnosis, and prevention, including vaccine development and long-term impact, with a central question on unresolved questions in pediatric dengue.
Evidence base and method
The review summarizes recent findings and highlights pressing research needs, though specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, and study counts are not reported.
Consensus and conclusions
Dengue burden has increased, particularly among children and adolescents, and expanded into previously unaffected regions; current data likely underestimates the true burden due to suboptimal surveillance systems.
Controversies and gaps
Key gaps remain in severe dengue pathogenesis, diagnostic limitations in the context of increasing vaccine availability, and unresolved questions regarding vaccine strengths, limitations, and long-term impact.
Research and practice implications
More extensive research and investment are needed to address the growing burden of dengue, including severe outbreaks and geographic spread; stronger regulatory engagement in innovative therapeutic and preventive measures is essential, particularly in low-income and middle-income regions; clear communication and education on dengue and available preventive measures are critical to strengthen trust and improve uptake.

Journal: Journal of Travel Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-22  |  DOI: 10.1093/jtm/taag065  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

共享证据,不同建议:针对各国旅行者的 Qdenga (TAK-003) 登革热 疫苗 指南的比较分析

Shared evidence, different recommendations: a comparative analysis of Qdenga (TAK-003) dengue vaccine guidance for travellers across countries
作者： Sarah L McGuinness, Dipti Patel, Helena H Askling, Camilla Rothe, Steven Schofield, Robert Steffen, Leo G Visser, Lin H Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热是旅行者中日益重要的疾病原因,Qdenga (TAK-003) 是第一个纳入多个国家旅行者指南的登革热 疫苗。然而,临床医生和旅行者可能会在不同司法管辖区遇到不同的建议。这项研究比较了各国旅行者使用 Qdenga 的指南。方法 我们利用调查数据和对公开建议的结构化审查,对各国旅行者使用 Qdenga 的国家指南进行了比较分析。根据登革热免疫状态合成指南,并在重复决策域中进行映射。结果 2026 年 3 月至 4 月期间,从 17 个国家的 18 个咨询机构获得了调查回复,其中包括 Qdenga 获得许可并发布指南的 12 个非流行国家。指导通常比监管授权更具限制性。虽然人们普遍同意向实验室确诊的既往登革热感染者推荐 疫苗接种,但对于没有感染记录的旅行者的建议差异很大。所有咨询机构都考虑了目的地特定的登革热风险,但在是否以及如何使用旅行持续时间、重复或累积暴露、旅行者特征和 宿主 易感因素来确定 疫苗 资格方面有所不同。一些建议将 疫苗接种 限制为既往感染过登革热的旅行者,而另一些建议将资格扩大到没有既往感染记录的特定旅行者,或根据暴露风险推荐 疫苗接种,无论血清状态如何。在评估既往登革热感染以及是否需要在旅行前完成两剂系列疫苗方面,方法也有所不同。结论 Qdenga 指南的差异反映了咨询机构如何解释共享证据以及管理因数据差距以及试验人群和旅行者之间的差异而产生的不确定性的差异。关于 疫苗 在血清阴性旅行者和老年人中的有效性和临床结果的更多数据可能有助于减少不确定性并支持未来建议的更大一致性。无论如何,在与旅行者讨论登革热 疫苗接种 时,透明度和共同的临床决策仍然至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查比较了各国旅行者使用 Qdenga (TAK-003) 登革热 疫苗 的国家指南,根据登革热免疫状态综合建议,并将其映射到决策领域。
证据基础与方法
使用调查数据和对咨询机构公开建议的结构化审查进行了比较分析。2026 年 3 月至 4 月期间,从 17 个国家的 18 个咨询机构获得了调查回复。
共识与结论
人们广泛同意为实验室确诊的既往登革热感染者推荐 疫苗接种,但对于没有感染记录的旅行者的建议差异很大。指导通常比监管授权更具限制性,所有咨询机构都考虑了目的地特定的登革热风险。
争议与缺口
指南的差异反映了在解释共享证据和管理因数据差距以及试验人群和旅行者之间的差异而产生的不确定性方面的差异。疫苗 在血清阴性旅行者和老年人中的有效性和临床结果存在不确定性。
科研与实践启示
需要关于 疫苗 在血清阴性旅行者和老年人中的有效性和临床结果的更多数据,以减少不确定性并支持未来建议的更大一致性。在与旅行者讨论登革热 疫苗接种 时,透明度和共同的临床决策仍然至关重要。

Shared evidence, different recommendations: a comparative analysis of Qdenga (TAK-003) dengue vaccine guidance for travellers across countries

Authors: Sarah L McGuinness, Dipti Patel, Helena H Askling, Camilla Rothe, Steven Schofield, Robert Steffen, Leo G Visser, Lin H Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue is an increasingly important cause of illness in travellers, and Qdenga (TAK-003) is the first dengue vaccine incorporated into guidance for travellers in multiple countries. However, clinicians and travellers may encounter differing recommendations across jurisdictions. This study compared guidance on the use of Qdenga in travellers across countries. Methods We conducted a comparative analysis of national guidance on Qdenga use for travellers across countries, using survey data and structured review of publicly available recommendations. Guidance was synthesised according to dengue immune status and mapped across recurring decision domains. Results Survey responses were obtained from 18 advisory bodies across 17 countries between March and April 2026, including twelve non-endemic countries where Qdenga was licensed and guidance published. Guidance was generally more restrictive than regulatory authorisation. While there was broad agreement in recommending vaccination for travellers with laboratory-confirmed prior dengue infection, recommendations for those without documented infection varied widely. All advisory bodies considered destination-specific dengue risk but differed in whether and how they used travel duration, repeated or cumulative exposure, traveller characteristics and host susceptibility factors to determine vaccine eligibility. Some recommendations restricted vaccination to travellers with prior dengue infection, whereas others extended eligibility to selected travellers without documented prior infection or recommended vaccination irrespective of serostatus based on exposure risk. Approaches also differed in assessment of prior dengue infection and whether completion of the two-dose series before travel was required. Conclusions Variation in Qdenga guidance reflects differences in how advisory bodies interpret shared evidence and manage uncertainty arising from data gaps and differences between trial populations and travellers. Additional data on vaccine effectiveness and clinical outcomes among seronegative travellers and older adults may help reduce uncertainty and support greater alignment of future recommendations. Regardless, transparency and shared clinical decision-making remain essential when discussing dengue vaccination with travellers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review compares national guidance on the use of the Qdenga (TAK-003) dengue vaccine for travellers across countries, synthesizing recommendations according to dengue immune status and mapping them across decision domains.
Evidence base and method
A comparative analysis was conducted using survey data and a structured review of publicly available recommendations from advisory bodies. Survey responses were obtained from 18 advisory bodies across 17 countries between March and April 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
There is broad agreement in recommending vaccination for travellers with laboratory-confirmed prior dengue infection, but recommendations for those without documented infection vary widely. Guidance was generally more restrictive than regulatory authorisation, and all advisory bodies considered destination-specific dengue risk.
Controversies and gaps
Variation in guidance reflects differences in interpreting shared evidence and managing uncertainty arising from data gaps and differences between trial populations and travellers. Uncertainty exists regarding vaccine effectiveness and clinical outcomes among seronegative travellers and older adults.
Research and practice implications
Additional data on vaccine effectiveness and clinical outcomes among seronegative travellers and older adults are needed to reduce uncertainty and support greater alignment of future recommendations. Transparency and shared clinical decision-making remain essential when discussing dengue vaccination with travellers.

蚊子的杀虫剂抗药性:可持续 媒介 控制的现状、机制、监测和创新策略。

Insecticide resistance in mosquitoes: Current status, mechanism, monitoring and innovative strategies for sustainable vector control.
作者： Divya Kataria, Sangeeta Janjoter, Nisha Dahiya, Mahima Yadav, Ritu Yadav, Vaishali Verma, Neelam Sehrawat
摘要中文翻译疟疾、登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等蚊媒疾病仍然是全世界主要的公共卫生挑战,每年造成数百万例病例和数千人死亡。媒介 控制一直是预防策略的基石,其中杀虫剂是最常用的干预措施。然而,杀虫剂的广泛且往往不加区别的使用导致蚊子种群出现杀虫剂抗药性,从而威胁到现有控制措施的可持续性。本综述介绍了杀虫剂耐药性的现状、其潜在机制以及全球耐药性趋势。还强调了替代的 媒介 控制策略,例如基因改造、基于沃尔巴克氏体的方法、植物杀虫剂和纳米技术驱动的控制措施。它还探讨了杀虫剂抗性监测、综合抗性管理策略的重要性,以及对具有独特作用模式的新化合物的需求,以确保 媒介 控制的持续有效性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查的重点是蚊子对杀虫剂的耐药性对登革热、疟疾、基孔肯雅热、寨卡和黄热病等蚊媒疾病控制的威胁,审查了现状、机制、监测和创新策略。
证据基础与方法
该综述对杀虫剂抗性机制、全球趋势和替代控制策略进行了全面概述,但没有详细说明具体的数据库、检索日期或研究计数。
共识与结论
蚊子的杀虫剂抗性对可持续的 媒介 控制提出了重大挑战,需要综合抗性管理和具有不同作用模式的新化合物。
争议与缺口
该审查强调需要更好地监测耐药性和制定创新策略,但没有详细说明具体的争议或方法学限制。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括实施综合抗性管理、开发具有新颖作用方式的新型杀虫剂,以及探索基因改造、基于沃尔巴克氏体的方法、植物源杀虫剂和纳米技术驱动的控制措施等替代策略。

Insecticide resistance in mosquitoes: Current status, mechanism, monitoring and innovative strategies for sustainable vector control.

Authors: Divya Kataria, Sangeeta Janjoter, Nisha Dahiya, Mahima Yadav, Ritu Yadav, Vaishali Verma, Neelam Sehrawat
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne diseases such as malaria, dengue, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever remain major public health challenges worldwide, causing millions of cases and thousands of deaths annually. Vector control has been the cornerstone of prevention strategies, with insecticides being the most commonly used intervention. However, the widespread and often indiscriminate use of insecticides has led to the emergence of insecticide resistance in the mosquito population, thereby threatening the sustainability of existing control measures. This review presents the current status of insecticide resistance, its underlying mechanisms, and global trends in resistance. The alternative vector control strategies, such as genetic modification, Wolbachia-based methods, botanical insecticides and nanotechnology-driven control measures, are also highlighted. It also explores the significance of insecticide resistance monitoring, integrated resistance management strategies and the need for new compounds with a distinct mode of action to ensure the continued effectiveness of vector control.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on insecticide resistance in mosquitoes as a threat to vector control for mosquito-borne diseases including dengue, malaria, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever, examining current status, mechanisms, monitoring and innovative strategies.
Evidence base and method
The review presents a comprehensive overview of insecticide resistance mechanisms, global trends, and alternative control strategies, though specific databases, search dates, or study counts are not detailed.
Consensus and conclusions
Insecticide resistance in mosquitoes poses a significant challenge to sustainable vector control, necessitating integrated resistance management and new compounds with distinct modes of action.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights the need for better monitoring of resistance and the development of innovative strategies, though specific controversies or methodological limitations are not detailed.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include implementing integrated resistance management, developing new insecticides with novel modes of action, and exploring alternative strategies like genetic modification, Wolbachia-based methods, botanical insecticides and nanotechnology-driven control measures.

全身感染中的眼部参与:临床表现概述

Ocular Involvement in Systemic Infections: An Overview of Clinical Manifestations
作者： Theofilos Kanavos, Effrosyni Birbas
摘要中文翻译传染病是一个主要的公共卫生问题,也是全球发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。眼睛由于其丰富的血管和独特的免疫微环境,容易受到广泛的细菌、病毒、真菌和寄生虫系统感染的影响,这可能通过病原体的直接侵入、免疫介导的机制或治疗并发症导致多种眼部和视觉表现。可能累及眼部的值得注意的全身感染包括结核病;螺旋体疾病梅毒、莱姆病和钩端螺旋体病;布鲁氏菌病;猫抓病;麻风;感染性心内膜炎;人类免疫缺陷病毒、巨细胞病毒、西尼罗河病毒和腺病毒感染;风疹;登革热;念珠菌病;隐球菌病;曲霉病;毛霉菌病;弓形虫病;疟疾;盘尾丝虫病;和囊尾蚴病。眼部表现可以作为潜在全身感染的早期诊断线索,而相反,全身特征可以帮助解释眼部发现。全身感染的有效调查和管理受益于多学科方法,眼科医生可以做出重大贡献,并帮助优化视力和整体健康结果。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是全身感染中的眼部受累,特别强调 登革病毒。它探讨了 登革病毒 及其相关疾病(包括 登革热、登革出血热和登革休克综合征)的临床表现及其对眼睛和视觉系统的影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对临床表现的总体概述,而不是系统或荟萃分析综述。它不报告具体的数据库、检索日期、资格标准或研究计数。该方法是叙述性的,总结了系统感染眼部受累的现有知识,包括 登革病毒。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,眼部表现可以作为全身感染(包括 登革病毒)的早期诊断线索。它强调了多学科方法在管理这些感染方面的重要性,眼科医生在优化视觉和整体健康结果方面发挥着关键作用。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,虽然已经认识到全身感染涉及眼部,但 登革病毒 中这种涉及的确切机制和程度仍不清楚。它还提到,眼科医生在处理这些感染中的作用尚未普遍标准化,这表明证据存在差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进一步研究来阐明 登革病毒 眼部受累的机制,并规范眼科医生在其治疗中的作用。它还意味着需要改进 监测 和诊断策略,以更好地识别和解决与 监测 相关的眼部并发症。

Ocular Involvement in Systemic Infections: An Overview of Clinical Manifestations

Authors: Theofilos Kanavos, Effrosyni Birbas
Original abstract (English)
Infectious diseases constitute a major public health concern and a leading cause of morbidity and mortality worldwide. The eye, by virtue of its rich vascularity and unique immunological microenvironment, is susceptible to involvement by a broad spectrum of bacterial, viral, fungal, and parasitic systemic infections, which may lead to diverse ocular and visual manifestations through direct pathogen invasion, immune-mediated mechanisms, or complications of therapy. Notable systemic infections with potential ocular involvement include tuberculosis; the spirochetal illnesses syphilis, Lyme disease, and leptospirosis; brucellosis; cat-scratch disease; leprosy; infective endocarditis; human immunodeficiency virus, cytomegalovirus, West Nile virus, and adenovirus infections; rubeola; dengue fever; candidiasis; cryptococcosis; aspergillosis; mucormycosis; toxoplasmosis; malaria; onchocerciasis; and cysticercosis. Ocular manifestations can serve as early diagnostic clues to underlying systemic infections, whereas, conversely, systemic features can help interpret ocular findings. Effective investigation and management of systemic infections benefit from a multidisciplinary approach, in which ophthalmologists can contribute substantially and help optimize both visual and overall health outcomes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on ocular involvement in systemic infections, with a particular emphasis on dengue virus. It explores the clinical manifestations of dengue virus and its associated diseases, including dengue fever, dengue hemorrhagic fever, and dengue shock syndrome, in the context of their impact on the eye and visual system.
Evidence base and method
The review is a general overview of clinical manifestations, not a systematic or meta-analytic review. It does not report specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or study counts. The methodology is narrative, summarizing existing knowledge on ocular involvement in systemic infections, including dengue virus.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that ocular manifestations can serve as early diagnostic clues for systemic infections, including dengue virus. It highlights the importance of a multidisciplinary approach in managing these infections, with ophthalmologists playing a key role in optimizing both visual and overall health outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while ocular involvement in systemic infections is recognized, the exact mechanisms and the extent of such involvement in dengue virus remain unclear. It also mentions that the role of ophthalmologists in the management of these infections is not universally standardized, indicating a gap in evidence.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further research is needed to clarify the mechanisms of ocular involvement in dengue virus and to standardize the role of ophthalmologists in its management. It also implies the need for improved surveillance and diagnostic strategies to better identify and address ocular complications associated with dengue virus.

门被病毒覆盖:een toenemende bedreiging voor de volksgezondheid

Door muggen overdraagbare virussen: een toenemende bedreiging voor de volksgezondheid
作者： K. TONG-MINH, W.D. BOON VON OCHSSÉE
摘要中文翻译蚊媒病毒:欧洲旅行者和公共卫生面临的风险日益增加 蚊媒病毒感染对公众健康构成越来越大的威胁,全球化和白纹伊蚊等入侵蚊种的发展加剧了这一威胁。国际旅行使旅行者接触到一系列病毒,而流行地区则面临着巨大的疾病负担和社会经济后果。日本脑炎、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和登革热说明了这种威胁的多样性。本文是根据近期文献和流行病学数据进行的叙述性回顾。由库蚊传播的日本脑炎会导致严重的神经系统并发症,特别是在亚洲。基孔肯雅热由伊蚊传播,其特点是长期关节疼痛。寨卡病毒也由伊蚊传播,因先天畸形而臭名昭著。登革热有 4 种血清型,全球分布,可导致严重的出血热。对于这些病毒感染都没有特定的 抗病毒药物 治疗;该方法主要是对症治疗。气候变化和入侵蚊子物种的进一步扩散预计将导致 暴发 的频率和地理传播增加。持续监控、媒介 控制、公众教育和 疫苗接种 工作对于应对这一日益严重的威胁至关重要。

这是基于最新文献和流行病学数据的叙述性评论,没有指定数据库、搜索日期或研究计数。
